TLO v1.7 Build 654 — Native Windows UNC network shares
=========================================================
- Full Inventory Path(s) accepts \\server\share\folder paths without a mapped drive, including .txt control files and quoted/semicolon-separated entries.
- Assign the stable identity UNC:server/share to each Windows network share (case-insensitive), independent of the remote disk volume label. Persist [UNC:server/share] /relative/folder in bootlist.csv and log SEARCH_PATH records. Local drives retain their existing volume-label behavior.
- Resolve share-scoped Copy Requests directly from the share identity and relative folder path. A disconnected share remains waiting; do not require a local mapped volume or attempt a physical Get-Disk lookup.
- Manual Updates compare both share identity and relative folder path, preventing same-named shows on two different servers/shares from being confused.
- Windows file://server/share/folder drag/drop URIs resolve to UNC paths. Linux/WSL require mounting the share first instead of silently interpreting UNC as a relative path.
- For generated setlist exports, only read network setlist files from accessible UNC roots explicitly selected during the current inventory, never from untrusted persisted logs alone. Device namespaces and traversal outside the named share are rejected.
- Add New Shows recognizes UNC destinations; any destructive legacy cleanup against a UNC path remains conservatively guarded. Build Process v129 remains a separate package; source builds are still published as pre-releases by default with signing and manual promotion unchanged.
- Native Windows, actual NAS/share enumeration, PyInstaller packaging, signing, and GitHub release gates require hosted/local Windows verification. No live production release altered.

--- Historical change records (unaltered below) ---

TLO v1.7 Build 653 — updater approval guard for public GitHub pre-releases
==========================================================================
Build Process v126 publishes complete Windows hybrid, Windows onedir, Linux,
and macOS test distributions as public pre-releases by default. Promotion is
a separate manually invoked operation that marks the already-tested release
as production and updates GitHub Latest. Historical production releases and
assets are not removed, replaced, or modified by promotion.

Main and Search already obtain update candidates via the official
/repos/onaracstlo-lab/TradersLittleOrganizer/releases/latest API, which does
not return pre-releases. Build 653 additionally rejects results that are draft,
pre-release, or lack explicit boolean false status fields, before considering
build numbers or signature metadata. Manual checks and Auto Update obey the
same selection gate. Such a result is reported as no approved update, not as
an update available to download. All existing signed metadata, authenticated
ZIP SHA-256, exact platform, archive inspection and download-only checks stay
intact. The normal update operation never installs a downloaded ZIP.

No GitHub releases were modified while preparing this source bundle. Native
Windows/macOS/Linux builds, Windows signing and release promotion require the
separate Build Process v126 and its hosted validation/signing gates.

TLO v1.7 Build 648

Alternatively use a trusted official manual update. A new updater cannot change the validator already installed in an old build.

TLO v1.7 Build 640
Conservative international city recognition and alias spelling preservation.
Add a curated conventional comma-delimited cities.csv (7,193 city records)
with one indexed namespace for names and aliases, fail-closed duplicate-name
handling, and path-supplied spelling retained in the city field and Show Name.
City recognition only fills missing path metadata after a uniquely resolved
Artist DB prefix and a complete date; it cannot guess region/country from the
city reference, promote music/venue words from the exclusion list, or overwrite
existing path, setlist, or online metadata. A newly supported Artist, City Date
pattern also works in Compliant mode when both artist and city are verified.
The stricter Artist Venue City Date fallback accepts only multiword, globally
unambiguous names, refuses City/Artist DB collisions, and leaves uncertain
venue/city fields blank. Metadata from conflicting setlist cities is not joined
with a stronger path city.

Use an optional TLOHome/TLO_DBs/cities.csv override when present; malformed
content is ignored safely rather than triggering silent canonicalization. The
curated database is bundled into Windows, macOS, and Linux frozen executables.
The existing caret-delimited local cities.csv format remains accepted; no new
build-process package is required. Source attribution: GeoNames CC BY 4.0.

TLO v1.7 Build 637
Build 637: Simplified duplicate identification, rename-only updates, SHN recovery.
Duplicate cleanup and (copyN)/(altN) collision naming compare recursive
relative file names, exact byte sizes and directory structure; they no longer
hash or read candidate file contents for duplicate identification.
Files of equal name/size are treated as duplicates even if content differs.
This explicitly supersedes Build 625's duplicate-move hash gate. FLAC corruption
repair remains independent, and all transfer/update integrity gates are unchanged.

After signed-asset verification and package inspection, update downloads are
published by an atomic same-directory no-overwrite rename rather than a hard
link. A conflicting existing Downloads file is never replaced. The update is
never installed or unzipped by the checker.

Convert shn continues to verify full decoded PCM equivalence. If native Trash
fails, the verified source is renamed without copying or deleting into
<source-directory>/.tlo-shn-originals/track.shn.<unique-id> on the same volume.
This folder is excluded from TLO traversal; its original remains recoverable.
If both methods fail, the newly created FLAC is rolled back while the original
SHN is retained. No fallback is used for ordinary corrupt-file removal.
GitHub Build Process v123 is unchanged; hosted native/signing gates pending.

TLO v1.7 Build 625
Verified duplicate file identity
The duplicate cleanup utility must verify matching directory structure and SHA-256 bytes for every regular file (including FLAC, SHN, MP3 and WAV) immediately before a duplicate is moved. It rejects symlinks, unreadable files and files modified during comparison. Coarse size-based matching can identify candidates but never authorize a destructive move.
GitHub Build Process v123 unchanged. Not release-attested.

TLO v1.7 Build 623
Lossless SHN conversion with reversible original disposal
SHN to FLAC conversion SHALL request FFmpeg -xerror and -v error, reject nonzero results and diagnostic errors, independently decode the source SHN and candidate FLAC to canonical 32-bit PCM, and require equal PCM MD5 and decoded byte/sample counts. The converter SHALL NOT publish an unverified FLAC or permanently delete the SHN. Once the verified FLAC is safely published, TLO SHALL send the SHN to native Trash. If Trash fails and the source remains, TLO SHALL roll back only the newly generated FLAC, retain the SHN, and report an error. The successful tag log SHALL record the verified PCM MD5 and PCM byte count.
GitHub Build Process v123 unchanged; native compilation, security scanning and signing remain mandatory.

TLO v1.7 Build 622
City-only place inference requires positive city evidence
Non-compliant Artist, City Date parsing SHALL require city confirmation from the built-in city vocabulary or TLOHome/TLO_DBs/cities.csv. A capitalized word alone is NOT city evidence. Recording descriptors such as Soundboard, Matrix, Rehearsals, Bootleg, Encore and Remaster SHALL NOT become cities without city-reference evidence. A comma-inverted Artist DB alias (including Kinks, The and Band, The) SHALL NOT be treated as an Artist, City prefix. Jethro Tull, Derby 2007-02-04 SHALL remain supported without an invented venue or state.
GitHub Build Process v123 unchanged; native compilation, security scanning and signing remain mandatory.

TLO v1.7 Build 621: Comma-delimited city-only shows and ambiguous date corroboration
=================================================================================
- Recognize non-compliant Artist, City Date when a leading complete name uniquely matches the Artist DB and a literal comma provides the city boundary. No venue or state is invented. Multiword city terms require recognized city-reference evidence; a plausible single capitalized city token is sufficient for the guarded comma structure.
- Keep an ambiguous two-digit numeric date unresolved unless selected local setlist evidence or unique, exact artist/date/city corroboration from enabled online sources selects one interpretation. Include the guarded DD-MM-YY interpretation in addition to legacy YY-MM-DD and MM-DD-YY; preserve year-range handling elsewhere.
- Compare all plausible dates across enabled etreeDB and setlist.fm sources (maximum three dates); do not choose by first returned result, online availability alone, or a mismatched city or artist. Unavailable sources and conflicting results fail closed.
- Once a date is established, existing authoritative lookup and local-field precedence still apply. Do not modify Compliant behavior, aggregation, tagging, or transfer logic.
- Add positive/negative regression coverage for Jethro Tull, Derby 02-04-07; matching selected setlist dates; each online source; contradictions, errors, wrong artists/cities; and unrelated earlier parsing cases.
- GitHub Build Process v123 remains unchanged. Local Ruff and hosted native/signing gates remain pending.

TLO v1.7 Build 620: Guard Who-style artist/date paths from false competing artists
================================================================================
- Preserve the complete performance date in compact etree-style source folders
  such as who1974-05-18.sbd and who2008-11-13.flac16 Osaka, Japan - Castle Hall.
- Exclude Disc1 - Soccer Rock and Disc2 - Love Rain O'er Me from generic
  Artist DB substring scanning. Such titles represent part descriptions, not
  performers. Do not change physical aggregation or the source folder names.

  component to the leading abbreviation. Artist-like words in the venue,
  city or collector text after the date may not trigger artist conflicts.

  artist prefixes, conflicts with truly different nearby artists, and the
  existing last-resort abbreviation and selected-setlist fallback.
- Remove flac16, flac24, SHN and closely related technical tokens from the
  leading portion of String2 only when immediately attached to the date by
  punctuation in the original path. Other String2 rules are untouched.
- Regression tests reproduce all supplied Who paths with conflicting Artist DB
  entries such as Love, Soccer Rock, New York, Marcus and Castle, and assert
  that artist and date resolve without needing setlist metadata.
- Hosted Ruff, native packaging/scan/signing still pending. Build process v123
  unchanged.

TLO v1.7 Build 619: Post-lookup right-to-left venue/city inference
===================================================================

  place fields empty. Never combine an inferred city with unrelated online

  (Boston, Philadelphia, San Francisco, Denver, and others), extended by
  optional TLOHome/TLO_DBs/cities.csv and its aliases. Remaining text is
  the venue; strip surrounding whitespace and punctuation.
- Keep city-only 'Live in Boston' as location-only, but require a nonempty
  preceding venue for 'at' forms. Reject ambiguous 'New York' without an
  independent region and unknown city endings such as Unknownville.
- Record medium-confidence inference and the original place, venue and city
  in metadata observations. Preserve exact known-venue and structured
  state/country cases; no changes to general String1/String2 handling.
- Add post-lookup and negative regression tests; update the earlier
  Build 618 withheld-city expectation to reflect the newly allowed inference.
- GitHub Build Process v123 unchanged; native lint/build/signing pending.

TLO v1.7 Build 618: Artist (Live) at/in venue/location before date
===================================================================

  'Lucinda Williams - The Catalyst, Santa Cruz, CA 8-31-07' to

  full Artist DB prefix instead of a later venue name that matches an artist.

  Parse state/country-anchored places using existing code. Split a regionless

  guessing. Support city-only 'Live in Boston' without inventing a state.

  collisions, unsupported dates and Compliant exclusion.

TLO v1.7 Build 585: Tag-first opt-in corruption validation and FLAC FFP checking
===============================================================================
- No media corruption scan runs unless a tagging mode or Deep Audio Check is enabled.
- Tag failure (apart from transient access/resource) is confirmed corruption; no further
  decoder checking is attempted for the failed file. Filesystem errors are unverifiable.
- Deep Audio Check uses a reversible tag write/read/restore probe, also without actual
  tagging; dry-run preview does not modify audio even temporarily.
- FLAC full-stream checking now computes the STREAMINFO MD5 over bit-depth-preserving
  decoded PCM streamed through pinned native LGPL FFmpeg; it also verifies successful
  decoder completion, sample count, and source stability, and rejects missing MD5.
- MP3 and other supported audio retain full-stream FFmpeg decode validation.
- The original FLAC framemd5 record/count implementation was removed.
- Tag-write failures during actual tagging are logged and run through unchanged
  corrupt-file, whole-show/disc-folder and percentage threshold policies; unverifiable
  access errors block corruption-driven deletion.
- Manual Tweaks never performs corruption verification or Trash operations.
- Review Operation/manual warn that Deep Audio Check can be very slow.
- User Manual has a concise What's New introduction in place of change-history lists.
- Paired GitHub Build Process v117 validates the raw PCM FFmpeg source recipe.
- No signing/security/scanner safeguards are disabled. Hosted CI must validate each
  native platform before redistribution.

TLO v1.7 Build 566 - FLAC Deep Audio Check sample-completeness validation (AC-01)
===============================================================================
- With Deep Audio Check selected, compare the exact total of decoded FLAC PCM
  samples to the nonzero declared FLAC STREAMINFO total_samples. A clean FFmpeg
  exit no longer masks a recording truncated after a valid audio frame.
- Extract per-frame sample durations from bundled FFmpeg framemd5 into a
  temporary, bounded local report, not a full PCM copy or any source mutation.
- A genuine FLAC sample-count mismatch is classified as corruption. Unknown
  STREAMINFO totals, malformed/oversized decoder output, decoder absence,
  cancellation, timeout, I/O and infrastructure errors are UNVERIFIABLE and
  fail closed without authorizing Trash or downstream mutation.
- Other audio codecs retain the prior full-decode status checks. Header-only
  runs, reversible tag tests, corruption defaults, Deep Audio Check default off,
  Dry Run behavior, transfer and updater paths remain unchanged.

  Requirements, Manual, FAQ, release metadata and the complete source bundle.

TLO v1.7 Build 563
====================

Staged Copy/Delete Original and live transfer consolidation (F-05/F-11)
--------------------------------------------------------------------
- Cross-filesystem Copy/Delete now copies complete source tree to an owned .tlo-partial-* sibling, verifies recursive paths/sizes and SHA-256 bytes, atomically publishes without replacing existing destinations, and only then deletes the source. Failure before publication preserves source; failed source deletion preserves the fully verified destination and reports recovery state.
- Same-filesystem Copy/Delete now atomically renames the complete source show into an excluded .tlo-partial-* sibling, then publishes with a second native atomic no-overwrite rename. On ordinary publish failure, attempt a safe atomic rollback; if rollback fails, preserve and report the exact staged recovery tree. Never move folder children piecemeal into a normal final show name.
- Tag Copy and Copy/Delete share the active staging/verification/publication helper. Ordinary Tag Copy retains the source and uses path/size verification, while cross-filesystem Copy/Delete requires SHA-256 before deletion.
- Remove obsolete live reservation/child-move helpers and migrate the historical racing-destination test to active atomic publication. Add live-path fault-injection, destination race, SHA-256 rejection, rollback/rollback-failure, and abrupt-worker-exit tests.
- Version, Requirements, Manual, FAQ, and bundle metadata are current. GitHub Build Process stays v108.

TLO v1.7 Build 562
====================

Staged and atomic ordinary Tag Copy (F-05 first phase)
------------------------------------------------------
- Inventory and GUI Tag ordinary Tag Copy now stage the full source tree under an exclusively created .tlo-partial-* sibling directory; Phase 1 excludes that prefix.
- Verify the complete source/staged recursive folder structure and relative file sizes before publishing; reject symbolic links after copying to prevent raced-in links from being published.
- Publish the verified tree in one atomic, no-overwrite directory rename. Concurrent destination collisions are retried with normal (copyN)/(altN) naming. Native Linux renameat2(RENAME_NOREPLACE), macOS renamex_np(RENAME_EXCL), and Windows rename no-overwrite semantics are used; unsupported atomic publish fails closed.
- Interrupted or forcibly terminated workers may leave a clearly marked staging tree but cannot expose an incomplete final-name copy. Normal exceptions clean up only this invocation's staging tree. Source is never removed by ordinary Tag Copy.
- Reject Tag Copy targets nested inside the source tree. The Copy/Delete Original engine is deliberately unchanged; its staged migration is Build 563.
- Add behavior/fault-injection tests for live-path verification, ordinary exceptions, destination races, source preservation, and abrupt subprocess termination. Update the Requirements, Manual, FAQ, versioned assets and release verification. GitHub Build Process remains v108.

TLO v1.7 Build 561
====================

Trusted native Windows PowerShell resolution for drive scheduling (F-08)
-----------------------------------------------------------------------
- Native Windows physical-drive lookup obtains the Windows directory using the existing trusted GetSystemWindowsDirectoryW/GetWindowsDirectoryW helper and runs the absolute System32\WindowsPowerShell\v1.0\powershell.exe path. No PATH, current-directory, or environment-variable executable search is permitted on this native code path.
- The trusted directory API being unavailable or returning an unsafe path yields an unknown physical-drive identity and falls back to existing visible-volume grouping instead of launching an untrusted executable.
- WSL continues to resolve powershell.exe via its separate interop path; PowerShell commands and normal scan/grouping behavior otherwise remain unchanged.
- Add behavior tests for a planted executable in PATH/current directory, malformed API directory values, invalid executable paths and drive letters, API failure, and WSL behavior; add a native-Windows path verification test.
- Requirements, User Manual, FAQ, source README, version metadata and release verification updated. GitHub Build Process stays at v108.

TLO v1.7 Build 560
====================

Native Windows Recycle Bin fail-closed protection (F-07)
--------------------------------------------------------
- Before a corruption-driven Windows Trash operation, conservatively verify a fixed local NTFS drive, shell Recycle Bin query, per-volume recycling enablement/size quota, and absence of an Explorer no-recycle policy. Unavailable or unconfirmable support becomes Keep-and-report; there is no direct permanent-delete fallback.
- The COM call is still IFileOperation with recycle-only and early-failure flags, without a NoConfirmation option. A failed folder or file Trash operation stops further corruption-driven Trash attempts for that show, marks the show unverifiable and suppresses downstream mutation; previously successfully recycled paths remain pruned.
- Distinguish WSL gio/Linux Trash (possibly a .Trash-UID folder on a mounted drive) from native Windows Recycle Bin in Manual and FAQ. Preserve Dry Run, existing corruption defaults, and other operating systems' Trash code.
- Native Windows runtime integration across normal NTFS, disabled/quota Recycle Bin, removable/network drives still requires Windows verification. Preflight cannot rule out an external configuration change between checks and Shell execution.
- Add Build 560 unit/behavior tests; GitHub Build Process remains v108 unchanged.

TLO v1.7 Build 559
====================

Windows Add Shows cleanup script volume-label safety (F-06)
-----------------------------------------------------------
- Each active Windows deleteReplacedFolders.bat entry checks the connected drive's actual volume label against the saved expected label before issuing rmdir; on mismatch, disconnection, missing or unusable PowerShell, or label query error, that entry is skipped.
- The expected label is carried to a locally rooted Windows PowerShell/.NET DriveInfo predicate as UTF-16LE Base64, avoiding cmd.exe control-flow interpretation of label punctuation, percent signs, and Unicode. No PATH-resolved PowerShell executable is used.
- Entries for unlabeled source volumes retain the visible [] designation but generate only a SKIPPED message and a commented-out example delete command. Multiple entries remain independent, with per-entry continue labels. A single editable TLO_DELETE_TARGET assignment supplies both the guarded drive and the deletion target; editing drive letters cannot accidentally guard one drive and delete another. A different expected volume label requires regeneration of the entry.
- Preserves the existing target-root/path rejection rules and POSIX cleanup behavior; updates Requirements, Manual, FAQ, and executable/fault-injection tests. GitHub Build Process remains v108 unchanged.

TLO v1.7 Build 557
====================

Optional complete-stream Deep Audio Check (F-02 phase 2)
----------------------------------------------------------
- Adds a default-off Deep Audio Check checkbox directly beneath Proper Grammar and the shared --deep-audio-check CLI option for inventory and standalone Tag workflows.
- When enabled, pre-mutation corruption validation uses TLO's application-bundled ffmpeg to decode each complete audio stream after the normal header/metadata check and, during real tagging, after the Build 556 reversible tag-write probe.

TLO v1.7 Build 556
====================

Reversible tagging-mode corruption validation (F-02 phase 1)
-------------------------------------------------------------
- Keeps non-tagging corruption detection lightweight: normal inventory validates the audio header/metadata parse only.
- When a real tagging operation is active (Tag In Place, Tag Copy, Tag Copy/Delete Original, or standalone Tag), corruption assessment also proves that each taggable audio file can accept a metadata write.
- The write probe uses a TLO-private temporary metadata value, reopens the file to verify persistence, restores the exact prior probe-tag value or prior absence, and reopens again to verify restoration before later mutation is allowed.
- Filesystem, permission, resource, validator-infrastructure, or restoration/verification failures remain UNVERIFIABLE and fail closed; a tag-format/write failure with no such underlying cause is classified as corruption.
- Full Inventory Dry Run never enables the temporary tag write.

TLO v1.7 Build 551
====================

Windows local-path hardening (R-02 / R-03; L-05 / L-11)
---------------------------------------------------------
- Centralizes Windows reserved device-name validation in tlo_security.windows_reserved_folder_name(). The shared rule now rejects the Microsoft-reserved superscript forms COM¹-COM³ and LPT¹-LPT³ in addition to the existing CON/PRN/AUX/NUL and ASCII COM1-COM9/LPT1-LPT9 forms.

TLO v1.7 Build 550
====================

Norton scanner receipt integrity (R-01 / L-10)
-------------------------------------------------
- Custom Norton scanner commands are parsed as argv and executed directly with shell=False; shell chaining/wrappers can no longer be used to create a misleading Norton-clean receipt.
- The executable that will actually run is resolved before scanning. Its basename must be an approved Norton scanner name. On Windows it must resolve beneath a standard Program Files tree and have a valid Authenticode signature whose signer identifies Norton.
- Norton scan receipts now record the resolved scanner executable path, executable SHA-256, Authenticode status, and signer subject in addition to the existing scanner/result/artifact hashes.
- Adds Build 550 regressions proving `norton::true norton.exe {path}` is rejected, executable resolution cannot be redirected to a differently named program, custom execution is shell-free, and receipt identity fields are bound to the resolved executable.

TLO v1.7 Build 549
====================

Small robustness fixes (P2-L1 / L-07 / L-08 / L-12)
------------------------------------------------------
- eTreeDB textual-month date normalization rejects oversized day tokens and contains ValueError/OverflowError so malformed remote date strings become unusable dates rather than unexpected exceptions.
- eTreeDB and setlist.fm HTTP-error handlers contain oversized/invalid bounded error bodies and still raise ETreeDBError/SetlistFMError instead of leaking ResponseTooLargeError/ValueError.
- tlo-deleteDupes FLAC validation treats a missing ffmpeg return code as unverifiable rather than healthy, and runs ffmpeg with LC_ALL=C so infrastructure/error classification is stable on platforms that honor that locale variable.
- lock_is_held() probes existing lock files without creating parent directories or writing placeholder bytes. Owned-lock acquisition briefly retries a transient nonblocking collision before reporting busy, reducing false-busy results caused by a concurrent probe.

TLO v1.7 Build 546
====================

Build/audit supply-chain hardening (M-04 / L-13 / L-14)
---------------------------------------------------------
- The hash-locked pip-audit helper now removes inherited PIP_* settings from its subprocess environment and invokes pip with --isolated for audit-tool installation and pip check, preventing user/site pip configuration from redirecting or altering those operations.
- The audit helper now audits requirements-audit.txt itself with the same strict --require-hashes --no-deps --disable-pip policy before trusting that toolchain to audit release dependencies.
- In addition to the normal host-platform audit of requirements-build.txt, the helper generates and audits a temporary marker-neutral exact-pin view so Windows/macOS-only pins such as pefile, pywin32-ctypes, colorama, and macholib are checked even when the audit runs on Linux.
- The local Windows packaging script no longer accepts signtool.exe from PATH. When Authenticode signing is requested, it selects only an x64 SignTool beneath the installed Windows Kits root, requires a valid Microsoft Authenticode signature on SignTool itself, records its exact path/SHA-256/file version/signature status/signer, and runs signtool verify /pa /all /v on every signed executable before malware scanning.
- Adds Build 546 regression coverage for pip-environment isolation, marker-neutral lock generation, audit-tool self-auditing, all-platform audit invocation, and the local SignTool trust/verification contract.
- GitHub Build Process remains v108 unchanged; no process-package change is required because the official process already provides the trusted SignTool gate and invokes the source-bundle audit helper.

TLO v1.7 Build 545
====================

Temporary-tree and symlink safety (P2-L3 / L-06)
--------------------------------------------------
- Aligns every reviewed TLO-owned transient folder with the shared recursive prune policy: Tag Copy partial trees now begin .tlo-partial-, case-only rename intermediates begin .tlo-case-rename-, and cross-filesystem reverse-restore trees begin .tlo-restore-. Interrupted leftovers therefore remain excluded from inventory, Copy Request discovery, and duplicate comparison.
- Adds .tlo-case-rename- to the shared tlo_path_policy temporary-tree prefixes and centralizes the reverse temporary-path constructor so its actual generated name is testable against that policy.
- Copy Requests now repeat the symbolic-link rejection immediately at the copy boundary. shutil.copytree preserves links instead of dereferencing them, and the completed TLO-owned temporary tree is rejected if any link is present before verification/publication.
- Exact recursive tree comparison now records symbolic links separately from regular files/directories and compares readlink() target text. It no longer hashes or sizes through links, so a symlink can never compare equal to a regular file merely because the linked-to bytes match.
- Adds Build 545 regressions for all real transient-folder creators, file and directory symlink comparison, a link-versus-regular-file mismatch, a changed link target, the evaluation-to-copy symlink race, and an ordinary no-link copy control.
- GitHub Build Process remains v108 unchanged.

TLO v1.7 Build 544
====================

Rollback-failure safety (P2-M1)
---------------------------------
- Addresses review finding P2-M1 only. The change is limited to recovery/error reporting for destructive mutation paths; normal successful behavior is unchanged.
- Case-only folder renames now log a failed rollback and raise an error that states the rename could not be undone and names the actual path that still holds the folder, including the hidden temporary sibling when applicable.
- Manual Updates now records rollback failures for generated-setlist cleanup, prior-setlist restoration, bootlist.csv restoration, folder rename rollback, and unidentifiedShows.txt restoration. The user-facing ManualUpdateError reports that automatic rollback was incomplete and identifies the affected current folder/state file where practical.
- Sibling-collection consolidation now records failures restoring the complete-path log or rolling back the collection container. An incomplete rollback is surfaced with the current recovery-container path instead of silently re-raising only the initiating error.
- Windows Recycle Bin COM-interface release failures remain non-fatal cleanup events but are now recorded through the low-noise suppressed-exception diagnostic logger.
- Adds Build 544 fault-injection coverage for failed case-only rename rollback, Manual Updates folder/bootlist/unidentified rollback, and sibling-collection log/folder rollback, plus a contract preventing direct `except Exception: pass` in the four reviewed destructive-operation modules.

The regression proves one valid signature succeeds and rejects a flipped signature byte, changed signed metadata, wrong key_id, and a same-length signature value at or above the modulus. The private fixture key is not included in the source tree.

TLO v1.7 Build 528
====================

Security/build-consistency remediation
--------------------------------------
- Update trust now requires a pinned RSA modulus of at least 3072 bits and a mandatory signed release_tag that exactly matches the candidate GitHub release tag. Schema 1 semantics remain fixed; any future signed expiry/freeze policy requires a new metadata schema.
- CSV formula escaping/unescaping is round-trip safe even when the original value already starts with apostrophes.
- SHN conversion passes ffmpeg an absolute local source path, reserves the final FLAC target exclusively, and will not overwrite a target that appears during conversion.
- Update settings and pruned logs commit via same-directory atomic replacement; log pruning uses surrogateescape so undecodable bytes are preserved.
- tlo-search no longer falls back to the developer path /mnt/c/b, requires a supported TLOHome source, and derives its internal/display version from tlo_version.
- Windows RTF files opened by TLO use the registered Windows handler rather than Notepad.
- Native tlo-gi packaging explicitly collects mutagen and imageio_ffmpeg and executes a frozen dependency/ffmpeg smoke test. Native build helpers reject a supplied bundle number that differs from tlo_version.BUNDLE_BUILD; the Windows local default output is user-writable.
- Release scan receipts use approved scanner identities and portable/non-absolute artifact roots; arbitrary successful commands do not satisfy the official scanner requirement.
- GitHub Build Process v102 coordinates the build-number, frozen tlo-gi dependency/smoke, and scanner-identity gates.

TLO v1.7 Build 527
===================
- Restores Windows Performance Mode priority transitions: Gentle uses idle priority, Balanced uses below-normal, and later Fast/Extreme explicitly restore the process priority class captured at application startup.
- Avoids permanently lowering the long-lived GUI/CLI process on Linux, WSL, and macOS. Gentle/Balanced niceness is applied only in spawned inventory worker processes; serial parent-process work remains at normal niceness and still honors worker/throttling controls.
- Clamps setlist.fm rate-limit spacing to at most one configured minimum interval and treats persisted last-request timestamps more than one interval in the future as corrupt state rather than sleeping without bound.
- Enforces one inventory-mutating run per TLOHome with an owned cross-process advisory lock held for the run. Search and unrelated read-only operations are not blocked.
- Replaces Copy Request stale-lock remove/recreate recovery with an OS advisory lock held for the operation. Crashed owners release the OS lock automatically, so a later operation reuses the lock file without racing a fresh owner.
- Adds Build 527 behavior coverage for Gentle/Balanced -> Fast/Extreme priority transitions, POSIX parent/worker priority behavior, future/corrupt rate state, inventory-instance exclusion/recovery, and live/crashed Copy Request lock ownership.
- Updates Requirements, User Manual, FAQ, source metadata, tests, and package naming for Build 527. GitHub Build Process remains v101.

TLO v1.7 Build 526

TLO v1.7 Build 512

Main GUI naming, mode-aware Max Workers, and Copy Request trailing-* folder expansion

- Changes the main GUI title bar from "TLO Inventory GUI" to "TLO Main GUI".
- Changes the bold main-window heading from "Traders Little Organizer™ Inventory App" to "Traders Little Organizer™ Main".
- Changes the automatic Max Workers value to follow the selected Performance Mode instead of always using the machine CPU count: gentle = 1; balanced = min(2, CPU count); fast = CPU count; extreme = min(4 x CPU count, 64), minimum 1.
- Selecting a different Performance Mode resets Max Workers to that mode's automatic ceiling; the field remains editable afterward for another positive ceiling.
- Adds one-time trailing-* expansion for direct Copy Request paths before copying starts.
- c:\TLO* expands to each immediate directory in c:\ whose name begins with TLO (possibly more than one directory).
- c:\TLO\* expands to each immediate child directory of c:\TLO and does not copy the c:\TLO directory itself.
- Wildcard matching selects directories only; regular files and symbolic-link directories are excluded.
- The same trailing-* expansion applies to direct path lines read from Copy Request .txt inputs.
- Wildcard parents must be accessible and at least one directory must match when a new request is created.
- Expanded wildcard paths are persisted in the Copy Request snapshot, so later Preview/Continue/Process All operations do not re-expand or require the original wildcard source to remain unchanged.
- Adds Build 512 regression coverage and updates Requirements, User Manual, FAQ, release metadata, and current-version contracts.

TLO v1.7 Build 511

Copy Request Path(s) mixed input and persistent aggregation

- Renames the New Copy Request input label from Request File to Path(s).
- Uses the same semicolon-separated entry and optional matching-double-quote rules as the main Inventory Path(s) field.
- Allows one Copy Request Path(s) expression to mix direct filesystem paths/volumes, ordinary Artist/date/Show request text, and .txt request-list files.
- Native-Windows drag/drop accepts one or more folders/.txt files and appends later drops instead of replacing the existing Path(s) value.
- Browse .txt can append one or more request-list files to the current Path(s) value.
- Expands each .txt request file immediately when a new request is created, using the same blank-line/#/REM whole-line comment convention as toBeInventoried.txt.
- Aggregates expanded .txt items with direct Path(s) items in input order before Preview/copy processing begins.
- Persists the expanded aggregate under TLOHome/copyRequests; Continue, Process All, Preview, and resume use the saved aggregate and do not require the contributing .txt files to remain available or unchanged.
- Determines an existing Path(s)-based request identity from the Path(s) expression plus Destination before opening any .txt input, allowing a saved request to reopen after a contributing list file is moved or deleted.
- Retains compatibility with Copy Requests created by older builds from a single Request File.
- Adds Build 511 regression coverage and updates Requirements, User Manual, FAQ, release metadata, and current-version contracts.

TLO v1.7 Build 509

Review remediation, Copy Request lock safety, and replacement-folder cleanup

- Corrects Copy Request PID liveness on Windows using Windows process APIs instead of os.kill(pid, 0); POSIX PermissionError is treated as evidence that the process exists.
- Records a process-start identity in Copy Request lock/temp ownership metadata so recycled PIDs are not mistaken for the original owner.
- Adds explicit GUI confirmation for clearing an aged stale Destination lock left by another computer or renamed host.
- Renames the Windows Add Shows cleanup file to deleteReplacedFolders.bat. Each entry identifies its recorded source volume as [Volume Label], including [] for an unlabeled volume, and uses a fully quoted rooted path. The batch file does not require that volume to be connected and is intended to be edited for current drive letters and additional master/backup copies.
- Removes incorrect caret escaping from values already inside double quotes; percent signs remain doubled for cmd.exe expansion.
- Preserves standard RTF smart quote, dash, nonbreaking-space, spacing, bullet, optional-hyphen, and nonbreaking-hyphen controls.
- Validates the normal setlist.fm key before consuming a rate slot and converts Unicode/value request-construction errors to SetlistFMError without exposing the key.

TLO v1.7 Build 507

Safety, recovery, and review remediation

Delete-script safety
- Canonicalizes Add Shows cleanup targets before use and rejects literal .. traversal, drive/mount roots, drive-relative forms, TLOHome, and ancestors of TLOHome. A cleanup target must be at least one directory below its recognized volume/mount root.
- Preserves the bootlist volume identity through deleteBackupFolders generation and writes an execution-time volume-label guard before destructive commands.
- Starts each Add Shows cleanup session with a fresh deleteBackupFolders script, archiving the prior script under TLOHome/logs instead of silently appending commands across sessions.
- Windows cleanup scripts select UTF-8 code page 65001 so non-ASCII paths remain usable.

Copy Request transaction and cancellation
- Treats every Copy Request pass as a recoverable transaction. The current target/temp pair and transaction phase are persisted in request state before destination mutation.
- Holds exclusive request and destination locks for the duration of a pass so concurrent TLO instances cannot sweep each other's live copies or race request state.
- Recovers only TLO-owned pending reservations and temporary trees recorded by that request. Unrecorded or non-empty destination folders are never removed by recovery.
- Keeps TLO ownership metadata through verification/publication and clears it only after completion state is durably recorded, closing the unmarked-temp crash window.
- Adds Cancel Copy. Cancellation is checked between files, cleans only the current TLO-owned pending transaction, preserves completed items, and leaves the request Open for continuation.

RTF, setlist.fm, and input handling
- Replaces the RTF regex stripping path with group-aware conversion that skips font/color/style/info/picture/header/footer destinations, honors ANSI code pages and Unicode escapes, handles TextEdit line breaks, and preserves escaped braces/backslashes and tabs.
- setlist.fm upgrade comparison uses UTF-8 bytes so malformed/non-ASCII pasted values cannot crash GUI startup. Control/invisible key characters produce a non-secret invalid-character status.
- Resolves normal and upgrade keys by process/session tier first and uses persisted Windows values only as fallback for missing values, without logging either key.
- Legacy binary .doc candidates are explicitly treated as unsupported rather than decoded as arbitrary text.

Filesystem and updater hardening
- Centralizes OS-managed directory names and TLO temporary-tree prefixes in tlo_path_policy.py. Phase 1 prunes macOS system folders and TLO temp trees in addition to the existing Windows system folders.
- Reuses the bounded shared DOCX reader for sibling-collection text extraction instead of a separate ElementTree XML parser.
- Restricts the unused open_paths helper so Windows .bat files are never launched through os.startfile.
- ZIP inspection now rejects absolute/drive-qualified/traversal member names, symlink members, excessive member counts, excessive expanded size, and unreasonable compression ratios before accepting an update package.
- SHA-256 remains mandatory. Independent signed release metadata is documented as a future security design requiring a signing key outside ordinary GitHub publishing credentials.

Requirements, documentation, and tests
- Adds Definitions and requirement-keyword conventions; distinguishes GUI labels/actions from internal concepts.
- Adds stable IDs for the new destructive/safety rules, moves destructive safety material into its owning sections, and expands the traceability matrix with real module/test mappings for current features.
- Documents Copy Request interruption/concurrency/cancel behavior, RTF semantics, delete-script safety, updater ZIP validation, shared pruning, and the intentionally retained non-instantiated TaggerWindow.
- Converts the supplied Build 506 probes into permanent corrected-behavior regression coverage and adds transaction, cancellation, delete-script, ZIP, pruning, .doc, and RTF fixtures.
- TaggerWindow remains in place by explicit owner decision and is not the normative implementation of GUI Tag behavior.

Verification
- Build 507 remediation module: 22 passed.
- Full suite: 1,598 passed.

TLO v1.7 Build 505

Targeted review remediation and Copy Request safety

Metadata / text decoding
- Setlist header metadata and date-fallback text now use the shared bounded text reader rather than independent UTF-16-first decoders.
- cp1252/latin-1 setlists no longer decode as UTF-16 mojibake merely because their byte length is even.
- Date fallback reads selected RTF and DOCX through the shared format-aware reader instead of treating container/control bytes as plain text.
- Scored decoder call sites used by setlist selection and sibling collection handling now converge on the same byte decoder.

Copy and verification safety
- Tree verification and verification-related size/path walks now fail closed when a subtree cannot be enumerated.
- Copy Requests reject Destination/source overlap in either direction, including Destination equal to the source. Source-as-target is never recorded as Already Satisfied/Complete.
- Whole-volume Copy Requests consistently exclude Windows System Volume Information and $RECYCLE.BIN plus macOS .Spotlight-V100, .fseventsd, .Trashes, and .TemporaryItems from sizing, copying, and verification; Preview/reporting identifies the exclusions.
- A bare volume label that also matches an Artist/alias or exact Show is ambiguous and is rejected with guidance to use [Volume].
- Copy Request destinations are reserved before copying rather than finalized by a check-then-replace race. Stale legacy .tlo-copy-* temporary folders are swept at pass start.
- Direct-source Show coverage filters lexical path candidates before filesystem probes.

setlist.fm / GUI hardening
- setlist.fm upgrade availability now requires SETLISTFMUPGRADE_API_KEY or SETLISTFM_UPGRADE_API_KEY to exactly match SETLISTFM_API_KEY. Key values are never logged.
- --setlistfm-upgrade is inert when --setlistfm-lookup is off rather than raising an unnecessary CLI validation error.
- On Windows, current persisted User/System environment values take precedence over a stale inherited process value; expandable registry values are expanded.
- Delete Extra Tags is also gated in config construction so future programmatic paths cannot leave it active without a tagging mode.
- setlist.fm rate-limit constants are shared instead of copied into multiple GUI/tag configuration builders.

Requirements / manual / FAQ / packaging
- Reconciles the v504 review findings R1-R7 and U1-U4: Section 20 now precedes the appendices, normative rules no longer carry build-history narration, recent manual notes are chronological, Copy Request hazards are documented, and Delete Extra Tags capitalization is consistent.
- The source bundle no longer ships superseded SHA256SUMS_v500-v504.txt or BUILD_VERIFICATION_v500-v504.txt at its root; those prior verification artifacts are archived in old-change-logs.zip.
- Adds executable Build 505 regressions derived from the supplied v504 probes, including permission-error verification, cp1252/RTF/DOCX decoding, overlap/self-copy, Artist/volume collisions, system-folder exclusions, and upgrade-key mismatch.

TLO v1.7 Build 504

GUI option-state and setlist.fm upgrade environment detection

- When Tag in Place, Tag Copy, and Tag Copy/Delete Original are all off, Delete Extra Tags is now forced unchecked as well as disabled/greyed.
- The three tagging modes remain mutually exclusive; selecting any one enables Delete Extra Tags again but does not re-check it automatically.
- setlist.fm upgrade environment detection still requires an explicit upgrade-enable variable, but now accepts both SETLISTFMUPGRADE_API_KEY and SETLISTFM_UPGRADE_API_KEY.
- On Windows, TLO checks the current persisted User and System environment values when the launching process inherited a stale environment snapshot, so variables added through Environment Variables or setx are recognized without exposing the API key.
- Normal setlist.fm still requires SETLISTFM_API_KEY, and the upgrade value is expected to contain the same API key.

TLO v1.7 Build 503

Copy Requests direct path/volume items

- A Copy Request item that is a rooted filesystem path, visible volume label, [Volume], or [Volume] path is treated as a direct tree copy rather than Artist/Show matching text.
- Direct path items copy the named directory tree to the selected Destination using the source leaf name; whole-volume items use the requested volume label as the Destination folder name.
- Direct items participate in the same whole-pass free-space preflight and temporary-copy/tree-verification/finalize safety flow as ordinary Copy Request folders.
- Every inventoried Show whose source folder is fully contained in a successful direct copy is recorded in normal completed-Show tracking. Mixed direct and Artist/date/Show request lines are therefore de-conflicted in the same pass and on future Continue passes.
- The direct item itself has persistent completion state so an already completed path/volume item is not recopied on resume.
- Unavailable direct paths/volumes wait for accessibility instead of being interpreted as Artist names.
- Copy Request preview/reporting includes direct-item availability, waiting, completion, copied history, and failures.

Tests / documentation / packaging
- Adds Build 503 behavior coverage for absolute paths, volume labels, [Volume] path syntax, same-pass de-confliction, persistent resume tracking, unavailable-path waiting, and preview/free-space integration.
- Updates Section 14.6, User Manual, FAQ, version stamps, and current-release contracts to v1.7 Build 503.

===== Consolidated archival history (previously in separate ZIP) =====


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v125.txt =====
TLO Inventory v125 changes

- Non-compliant artist-no-space-date handling now checks eligible path components for a DB-backed artist before treating String1 as an abbreviation.
- If a DB-backed path artist is found, that artist is used and the abbreviation lookup is skipped.
- If no DB-backed path artist is found, the existing abbreviation lookup rules are used.
- If incompatible DB-backed path artists are found before abbreviation lookup, the show remains in artist conflict rather than allowing the abbreviation to override the conflict.
- Requirements and user manual updated.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v126.txt =====
TLO Inventory v126

- Non-compliant mode now aggregates related release-part music folders under a common parent when the music folder name is, or ends with, a wrapper-like part marker such as CD1, CD 1, Disc 1, Disk3, Part 2, or Set 1.
- Exact wrapper part folders such as Jayhawks - Big Release/CD1, /CD2, and /CD3 are processed as one logical show rooted at Jayhawks - Big Release.
- Wrapper-suffixed part folders such as Marcus, Jay/Big Release CD 1 and Big Release CD 2 are processed as one logical album named Big Release under the common parent. The path is checked for a DB-backed artist and can resolve to a show such as Jay Marcus - Big Release.
- Aggregated release-part groups collect music files and unique setlist files from all related part folders.
- Postprocess exports one setlist file for the single bootlist entry by concatenating unique source setlist texts.
- Requirements and user manual updated.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v127.txt =====
TLO Inventory v127

- Non-compliant mode now uses selected setlist file contents as a date fallback after existing date methods fail.
- The fallback runs only after path pattern dates, path date-only search, FLAC DATE tags, and music filename dates fail to produce a usable date.
- The fallback examines setlist file contents only, not the setlist filename or path.
- For aggregated groups, one leading-content date candidate is collected from each unique source setlist and normal date conflict handling is applied across those candidates.
- Requirements and user manual updated.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v128.txt =====
v128

- In non-compliant mode, complete dates now win over less-specific date candidates when there is only one complete date.
- Added recognition of month-year date text such as November2020 as a partial yyyy-mm-xx candidate.
- If non-compliant date candidates still conflict, selected setlist file contents are checked and can validate one conflicting candidate.
- Requirements and user manual updated.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v129.txt =====
v129

- Added a Louisiana guard to non-compliant local setlist metadata location parsing.
- LA and L.A. city aliases from cities.csv are accepted as Los Angeles only when explicit California evidence immediately follows, such as LA CA or LA, California.
- Terminal or trailing LA remains available as the Louisiana state abbreviation.
- Requirements document and user manual updated in the relevant location/state/setlist-metadata sections rather than appended.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v130.txt =====
v130

- Non-compliant path String2 parsing now accepts full U.S. state names as state/location anchors and normalizes them to two-letter state codes.
- Full state names can appear at the end of String2 or immediately before a trailing source/recording tail.
- Short city/state/source fragments using full state names can populate location without inventing a one-word city from the final token.
- Non-compliant setlist venue/location fallback now scans only header/metadata lines and stops before numbered track lists.
- Setlist header location parsing now accepts city + full state name with or without a comma, such as Boca Raton,Florida.
- This prevents track titles such as HANG ON SLOOPY from being parsed as city HANG / region ON.
- Updated requirements document and user manual to place these parsing rules in the relevant sections.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v131.txt =====
v131
- Rejects unusable .txt setlist candidates before ranking/selection, including empty, unreadable, binary-like, NULL-filled, no-readable-alphabetic-content, checksum-only, and housekeeping text files.
- Decodes UTF-16 setlist text before NULL/binary rejection so valid UTF-16 files are not mistaken for NULL-filled files.
- Restricts parent-directory setlist lookup to directly related parent/child structures: one-child show parents, exact wrapper/common release parts, or wrapper-suffixed siblings sharing the same stripped release base.
- Prevents broad artist/collection/storage parent directories with unrelated child folders from donating a parent-level setlist to one child show.
- Updates requirements and user manual in the relevant setlist identification and setlist search behavior sections.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v132.txt =====
v132

- Added compact date normalization in the active Phase 2/3 date extractor:
  - yyyymmdd normalizes to yyyy-mm-dd when it is a valid calendar date.
  - compact plausible year ranges such as yyyyyyyy normalize to yyyy-yyyy when both four-digit halves are plausible years.
  - dashed plausible year ranges yyyy-yyyy are recognized and preserved.
- Updated generated setlist filename fallback sanitization so date/range dashes are not stripped from fallback SHOW_NAME text.
- Updated generated setlist filename Date component sanitization so compact Date values are normalized before filename creation.
- Updated requirements and user manual to document the compact-date/range and setlist filename behavior.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v133.txt =====
v133

- Setlist metadata location parsing now treats two-letter state/province abbreviations as case-sensitive. For example, ME is Maine but Me in a song title is not.
- Track-list boundary detection now stops before plain track-number lines such as "01 Title" and "1 Title", and before track-number/time/title lines such as "01 [08:23] Title".
- Setlist metadata lookup now skips leading false headers made of prose/commentary/noise before the actual artist/venue/location header block.
- Requirements and user manual updated in the setlist metadata and state/location sections.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v134.txt =====
v134
- Requirements documentation cleanup only; no metadata-extraction logic changes beyond version labels.
- Corrects wrong Section 9.1 references to the date-conflict/missing-date table; the table is in Section 10.1.
- Fixes compliant-mode String1 - String2 wording so String2 is no longer mislabeled as non-compliant in Section 8.3.
- Replaces the inline multi-part city prefix list with a reference to the authoritative Appendix E5 list.
- Consolidates duplicated setlist.fm rate-limit/call-cap wording into Appendix I and keeps Section 9 as the behavioral reference.
- Trims repeated compliant-mode FLAC tag exclusion, --myTLO, aggregated-wrapper, and blank-show reporting language to canonical references.
- Clarifies usable online lookup result requirements, parent-folder setlist eligibility timing, missing venues.txt behavior, pause/resume granularity, slam normalization/sanitization, --max-workers capping, startup version source, and log-token exhaustion behavior.
- Updates the requirements DOCX and re-labels the user manual for the v134 bundle.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v135.txt =====
v135

- Rejects prose/commentary and long sentence-like lines as setlist venue/location evidence.
- Prevents venue matches embedded in recording notes, such as "stage" inside a long taper comment.
- Requires stronger location evidence for city-list matches; city-only words such as "Nice" are not accepted as a location by themselves.
- Preserves full day precision from compact music filename date candidates instead of degrading them to yyyy-mm-xx.
- Normalizes compact and separated date tokens before generated setlist filename sanitization, including artist-date strings with extra separators.
- Updates requirements and user manual for the revised setlist metadata and filename-date rules.

- Accepts labels only near the start of a logical line after optional light decoration, not when embedded in prose or long commentary.
- Keeps the existing protections against track-list/title false positives, long prose/commentary venue matches, and case-insensitive state-code matches.
- Updates the requirements document and user manual to describe explicit setlist metadata labels.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v137.txt =====

- Preserves date dashes when a String1 - String2 show has a date-like String2 value, e.g. Iris Dement - 1998-10-03 exports as IrisDement-1998-10-03.txt.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v139.txt =====
TLO Inventory v139

- Online lookup precedence update:
  - Explicit labeled setlist metadata, such as Venue:, City:, Country:, and Location:, is now treated as authoritative local source metadata.
  - eTreeDB and setlist.fm may fill blank fields and may replace path-derived or heuristic venue/location fields, but they no longer silently overwrite explicit labeled setlist venue/location values.
  - If an online lookup disagrees with explicit setlist metadata, the setlist value is retained and an observation is logged.
- eTreeDB location normalization update:
  - Recognized non-US country names returned in eTreeDB's generic state/location field are stored as COUNTRY rather than REGION.
  - Example: Copenhagen / Denmark becomes CITY=Copenhagen, COUNTRY=Denmark, REGION blank.
- Requirements and user manual updated for the new precedence rule.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v140.txt =====
TLO Inventory v140

- Non-compliant String1 - String2 update:
  - If String2 is date-like and consumes the full String2 value, such as Devo - 2010-08-01, String2 is treated as the show date, not as an album name.
  - True album-style String1 - String2 folders continue to use String2 as ALBUM_NAME and show as Artist - Album Name.
- Setlist metadata update:
  - Explicit setlist label extraction now still runs when a selected setlist exists for a String1 - String2 path, so labels can fill missing venue/location fields.
  - Venue labels that include trailing comma-separated location text are split when the trailing location validates. Example: Venue: Osheaga Festival, Parc Jean-Drapeau, Montréal, QC becomes venue Osheaga Festival, Parc Jean-Drapeau and location Montréal QC.
- Requirements and user manual updated.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v159.txt =====
TLO Inventory v159

Date handling and analysis updates:
- Kept FOUR_PLUS_FOUR_RE and refined its normalization behavior.
- Space-form YYYY MMDD / YYYY DDMM now must resolve to a valid date; US MMDD is preferred, with European DDMM used when US order is invalid.
- Dash-form YYYY-MMDD first checks exact YYYY-YYYY range handling; valid exact ranges remain ranges and non-range dash forms may resolve as compact dates.
- FOUR_PLUS_FOUR_RE now requires a four-digit month/day tail, so compact x-tail forms such as 202x-xxxx are rejected.
- Helper recognizers for setlist selection, setlist filename cleanup, and setlist header detection were updated for the retained FOUR_PLUS_FOUR forms.
- Date audit workbook was pared to separate core date-assignment regex/function families from helper-only recognition patterns.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v160.txt =====
TLO-GI v160 changes
===================

Date parsing updates:
- Restored supported textual date forms that were over-pruned from the v159 analysis list.
- Textual month dates now explicitly cover no-space forms such as 14APR01, 14APRIL01, 14April01, Apr142001, and 2001APR14.
- Textual month dates cover dash, dot, and underscore forms such as 14-Apr-2001, 14.APR.2001, and 14_April_2001.
- Textual month dates cover comma/space variants such as April 14 2001, April 14, 2001, and April 14,2001.
- Month names and abbreviations are accepted only as all-uppercase, all-lowercase, or Title-case; mixed internal casing is rejected.
- All-numeric separated dates accept only dash, dot, underscore, or spaces, with no delimiter mixing except for yyyy mm-dd, yyyy mm.dd, and FOUR_PLUS_FOUR_RE handling.
- FOUR_PLUS_FOUR_RE is retained. Space-form values must resolve to a valid US MMDD or European DDMM date. Dash-form values first check exact yyyy-yyyy range handling before compact-date parsing.

Analysis/docs:
- Rebuilt the date-identification workbook with restored textual cases and helper-recognition separation.
- Updated the requirements document to describe the v160 date handling behavior.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v161.txt =====
TLO-GI v161 changes
====================

Date parsing updates:
- Restored/confirmed support for two-digit years and one-digit numeric month/day components in separated numeric dates.
- Numeric-only path/filename dates allow dash, dot, underscore, or spaces, including optional spaces around punctuation such as " - ", "- ", " -", and " . "; effective separator kinds may not mix.
- Removed the older yyyy mm-dd / yyyy mm.dd mixed-separator exception from general numeric date parsing; the only accepted mixed numeric case is the retained FOUR_PLUS_FOUR_RE form.
- Retained FOUR_PLUS_FOUR_RE behavior for yyyy MMDD / yyyy DDMM and non-range yyyy-MMDD / yyyy-DDMM, with dash forms checked for exact year-range meaning first.
- Added slash-delimited numeric date recognition only for FLAC DATE tags and selected setlist-file content: m/d/yy, mm/dd/yy, m/dd/yy, mm/d/yy, and the corresponding four-digit-year forms, allowing spaces around slashes.
- Slash dates default to US month/day/year when valid; if US order is invalid, European day/month/year is attempted; if both fail, no date is produced.
- European-style path/pattern/filename dates now require confirmation from selected setlist content before they are accepted; if unconfirmed, range candidates may still be considered when supported by setlist collection clues.
- Updated helper-only setlist date-recognition patterns and setlist metadata header recognition to include the restored numeric forms and slash tag/setlist forms.

Validation:
- Ran py_compile across all Python files in the bundle.
- Ran targeted date parser checks for one-digit numeric components, two-digit years, text date forms, strict non-slash numeric separators, FOUR_PLUS_FOUR_RE, slash tag/setlist parsing, and European path-date setlist confirmation.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v188.txt =====
v188

- In compliant mode, video media found under standard video wrapper folders such as VIDEO_TS, BDMV, CERTIFICATE, DVD, BluRay, Video, and Audio_TS now checks the parent folder for the compliant String1 Date String2 and String1 - String2 patterns.
- In compliant mode, when String1 Date String2 and String1 - String2 do not match, String1 Date is now tried; when it matches, the show name is Artist Date.
- In compliant mode, when all found music files are .mp3 and the folder name is a four-digit year from 1959 through 2005, the show name is that year.
- In compliant mode only, malformed dates of the form yyyy-mm dd are repaired to yyyy-mm-dd during folder-name matching; the corrected date appears in the show name and the metadata log records the repair.
- The previous compliant fallback to the folder name remains after the new pattern checks and MP3 year special case fail.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v189.txt =====
v189: Adds the TLO Tagger GUI/CLI workflow and tagPath support for readyForXfer tagging.

- Added tlo_tag_lib.py for shared tagger discovery, metadata extraction, setlist track parsing, and mutagen tag writing.
- Added standalone tlo-tag.py with --TLOHome, --myTLO, --compliant, --tag-path, and optional positional tagPath support.

- Tagger defaults to TLOHome/readyForXfer or myTLO/readyForXfer, while a non-empty tagPath overrides readyForXfer only for tagging.
- Compliant tagging uses String1 Date String2, String1 - String2, and String1 Date rules for artist/album metadata.
- Non-compliant tagging reuses inventory metadata extraction and builds album from identified Date, Venue, and Location.
- Track parsing recognizes common setlist forms including 1 Name, 1Name, 1-Name, 01-Name, 1)Name, 1) Name, and d1t01 Name, then normalizes track numbers to 1-n.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v191.txt =====
TLO Inventory v191 changes

- Recognizes setlist checksum/hash rows in the form filename.ext:<32-64 hex characters>, including numbered audio filenames such as:
  01 Highway Star.flac:81b2cf16b83390c1875f67afb2d37350
- Prevents those filename.ext:<hex> rows from being parsed as tagger track names.
- Treats consecutive filename.ext:<hex> rows as checksum/hash sections for tagger parsing and setlist metadata boundary requirements.
- Adds regression tests for filename.ext:<hex> checksum rows in both setlist metadata boundary handling and tagger track parsing.
- Updates the requirements DOCX to v191 and includes it in the bundle.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v192.txt =====
v192 update notes

- Updated tlo-tag.py / the tlo-ggi.py Tag workflow so tagging discovery uses the same inventory Stage 1/Stage 2 grouping path for music-file discovery, selected setlist discovery, wrapper aggregation, and metadata inputs.
- In non-compliant tagger mode, FLAC tag sampling and show metadata extraction now follow the same inventory group structure before tagging files.
- Changed tagger processing so folders without a selected setlist are no longer skipped solely for that reason. Metadata is still required, but track titles can fall back to audio filenames.
- Added filename-title fallback for no-setlist tagging. Titles are taken from text after a leading track number or d1t01-style filename pattern; files without an identifiable title are tagged as unknown.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v193.txt =====
TLO Inventory v193 changes
==========================

Behavior changes
----------------
- Added Tag During Inventory support for full inventory runs.
- Added a main-window GUI checkbox labeled "Tag During Inventory" to the right of the Compliant checkbox.
- Added --tag-during-inventory to the inventory CLI and GUI launcher so the checkbox can be preselected from the command line.

- If no show name can be determined during inventory-time tagging, Artist and Album tags are written as "Unknown" while track number and title tags are still written when possible.

Tests

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v195.txt =====
CHANGES v195
============

- Prepends the visible volume name in brackets to the search path shown at the top of each per-search-path log, including [] when the volume name is empty.
- Adds startup handling for inventory roots whose visible volume name already appears in bootlist.csv. Matching volumes can be skipped, overwritten, or appended.
- Keeps empty volume names as empty for volume matching rather than substituting an UNK display name.
- Updates postprocess so append/skip decisions preserve existing bootlist rows and setlist files, while overwrite decisions remove matching existing bootlist rows before adding newly inventoried rows.
- Expands video-folder compliant pattern recovery: for folders containing video files, path subdirectories are checked for String1 Date String2, String1 Date, and String1 - String2 patterns so wrapper folders such as VIDEO_TS can resolve from a parent show folder.
- Adds regression tests for bracketed log headers, empty-volume display, volume-action postprocess behavior, and video subdirectory pattern matching.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v196.txt =====
v196 - Group-log volume checks and generated-setlist collision handling
---------------------------------------------------------------------
- Inventory startup now decides whether a queued volume was already addressed by reading the top SEARCH_PATH/header lines in TLOHome/logs/groups*.log, not by scanning bootlist.csv.
- Existing bootlist rows are still filtered during postprocess according to the user's skip/overwrite/re-inventory decision.
- Generated-setlist collision behavior was introduced here and later superseded by v206 size-aware alternate handling.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v197.txt =====
v197 - Explicit search-path volume prefixes

- Main-window Search Path, CLI --search-path, and toBeInventoried.txt entries may start with an explicit bracketed visible volume prefix.
- Accepted forms include [string]E:, [string]E:\path, [string]/mnt/e, and [string]/mnt/e/path. A space after the closing bracket is optional.
- The bracketed text is normalized by stripping brackets and outer whitespace and is written to logs as [Volume] Path.
- If no bracketed volume is supplied, the visible volume is the valid empty value [] for both log output and existing-volume comparison.
- Existing-volume decisions compare normalized visible volume names from queued paths and groups*.log top SEARCH_PATH/header lines case-insensitively.
- Search path physical normalization continues to support Windows drive paths and /mnt/<drive> paths across Windows/WSL-style entry forms.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v198.txt =====
v198 - Existing-volume log reuse policy

- When the existing-volume startup prompt returns Skip, matching search paths are still removed from the run.
- When the prompt returns Overwrite, the inventory reuses the existing groups/log token for that volume and opens the related conf/dead/comp/dups/groups/meta/tag logs in overwrite mode. It does not allocate a new token/log set for that queued path.
- When the prompt returns Re-inventory, the inventory reuses the existing groups/log token for that volume and opens the related conf/dead/comp/dups/groups/meta/tag logs in append mode.
- The GUI button formerly labeled Append is now labeled Re-inventory; command-line/non-GUI prompts accept skip, overwrite, or re-inventory. Legacy append remains accepted as an alias.
- Ctrl-C cleanup only deletes newly allocated log tokens, not pre-existing log tokens reused for overwrite/re-inventory.
- Regression tests now cover existing-token reuse and append/overwrite logger modes.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v199.txt =====
v199 - Tag track numbering and unnumbered setlist fallback

- Tagging now writes zero-padded track numbers: 01, 02, ... for folders with up to 99 tracks, and 001, 002, ... for folders with more than 99 tracks.
- Setlist track parsing now accepts track lists that begin at 0 as well as 1, including related forms such as 0 Song, 0-Song, 0) Song, Track 0 - Song, d1t00 Song, and 0Song.
- When a setlist has no numbered tracks, the tagger can derive titles from comma-separated long lines when the item count matches the music-file count.
- If the number of long comma-separated lines matches the music-file count, each line is used as one track title in order.
- Tag logs and summaries now note comma-separated fallback actions and list affected folders succinctly.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v200.txt =====
v200 Search-path OS volume label fallback
----------------------------------------
- If a search-path entry does not include a bracketed visible volume prefix, TLO resolves the operating-system volume label for the physical search root and uses that label for log writing and existing-volume comparison.
- Explicit bracketed labels still win, including [] as an explicit blank visible volume.
- Log headers and top SEARCH_PATH lines continue to use [Volume] Path format; unprefixed paths now write the OS label when one is available, otherwise [].
- Existing unbracketed groups*.log SEARCH_PATH/header values are interpreted with the OS volume label when possible so comparison remains consistent with new unprefixed entries.
- Added regression tests for OS-label fallback, explicit blank prefixes, and unbracketed group-log matching.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v201.txt =====
v201 Explicit volume-label mismatch guard
-----------------------------------------
- Explicit bracketed search-path volume labels are validated against the operating-system volume label for the mounted search root when the OS label can be resolved.
- If a supplied bracketed label does not match the mounted drive label, TLO raises an alert and does not process that path.
- Example: [www]E: is rejected when drive E reports Backup-1.
- Unprefixed paths continue to use the OS volume label automatically for log writing and existing-volume comparison.
- Explicit [] is allowed only when the mounted drive label is blank or cannot be resolved; if the OS reports a nonblank label, [] is rejected as a mismatch.
- Added regression tests for matching explicit labels, mismatched labels, and explicit blank mismatch handling.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v205.txt =====
TLO Inventory v205

Changes:
- Added eTreeDB setlist song-title fallback for tagging.
- When eTreeDB lookup is enabled during standalone Tag or Tag During Inventory, and no usable local setlist titles are available, TLO queries eTreeDB by artist and yyyy-mm-dd date for set1/set2/set3 song titles.
- eTreeDB setlists are normalized using the supplied etree_v6 approach: exact artist/year lookup through artistsRoot, nested performances, exact local date matching, set1/set2/set3 order, &gt cleanup, encore-marker removal, comma-list splitting, and continuous numbering.
- eTreeDB titles are used only when the returned title count matches the taggable music-file count; otherwise existing fallback behavior continues.

Validation:

- Help checks for tlo-gi.py, tlo-ggi.py, tlo-tag.py, and setlistFM.py


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v206.txt =====
v206 - Size-aware setlist alternate handling
-------------------------------------------
- Generated setlist filename collisions now compare file sizes only. Existing TLOHome/setlists files are not opened for content comparison.
- If the existing file size equals the size the new exported setlist would write, TLO assumes the files are identical, does not write a new setlist file, and resolves the row to the existing filename.
- If the file sizes differ, TLO writes the new setlist as an alternate with an (altN) suffix before .txt, such as Artist2001-04-14Venue(alt1).txt.
- New generated setlist collisions no longer use plain trailing-integer suffixes or (dupN) suffixes.
- Updated requirements, user manual, README, and regression tests.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v207.txt =====
v207 - Current-run postprocess and path-scoped overwrite

- Fixed postprocess aggregation so it reads only the current inventory run log token(s) when available instead of every historical meta*.log file under TLOHome/logs.
- Fixed path-scoped overwrite/re-inventory behavior so a run for a child path such as [Back-up-1] /mnt/e/MP3 does not rebuild/export rows for unrelated folders on the same volume.
- Existing bootlist rows are now removed only when their VolumePath is under the overwritten search-path subtree.
- If a child path overlaps a broader prior group log, TLO uses a fresh log token and performs output replacement by path rather than truncating the broader log. Exact-path or fully contained related logs may still be reused safely.
- Postprocess status now reports when records were read from current log tokens.
- Added regression coverage for current-token postprocess and path-scoped overwrite.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v208.txt =====
v208 - Skip/Re-inventory collision handling and faster postprocess
-----------------------------------------------------------------
- Removes the user-facing Overwrite choice from existing-inventory collisions; prompts now offer only Skip or Re-inventory.
- For one colliding search path, the GUI shows a simple Skip/Re-inventory alert. For multiple colliding paths, it shows a multi-row decision window.
- Exact-path Re-inventory replaces the prior logs/output for that path. Child-path Re-inventory prunes prior child-subtree log entries from the related parent token and appends the new child results to that same token.
- Postprocess prefers the just-completed in-memory metadata records instead of re-reading reused historical meta logs, preventing child reruns from aggregating unrelated old records.
- Setlist export now avoids repeated full setlists-directory scans and missing-setlist placeholder generation no longer recursively walks broad roots.
- Requirements DOCX, user manual, README, and regression tests updated.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v212.txt =====
v212 - Compliant date-first parsing, compliant artist mode, and Quit cleanup alert

- Added compliant artist handling mode:
  - Master checks String1 against the Artist DB and uses the DB master name when matched.
  - As-Is uses String1 directly as the artist with no Artist DB lookup.
  - CLI accepts --compliant-artist-mode master|as-is; interactive compliant CLI runs prompt when the option is omitted.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v213.txt =====
v213

- Fixed a GUI startup regression where pressing Inventory could appear to do nothing while inventory-root preparation ran on the Tk main thread.
- The GUI now marks full inventory active, disables the mutually exclusive buttons, clears the output window, and starts the worker before root preparation and existing-inventory checks run.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v214.txt =====
