Later stages rescan the known music directory only when exact filenames or tag samples are needed.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v215.txt =====
v215: Phase 1 now logs only one representative full media-file path per discovered music/video folder. It no longer records directory rows, txt/rtf/Word setlist candidates, compact music-directory markers, media-file counts, or every media filename during the tree walk. Later phases derive the music directory and media type from that sample path, rescan the known media folder only when exact media filenames or tag samples are needed, and scan the music folder plus allowed immediate parent for setlist files at selection time.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v216.txt =====
v216: Centralizes inventory/tagger command-line and GUI option definitions in tlo_options.py. CLI parsers, GUI checkboxes, defaults, canonical argparse destination names, and the setlist.fm/eTreeDB dependency now share one registry/function. TLOHome/myTLO precedence is preserved through a shared resolver, --max-workers supplied detection uses argparse.SUPPRESS instead of raw argv scanning, GUI and CLI performance-mode defaults now both start at balanced, and --debug [BOOL] is kept as the single documented compatibility exception.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v217.txt =====
v217: Caps postprocess ThreadPoolExecutor workers so large filename-group counts cannot create one thread per filename group. Extreme mode still queues every filename group as work, but the executor worker count is bounded by Max Workers when supplied, otherwise by an I/O-oriented safety cap. This prevents large runs such as 23,977 filename groups from failing with "can't start new thread".

Existing --TLOHome, --myTLO, --debug, --silent, --compliant, and -$slam behavior is unchanged. Adds regression coverage that the removed camelCase flag spellings are rejected.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v224.txt =====

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v225.txt =====
v225 - Setlist prose parsing fix for inventory-time tagging
- Fixed a tag-title regression where collector notes or prose after a valid setlist could be misparsed as an extra numbered track, causing a false track-count mismatch.

Build 235 changes

- Setlist discovery also checks sibling information folders such as info, setlist, set list, data, notes, docs, and text when they are inside such a compact parent.
- Inventory and tagging share the same enhanced setlist discovery because both use the common setlist-selection helper.
- Filename-derived title fallback now rejects fill-in identifiers such as Track, Track 01, d1t03, or s1t04 when no real song name appears after the track token.
- Filename-derived title fallback accepts embedded date/disc/track prefixes when a real title follows and, when a setlist exists, still requires confirmation from the setlist text.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v236.txt =====
Build 236 changes

- Setlist parsing now recognizes set/disc track-token rows such as s2t01 - Song, cd1t01 Song, d01t01: Song, Disc 1 Track 04 Song, and Set 2 track 05 Song.
- These tokenized rows are handled in the same primary parser used for normal numbered setlist rows.
- After a song list has started, a line beginning with Patch, case-insensitive, stops further setlist parsing, like checksum/hash sections.
- Filename-derived tagging fallback now also recognizes cd/disc/disk track-token prefixes when a real title follows.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v237.txt =====
Build 237 changes

- Tag writing now clears stale TRACKTOTAL, DISCNUMBER, and DISCTOTAL metadata when writing Artist, Album, TrackNo, and Title.
- FLAC/Vorbis/Easy tags remove those keys case-insensitively.
- ID3 fallback removes TPOS and matching TXXX total/disc frames.
- MP4 fallback clears disc metadata and preserves track number without writing a track-total value.
- Added regression tests for Easy/Vorbis, ID3, and MP4 clearing behavior.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v238.txt =====
Build 238

- Added --convert-shn to the inventory CLI, GUI launcher, and standalone tagger CLI.
- Added a main-window checkbox labeled "Convert shn".
- When Convert shn is enabled, Tag During Inventory and standalone/GUI tagging convert .shn/.shnf files to .flac before title selection and tagging.
- Successful SHN conversions delete the original SHN/SHNF file only after a non-empty FLAC is produced.
- Failed SHN conversions are logged as tag errors and left in place.
- PyInstaller/native app deployments may provide ffmpeg next to the executable or through PATH; TLO also honors TLO_FFMPEG/TLO_SHN_CONVERTER when set.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v239.txt =====
v239 changes

- If the bundled converter is unavailable, conversion fails with a clear tag-log error and leaves the original SHN/SHNF file in place.
- Conversion log lines identify the app-bundled converter path.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v263.txt =====
TLO Inventory Build v263

- Added --tag-copy-and-delete DIR.
- Added the main GUI text box labeled "Tag Copy and Delete Path".
- The path must be an existing fully qualified destination directory.
- During full inventory, when Tag Copy and Delete Path is filled in, TLO transfers the original show folder after metadata capture while retaining the original path in inventory/bootlist output.
- If source and destination are on the same filesystem/partition, TLO moves/renames the folder into the destination.
- If source and destination are on different filesystems/partitions, TLO copies the folder, verifies relative file paths and file sizes, and deletes the original only after verification succeeds.
- The moved/copied destination is not added to the inventory.
- If inventory-time tagging is enabled, tagging operates on the transferred folder while the inventory record remains anchored to the original path.
- Added regression coverage for option validation, same-partition move behavior, and cross-partition copy/verify/delete behavior.

============

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v281.txt =====
TLO-GI v281 changes
====================

- Added per-search-path directives for toBeInventoried.txt:
  - --$slam ARTIST_NAME (and legacy -$slam remains accepted)
  - --$copy DESTINATION
  - --$copy-delete DESTINATION
- Directive order after the path does not matter. --$copy and --$copy-delete are mutually exclusive on a single line.
- Added matching single --search-path command-line options:
  - --$slam / -$slam
  - --$copy
  - --$copy-delete
- Added startup destination-capacity checks for per-path copy/copy-delete destinations, single --search-path copy/copy-delete destinations, Tag Copy destinations, and global Tag Copy and Delete Path overrides.
- A filled global Tag Copy and Delete Path overrides per-path destinations and is checked against all accessible queued inventory roots when no Search Path override is supplied.
- Capacity failures identify the destination, related source path(s), required space, available space, and additional space needed.
- Copy/Delete checks count zero additional space when the source and destination are on the same filesystem because the operation is performed as a move.

TLO Inventory v287
==================

- Improved tag-title extraction for unnumbered setlists whose song list begins after a show-qualified disc/set heading such as "Disc One/Late Show:".
- Bare Encore/Encore: separator lines are skipped as section markers and are not counted as song titles when matching the setlist title count to the audio-file count.
- The actual encore song title after the separator remains eligible for tagging when the final count matches the audio files.
- Updated requirements and user manual for the setlist title parsing change.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v288.txt =====
CHANGES v288
============

- Fixed tag-title extraction for setlists that number songs as disc-track dash rows, such as "1-1 Back Seat Betty" and "1-4 Jean Pierre [nc] 12:20 (end cut)".
- The first number is treated as the disc/set and the second as the track, using the existing CD/set-prefixed sequence logic internally.
- Trailing duration-plus-condition notes such as "12:20 (end cut)" are stripped from the written song title.
- Revision headings and repair notes after a valid numbered list are no longer selected as unnumbered fallback titles for this case.
- Added regression tests for the Miles Davis-style disc-track dash format and for disc changes without an intervening heading.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v289.txt =====
CHANGES v289

- Broadened high-confidence tag-title parsing for debug-review cases without loosening unsafe prose fallback behavior.
- Added support for numbered Disc/Set sections that restart at track 1 while writing sequential Track Number tags.
- Added support for side-letter track rows such as A01 and B05.
- Confirmed/expanded literal prefix support such as t01:, d1t01, cd1t01, and 01 - Title.
- Added SET ONE / SET TWO comma-list parsing where short comma lists inside explicit set sections can be combined safely, while internal medley arrows such as Jam>Flute Down remain part of one title.
- Inventory-time tagging can now keep a strong short numbered setlist and write Unknown only for unmatched extra trailing audio files.
- Normalized remaining per-file tag errors, including SHN non-taggable errors, to the full-path form: ERROR: '/full/path/file.ext' - message.
- Updated requirements and user manual to Build 289.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v290.txt =====
CHANGES v290

- Split tag logging into success and error files.
- Inventory-time tagging now writes status/success lines to tagsN.txt and errors/warnings/skips to tageN.txt, using the same N token as the other inventory logs.
- Standalone/GUI Tagger output now writes status/success lines to tagsT.txt and errors/warnings/skips to tageT.txt.
- Completion lines with file_errors greater than zero are routed to the error tag log.
- Tag debug files written under TLOHome/debug are now named after the generated inventory setlist filename for the show metadata, not the original info.txt/setlist filename.
- Audited SHN conversion/tagging error output so conversion failures and tag-file errors use the concise full-path error format.
- Updated command-line/help text, requirements, and user manual to Build 290.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v291.txt =====
CHANGES v291
============

- Improved numbered setlist title parsing for Set I/Set II duration headings.
  Lines such as "Set I - 89 min." and "Set II - 99 min." are now treated as
  clear set boundaries, so numbered setlists may restart at 01 after the next
  set heading while the written Track Number tags remain sequential.
- Strips embedded encore markers from numbered title rows. For example,
  "09. E: Midnight Hour" and "09. Encore: Midnight Hour" now write the title
  as "Midnight Hour".
- Added regression coverage for the Dead and Company 2016-07-13 style setlist
  with two numbered sets and a numbered encore row.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v292.txt =====
CHANGES v292
============

- Improved numbered setlist title parsing for files that omit explicit Set, Disc,
  CD, or Disk headings. If an established numbered list ascends from 0 or 1 and
  then restarts at 1, TLO now treats that as a new section when the next parsed
  row confirms the restart by continuing to 2.
- Blank lines are not required for the implicit restart. Both "1, 2, 3, 1, 2"
  and "1, 2, 3, blank, 1, 2" style lists are accepted.
- A lone unconfirmed second "1" is still ignored so revision notes or other
  non-song lines are not accidentally written as song-title tags.
- Added regression coverage for implicit set/disc resets with and without blank
  separators and for an unconfirmed reset that must not be added as a title.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v293.txt =====
TLO Inventory v293

- Restored the intended complete-path logging behavior defensively: compN.log is compacted after Phase 1 so only one representative media-file row is retained per music directory.
- Append-mode and reused complete-path logs are compacted as well, so legacy runs that listed every media file in a folder do not continue to feed duplicate rows forward.
- Full audio-file enumeration remains in later stages only. Grouping/tagging rescan the known music directory when counts or full file lists are needed.
- Updated requirements and user manual.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v294.txt =====
Build v294 changes

- Numbered setlists now continue across blank lines and visual divider/break lines when the following numbered row continues the established sequence, including encore songs after a separator.
- Added a conservative repair for one skipped-number typo followed by a duplicate number in an otherwise established numbered setlist. Example: 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 9, 9 preserves both title rows by treating the first 9 as the missing track 8.
- Common HTML entities in setlist title rows, such as &gt;, are decoded before titles are written to tags.
- Added regression tests for encore divider lines and the Dead and Company 2016-07-15-style skipped-number duplicate pattern.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v295.txt =====
Build v295 changes

- Numbered setlist title parsing now tolerates large non-song gaps between valid numbered song rows. After a numbered list has started, TLO can skip notes, URLs, personnel details, comments, and blank lines and continue when the next numbered row continues the expected sequence or begins a confirmed reset.
- Terminator-style headings such as Note:, Source:, Transfer:, and Comments: no longer end an otherwise valid numbered list when a later numbered row continues correctly.
- Patch notes and checksum/hash sections remain hard stops so post-set patch prose cannot become song titles.
- Added regression tests for a Rare Miles-style setlist where tracks 1-12 and 13-16 are separated by explanatory notes and performance metadata.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v296.txt =====
CHANGES v296

- Added a compact problem summary by reason code to the tag error log when reason-coded tag problems occur.
- Stopped creating TLOHome/debug files for bad audio-file cases. Invalid FLAC files, per-file read/write failures, SHN conversion/file errors, and non-taggable audio sources now log only to tageN.txt/tageT.txt using the full-path ERROR_AUDIO_FILE format.
- Kept debug files for setlist/title diagnostics such as title-count mismatches, title-parse failures, and generated Unknown title evidence.

Issues are classified where possible as unidentified shows, missing setlists, track-title mismatches, unreadable audio, copy/verification problems, online lookup problems, tagging warnings, or file/operation errors. - Added pause checks between standalone/GUI tagger groups so the Tagger Pause/Resume control can stop safely between folders.
- Updated GUI help, requirements, user manual, FAQ, release metadata, and regression tests.
- All 450 source regression tests pass.

Behavior preserved:
- Standalone and GUI Tag always tag the selected path directly and do not copy or move folders.
- Verified SHN-to-FLAC conversion remains the only standalone Tag case that deletes a source file, and deletion occurs only after successful replacement verification.
- Inventory, Add Shows folder routing, copy/delete, tagging, metadata parsing, lookup, logging, and output behavior remain unchanged beneath the new user-interface layer. Add Shows now additionally returns path-specific error details to its completion window.

Build 339 main-window copy/delete and artist-name controls:

- The destination window warns that the original material will be removed.
- The destination must be an existing fully qualified directory, and TLO must be able to read its available storage before OK is enabled.
- The existing exact source-size destination-capacity preflight remains in place before Inventory changes content.
- Cancelling or closing the destination window clears the Tag Copy/Delete Original checkbox.

Artist-name behavior:
- Added an As-Is Artist Name checkbox to the main Inventory window and the tagger-scoped controls.
- The checkbox defaults to unchecked.
- When unchecked, a discovered artist or alias that matches one Artist DB entry uses the database master artist name.
- When checked, TLO preserves the artist spelling and wording found by the metadata source that identified the show.
- The setting applies to compliant and non-compliant metadata collection, including path patterns, audio tags, selected setlist metadata, Add Shows, standalone Tag, and multiprocessing workers.
- The Artist DB is still used to identify aliases and resolve artists when As-Is Artist Name is checked.
- Removed Master-versus-As-Is GUI windows and interactive command-line prompts.
- The hidden legacy --compliant-artist-mode argument remains accepted for compatibility, while --as-is-artist-name is the documented control.

Behavior preserved:
- Cross-partition copy/delete still verifies copied file sizes before removing originals.
- Same-partition copy/delete may use a move operation as before.
- Unidentified shows remain untouched by copy/delete, rename, and tagging operations.
- Tag Copy/Delete Original remains a Full Inventory workflow and is ignored by Add Shows and standalone Tag.
- Standalone Tag still performs no copying or folder deletion; verified SHN-to-FLAC conversion remains the only source-file deletion case.

Validation:
- All 459 source regression tests pass.
- All top-level Python source files compile.
- GUI construction verifies that the old path widgets are absent and the new three-row checkbox layout is present.
- GUI and standalone Tag help output checks pass; --myTLO remains hidden.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v361.txt =====
TLO v361 / v1.3 Build 361

Commercial-release parsing:
- Recognizes non-compliant commercial-release folders only when the leading release-year component is exactly a four-digit 19xx or 20xx year, either parenthesized or bare, followed by Artist - Album.
- Examples: (1991) - Rod Stewart - Vagabond Heart and 1995 - Rod Stewart - A Spanner In The Works.
- The recognized release year is classification evidence only. It is not assigned to DATE and is not included in the commercial-release SHOW_NAME.
- Full or partial dates, year ranges, two-digit years, x-filled years, and years embedded in other text are not treated as commercial-release year prefixes.
- Removes one exact redundant resolved-artist prefix from a non-compliant album tail before building Artist - Album. Example: Rod Stewart - Rod Stewart - Camouflage becomes Rod Stewart - Camouflage.
- Commercial-release Artist may be resolved from the middle Artist component even when audio tags are blank.

Copy and move completeness:
- Tag Copy and Copy/Delete Original operate on the complete identified folder tree rooted at MAIN_DIR_PATH, not only inventoried music files or selected setlists.
- Same-partition Copy/Delete Original continues to use a directory rename/move, which carries the entire tree including nested non-music files and empty subdirectories.
- Cross-partition Copy/Delete Original and Tag Copy recursively copy the entire tree.
- Real-copy verification now checks descendant directory structure in addition to every relative file path and file size, so empty folders and nested non-music folders are part of verification.

Documentation and tests:
- Updates the requirements and user manual with the commercial-release year restrictions, duplicate-artist removal, and complete-folder transfer rules.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v362.txt =====
TLO v362 / v1.3 Build 362

Copy versus alternate classification:
- A folder/show collision is no longer labeled as a copy merely because the intended compliant name already exists.
- TLO first compares the incoming/source folder against the existing same-base folder family recursively.
- `(copyN)` is used only when another same-base tree has exactly the same descendant folder structure, relative file set, file sizes, and byte-for-byte file contents.
- File contents are verified with SHA-256 after the faster structure/path/size checks pass. File timestamps and other filesystem metadata do not determine copy identity.
- If no exact same-base tree exists, the collision is named `(altN)` instead.
- Any unreadable or otherwise unverifiable comparison is treated as non-identical and therefore cannot be labeled as a copy.
- An already-existing source folder ending in `(copyN)` is revalidated during metadata extraction. Its Show qualifier remains `(copyN)` only when another same-base sibling tree is exact; otherwise the Show qualifier is exposed as `(altN)`.

Transfer behavior preserved:
- Tag Copy and Copy/Delete Original still transfer the entire identified folder tree rooted at MAIN_DIR_PATH, including all nested files and empty directories.
- Same-partition Copy/Delete Original remains a direct directory rename/move without size verification.
- Real copies continue to verify complete relative file/folder structure and file sizes before processing continues or an original is removed.

Documentation and tests:
- Requirements and user manual now define exact recursive content equality as the only basis for copy labeling and define alt labeling for non-identical collisions.
- Adds behavioral tests for exact copies, same-size/different-byte files, extra/missing files, empty-folder differences, existing copy-suffix revalidation, Tag Copy naming, and Rename Compliantly naming.

Versioning:
- Advances the application bundle to v1.3 Build 362.
- Archives CHANGES_v362.txt in old-change-logs.zip.

Validation:
- Full compatibility suite under a virtual display: 579 passed.
- Without a graphical display: 577 passed, 2 GUI tests explicitly skipped.
- Python compilation, requirements DOCX rendering, and user-manual rendering passed.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v363.txt =====
TLO v363 / v1.3 Build 363

New duplicate-folder cleanup main:
- Adds tlo-deleteDupes.py as a console utility.
- Requires one fully qualified Input Path and uses shared TLOHome/--myTLO/environment resolution.
- Searches recursively for copy-suffixed sibling folders in both (copyN) and (copy N) forms, case-insensitively.
- A candidate is removed only when the unsuffixed original sibling exists and the two trees have the same relative directory structure, relative file names, and file sizes. Empty directories count as structure.
- This cleanup rule intentionally uses names/structure/sizes rather than byte hashes; timestamps and ordinary filesystem metadata are ignored.
- Symbolic links are not followed and must have the same relative path and target to compare equal.
- The unsuffixed original directory is never sent to Trash.
- Matching copy folders are moved through send2trash to the platform Recycle Bin/Trash rather than permanently deleted.
- After each successful Trash move, the full pre-deletion copy-folder path is appended to TLOHome/deletedDirs.txt and flushed.
- Ctrl-C stops further processing, closes the log during unwinding, and exits with status 130.

Distribution/build integration:
- Adds tlo-deleteDupes to Windows onefile, shared Windows onedir, generic Linux, and macOS builds.
- Adds send2trash to the generated build requirements and runner import checks.
- GitHub Build Process v056 validates the new executable in complete/update ZIPs and shared Windows onedir output.

Documentation and tests:
- Requirements add Section 18 defining the utility, comparison rule, Trash behavior, logging, Ctrl-C behavior, and packaging dependency.
- User Manual adds Section 20 and application-layout/command examples.
- FAQ documents safe duplicate cleanup.
- Adds behavior tests for suffix parsing, recursive equality, nested/empty-folder differences, original preservation, recursive discovery, path validation, TLOHome precedence, logging, and Ctrl-C status.

Versioning:
- Advances the application bundle to v1.3 Build 363.
- Archives CHANGES_v362.txt in old-change-logs.zip.

Validation:
- 595 tests passed under xvfb-run.
- 593 tests passed and 2 GUI tests skipped without a graphical display.
- The Build Process compatibility entry point passed all 595 tests under xvfb-run.
- Python compileall completed successfully.
- Requirements DOCX and User Manual RTF rendered cleanly after final edits.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v364.txt =====
TLO v1.3 Build 364

tlo-deleteDupes FLAC integrity repair
- Before any structurally qualifying copy folder is sent to Recycle Bin/Trash, recursively full-decode every .flac in the unsuffixed original folder that will be kept.
- FLAC validation uses the bundled imageio-ffmpeg ffmpeg executable with decoder errors treated as corruption.
- A corrupt original FLAC is matched by relative path against qualifying copy folders in numeric copy order: copy1, copy2, copy3, and so on.
- The first healthy counterpart is copied to a temporary file beside the original, atomically installed, and decode-validated again after replacement.
- If all counterparts are corrupt/missing or replacement fails, report the unrepaired file and continue. The otherwise qualifying duplicate copies are still trashed, as requested.
- Structurally non-matching copy-suffixed folders are not repair sources and are not trashed; Build 363's directory-structure/name/size duplicate safety rule remains unchanged.
- The unsuffixed original folder is never trashed. deletedDirs.txt continues to record only successfully trashed duplicate-folder paths.
- Ctrl-C behavior remains unchanged: stop work, unwind/close the log, and return status 130.

Packaging
- tlo-deleteDupes now collects both send2trash and imageio_ffmpeg on Windows, Linux, and macOS.
- GitHub Build Process v057 extends the shared Windows onedir tlo-deleteDupes analysis with imageio-ffmpeg data/binaries as well as send2trash.

Version
- Public version remains v1.3.
- Bundle/build advances to Build 364.

Validation
- Build Process compatibility command: 600 passed under xvfb-run.
- Normal headless pytest discovery: 598 passed, 2 GUI tests skipped for no graphical display.
- Full-stream validator smoke check: generated valid FLAC accepted; truncated FLAC rejected.
- Requirements DOCX and User Manual RTF rendered and visually reviewed with no clipping or layout defects.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v365.txt =====
TLO v1.3 Build 365

FAQ
- Adds: "Does TLO inventory absolutely every music file?" The FAQ explains that once TLO identifies a music file in a folder, traversal stops below that music-bearing folder; nested music folders are therefore ignored, a pattern often seen when an opening act is nested beneath a main-show folder.

tlo-deleteDupes duplicate discovery
- Retains the existing exact-base-name rule for folders ending in (copyN) or (copy N).
- A copy-suffixed folder may now also match a different-named, same-parent kept folder when the normalized artist and date are the same.
- Exact unsuffixed base-name matches are preferred when present; otherwise same-artist/date sibling candidates are checked deterministically.
- Artist/date matching recognizes normal TLO-style yyyy-mm-dd dates (including xx month/day components), four-digit year ranges, and standalone four-digit 19xx/20xx years. Unknown-date values are not used to broaden duplicate discovery.
- Name similarity alone never deletes a copy. The existing full recursive duplicate rule still requires the same relative folder structure, relative file names, file sizes, and symlink targets before a copy qualifies.
- Only copy-suffixed folders are deletion targets. The selected kept/original sibling is never sent to Trash.

tlo-deleteDupes Trash behavior
- Makes the existing folder-level behavior an explicit safety contract: one send2trash call is made on the duplicate directory itself, not on its child files, so the duplicate is recoverable from Recycle Bin/Trash as a folder tree.
- FLAC validation/repair from Build 364 remains in effect before qualifying duplicate folders are trashed, including for same-artist/date matches with different folder names.

Version
- Public version remains v1.3.
- Bundle/build advances to Build 365.
- GitHub Build Process v057 remains appropriate; no dependency or packaging change is required.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v366.txt =====
TLO v1.3 Build 366

tlo-deleteDupes unsuffixed duplicate discovery
- Extends duplicate cleanup to same-parent folders that do not have a (copyN)/(copy N) suffix.
- When two or more unsuffixed sibling folder names resolve to the same normalized artist and date, tlo-deleteDupes sorts those folder names alphabetically without regard to case.
- The alphabetically first folder is the master and is always retained.
- Every later unsuffixed folder is treated as a copy candidate and is eligible for Trash only when it passes the existing complete recursive comparison against the master: same descendant directory structure (including empty directories), same relative file names, same file sizes, and matching symlink names/targets.
- A later folder that differs from the first master is retained even if it happens to match another later folder. The first alphabetical folder remains the only master for that unsuffixed artist/date set.
- Exact-name and same-artist/date discovery for explicit (copyN)/(copy N) folders remains supported. If an explicit copy points to an unsuffixed folder that itself qualifies as a duplicate of the alphabetical master, the explicit copy is associated with the surviving master so an intermediate folder is not required to remain.

tlo-deleteDupes FLAC repair and Trash behavior
- FLAC validation and repair continue to run on the folder that will be kept before any qualifying duplicates are trashed.
- Explicit copy-suffixed repair sources retain numeric copy order. Qualifying unsuffixed repair sources are considered in alphabetical order after the master.
- Failure to repair a corrupt FLAC still does not retain otherwise qualifying duplicate folders.
- Every qualifying duplicate is still sent to Recycle Bin/Trash as one directory-level object and logged by full pre-trash path in TLOHome/deletedDirs.txt.

Documentation
- Updates the duplicate-cleanup FAQ, User Manual, and Requirements Section 18 to describe alphabetical master selection for unsuffixed same-artist/date folders.

Version
- Public version remains v1.3.
- Bundle/build advances to Build 366.
- GitHub Build Process v057 remains appropriate; no dependency or packaging change is required.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v367.txt =====
TLO v1.3 Build 367

tlo-deleteDupes partition-root duplicates holding folder
- Replaces Recycle Bin/Trash disposal with a same-partition holding folder named duplicates at the root of the filesystem/partition containing the Input Path.
- Creates the duplicates holding folder automatically when it does not already exist.
- Moves each qualifying duplicate as one complete directory tree; child files and nested folders are not moved individually.
- On Windows, a search on E: uses E:\duplicates. On Linux/macOS, the holding folder is created at the mounted-filesystem root containing the search path, such as /mnt/music/duplicates or /Volumes/Music/duplicates.
- If the search path includes the partition root, the duplicates holding folder is excluded from duplicate discovery so previously moved material is not processed again.
- Never overwrites an existing folder in duplicates. A destination-name collision is preserved with a suffix such as (moved 2), (moved 3), and so on.
- TLOHome/deletedDirs.txt is retained for compatibility and records each qualifying duplicate folder's full pre-move source path after a successful move.
- Ctrl-C still stops further work and closes deletedDirs.txt; folders already moved into the duplicates holding folder remain there.

tlo-deleteDupes duplicate identification and FLAC repair
- Existing Build 366 alphabetical-master, explicit (copyN)/(copy N), same-artist/date, recursive structure/name/size comparison, and FLAC repair rules remain unchanged.
- FLAC validation/repair completes before any qualifying duplicate folder is moved to the partition-root duplicates holding folder.
- Failure to repair a corrupt master FLAC still does not retain otherwise qualifying duplicates.

Packaging
- tlo-deleteDupes no longer imports or packages send2trash.
- imageio-ffmpeg remains bundled so full FLAC decode validation is self-contained.
- GitHub Build Process advances to v058 to remove the obsolete send2trash build dependency and update deleteDupes packaging text/specification.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v368.txt =====

tlo-deleteDupes simple mismatch logging
- Adds TLOHome/deleteDupesMismatches.txt as a separate persistent log for same-artist/date candidate comparisons that do not qualify as duplicates.
- Each mismatch record is simple CSV with exactly three fields: folder name, folder name, simple reason. Full paths are not written to the mismatch log.
- Folder names that contain commas are CSV-quoted automatically.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v370.txt =====
TLO v1.3 Build 370 changes

Duplicate equivalence clustering
- tlo-deleteDupes now compares candidate copies with one another in addition to comparing them with unsuffixed folders.
- Exact X/(copyN) families are discovery components even when the unsuffixed X folder is absent or has different contents.
- Same-parent folders with the same normalized artist/date remain candidate siblings even when the rest of their names differ.
- Candidate discovery is separated from duplicate proof: recursive directory structure, relative file names, file sizes, and symlink names/targets still determine content equivalence.
- Identical trees form content-equivalence clusters. Each cluster keeps one representative and moves the remaining identical folders to the partition-root duplicates folder.
- Keeper preference is: an unsuffixed folder when available; alphabetically first among multiple identical unsuffixed folders; otherwise the lowest-numbered copy, with alphabetical order as a final tie-breaker.
- Different content clusters remain in place as independent variants. For X, X (copy2), and X (copy3), if X differs while copy2 and copy3 are identical, X and copy2 remain and copy3 is moved.

FLAC repair and mismatch logging
- FLAC integrity checking and repair are applied independently to each content-equivalence cluster before duplicate members of that cluster are moved.
- Repair sources come only from other identical members of the same cluster.
- deleteDupesMismatches.txt now reflects copy-to-copy comparisons as well as copy-to-unsuffixed and unsuffixed-to-unsuffixed comparisons, while retaining the existing concise reasons.

Tests and documentation
- Adds regression coverage for copy2/copy3 equivalence when the unsuffixed X differs, copy-only families with no X, and unsuffixed keeper preference within a matching cluster.
- Updates Requirements, User Manual, FAQ, source README, and duplicate-cleanup contracts for content-equivalence clustering.
- GitHub Build Process v066 remains the recommended release process; no packaging dependency changed in Build 370.

Preserved behavior
- Build 369 Research CLI/GUI behavior and packaging remain unchanged.
- Duplicate folders are still moved as complete folder trees into the partition-root duplicates folder.
- Duplicate proof still compares names/structure/file sizes rather than file bytes, and FLAC validation/repair still uses full decode checks.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v375.txt =====
TLO v1.4 Build 375 changes

Venue/location correction
- Path String2 parsing now removes the specific festival/archive numbering pattern in which a numeric-only putative venue is followed by a numbered city token, such as "04 05.Reidsville NC". That example becomes City=Reidsville, Region=NC with no numeric venue; it no longer becomes Venue=04 / City=05.Reidsville.
- Selected-setlist venue/location metadata may now correct a conflicting existing venue, city, region, or country only when the existing value is supported solely by lower-confidence path_part evidence and the selected-setlist confidence is strictly higher.
- After a selected-setlist correction, LOCATION is rebuilt from the final City + Region/Country so stale path-derived location text cannot remain.
- Stronger evidence is protected: selected-setlist metadata does not replace eTreeDB (95-confidence), other non-path evidence, or equally/strongly supported path metadata.
- Artist handling at the selected-setlist venue/location stage remains fill-only; this change is limited to venue/location correction.

Regression coverage
- Added the Country Gentlemen / Camp Springs Bluegrass Festival 1971-09-03 case. The path fragment "Camp Springs Bluegrass Festival.1971-09-03 04 05.Reidsville NC" no longer produces Venue=04 / City=05.Reidsville, and the selected setlist can establish Venue=Camp Springs Bluegrass Festival / Location=Reidsville, NC.
- Added a guard proving a 95-confidence eTreeDB venue/location is not overwritten by a 92-confidence selected-setlist result.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v378.txt =====
TLO v1.4 Build 378 changes

Artist abbreviation precedence
- Non-compliant artist identification now treats short no-space-date artist prefixes such as gd in gd1977-05-08... as weak abbreviation evidence.
- These short prefixes are deferred while TLO checks stronger local evidence, including a full DB-backed artist elsewhere in the path, selected-setlist metadata/filename evidence, usable audio artist tags, and other structured artist fallbacks.
- The path component containing the abbreviation is excluded from the normal subdirectory artist scan so the same short prefix cannot be rediscovered and promoted early.
- If stronger evidence identifies the artist, the short abbreviation is not used.
- If no stronger artist evidence is found, the abbreviation may resolve through artists.sqlite only as the final local artist fallback.
- Last-resort abbreviation evidence is logged separately at lower confidence.
- When eTreeDB or setlist.fm is enabled and an exact performance date is available, TLO attempts the enabled artist/date lookup after selecting the last-resort abbreviation; a successful lookup is logged as additional confirmation.
- If no enabled online lookup confirms it, the metadata log explicitly records that the abbreviation remained unconfirmed.
- Music filenames continue to be usable as a date fallback; a short artist-style filename prefix is not promoted ahead of stronger artist evidence.

Regression coverage
- Added focused tests proving that a full artist name elsewhere in the path beats the abbreviation.
- Added a test proving explicit selected-setlist artist metadata beats the abbreviation.
- Added a test proving a usable deferred raw artist tag beats the abbreviation.
- Added a test proving the abbreviation still works as the final fallback when no stronger artist evidence exists.

Versioning
- Public application version remains v1.4.
- Bundle/build number advances from 377 to 378.
- GitHub Build Process v067 remains compatible; no build-process change is required.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v379.txt =====
TLO v1.4 Build 379 changes

Track-title validation and EAC filtering
- Exact Audio Copy (EAC) extraction reports are now treated as technical content rather than possible song-title sources.
- Recognized EAC markers including Exact Audio Copy, EAC extraction logfile, and TOC of the extracted CD begin a technical tail that is not mined for track titles.
- EAC TOC rows such as "1 | 0:00.00 | 13:17.25 | 0 | 59799" are rejected even when their leading row numbers form a perfect 1..N sequence.
- Numbered track-title parsing now applies a conservative plausibility check for numeric multi-column timestamp/sector rows, while preserving legitimate numeric song titles.
- Explicit local Songs:/Tracks: unnumbered blocks are retained as real song-list evidence even when their title count does not equal the audio-file count.
- A mismatched local song list is reported, but TLO does not truncate, shift, or force those titles onto a different number of audio files.
- A valid numbered song list still retains normal precedence after passing the technical-data and title-plausibility filters.

Foghat/EAC regression
- Added a focused regression based on the reported Foghat Superstar Concert Series info-file structure: five real titles under Songs:, four FLAC checksum rows, and a four-row EAC TOC followed by EAC per-track detail sections.
- The five real titles are recognized as the local song-list candidate.
- The four EAC TOC/detail rows are never used as song titles.
- Because five titles cannot be safely mapped one-to-one onto four audio files, the direct local-title mapping is rejected and normal safe fallback behavior continues.

Versioning
- Public application version remains v1.4.
- Bundle/build number advances from 378 to 379.
- GitHub Build Process v067 remains compatible; no build-process change is required.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v380.txt =====
TLO v1.4 Build 380 changes

Compliant String1 - String2 Date precedence
- Adds a narrow compliant pattern between String1 Date String2 and the generic String1 - String2 fallback.
- When String1 Date String2 does not match, TLO now inspects String2 in a String1 - String2 folder for one complete performance date at the beginning or end of String2.
- A recognized complete boundary date is normalized and assigned as Date; the remaining String2 text is preserved as the raw album/show/venue tail.
- Example: "The Georgia Satellites - Santa Cruz 01-17-87" now resolves as Artist "The Georgia Satellites", Date "1987-01-17", String2/Album/Venue "Santa Cruz", and Show "The Georgia Satellites 1987-01-17 Santa Cruz".
- The same narrow rule recognizes a leading complete date inside String2, such as "Artist - 01-17-87 Santa Cruz".
- Bare years, year ranges, partial dates, and dates embedded in the middle of String2 are not reclassified by this new branch; existing compliant precedence continues for those cases.
- Generic String1 - String2 behavior remains unchanged when the boundary-date refinement does not match: no Date is assigned and String2 remains raw.

Add Shows alignment
- Compliant Add Shows duplicate detection recognizes the same String1 - String2 Date form before falling back to exact generic dash-show matching.
- New-show record construction preserves the full compliant parser result for this form, including normalized Date and cleaned String2.

Regression coverage
- Adds the reported Georgia Satellites / Santa Cruz / 01-17-87 case.
- Adds leading-date, generic-dash non-regression, and Add Shows coverage.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v383.txt =====
TLO v1.4 Build 383 changes

Reverse Copy/Delete + Rename
- Adds a Reverse Copy/Delete + Rename button to the main Inventory GUI.
- The reverse window uses the Inventory GUI's normal TLOHome resolution and displays the resolved TLOHome.
- It asks for the original partition name and the destination used by the prior Tag Copy/Delete Original operation. A visible volume/partition label is preferred and resolves to its current mounted drive/root; rooted forms such as D, D:, D:\\, and /mnt/d remain accepted as a fallback.
- Reverse discovery reads successful tags*.txt records (plus compatible legacy tag*.log records) and only accepts logs that explicitly show mode=copy-and-delete with rename compliantly=yes.
- TAG_COPY_DELETE_MOVE/COPY source -> destination lines are treated as the exact authoritative original/final folder mapping; no folder names are reconstructed from metadata.
- For a named original partition, the successful tag-log volume label must match and logged Windows/WSL source paths are remapped to the partition current root while preserving the path below the old drive root; rooted-path fallback input still filters directly by source root. Destination mappings are also filtered and de-duplicated.

Restore safety
- Restores folder name and location only. Audio tags are intentionally left unchanged.
- If the logged original path already exists while the moved folder also exists, TLO reports a conflict and leaves both trees untouched.
- Missing and already-restored mappings are reported instead of guessed.
- Same-filesystem reversals use a directory rename/move and do not perform a redundant size comparison.
- Cross-filesystem reversals copy the complete tree, including non-music files and empty directories, verify relative file paths/sizes and descendant directory structure, and only then delete the moved copy.
- A failed cross-filesystem copy/verification removes the partial newly-created original tree and leaves the current moved tree intact.

Logging and concurrency
- The original partition input, resolved original root, selected destination, and action are appended to TLOHome/logs/runSettings.log.
- Detailed per-folder results are appended to TLOHome/logs/reverseCopyDelete.log.
- Reverse Copy/Delete + Rename cannot overlap active Full Inventory, Add Shows, or Tag workflows.

Regression coverage
- Adds exact-name/location restore coverage, TLOHome environment-resolution coverage, named-volume/current-root remapping, ambiguous-volume rejection, combined-run filtering, conflict protection, destination/root filtering, and forced cross-filesystem copy/verify/delete coverage.
- Adds GUI coverage for the new button and TLOHome-scoped reverse dialog.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v384.txt =====
TLO v1.4 Build 384 changes

Reverse Copy/Delete + Rename log identification
- Original Partition Name is broadened to Original Partition / Path. It accepts a current visible volume label, a drive/root such as D: or /mnt/d, or a full original search path such as D:\somePath or /mnt/d/somePath.
- The historical volume label and drive letter no longer have to match the current partition. This covers renamed partitions as well as drive-letter changes.
- Reverse discovery evaluates eligible combined Copy/Delete Original + Rename Compliantly success logs and identifies one applicable log before any folder is moved.
- Log identification uses current/original path evidence, exact destination-folder matches, and artist+date evidence from logged/current folder names when stronger path evidence is unavailable.
- Artist+date comparison tolerates common Artist DB/display reorderings including Last, First -> First Last and Name, The <-> The Name.
- If more than one log matches equally well, reversal stops and asks for a narrower Original Partition / Path instead of guessing.

Selected-log-only restoration
- After one success log is selected, TLO does not search other logs again. The GUI passes the prepared selection directly to the worker so discovery is not repeated at execution time.
- All eligible combined-operation mappings from the selected log are considered. Logged source paths are remapped to the current original partition/path; logged destination paths are remapped beneath the current Copy/Delete Destination.
- Artist/date normalization is used only to identify the log. It is never used to guess which physical folder should be moved.
- Each restore still requires the exact destination path represented by the selected log. If that folder has been manually renamed or moved after TLO processing, it is left untouched and reported as SKIPPED_UNMATCHED.
- Existing-original conflict protection, same-filesystem move behavior, verified cross-filesystem copy/delete behavior, and unchanged audio tags remain in effect.

Logging and GUI
- The confirmation window reports the selected success-log filename and the evidence used to identify it.
- runSettings.log records the selected success log in addition to the original input/path and destination.
- reverseCopyDelete.log records the selected log/evidence and reports skipped unmatched folders separately from conflicts/errors.

Regression coverage
- Adds full-path remapping coverage for changed drive/volume identity, changed current volume-label coverage, Last/First and ', The' artist normalization, one-log-only selection, no-second-search execution, and manually renamed destination folders being left untouched.

Versioning
- Public application version remains v1.4.
- Bundle/build number advances from 383 to 384.
- GitHub Build Process v067 remains compatible; no build-process change is required.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v390.txt =====

- Phase 1 and Phase 2/3 now continue descending into child directories after a parent music folder is recorded.
- A nested music folder can therefore be discovered as an independent opening act.
- Existing wrapper/grouping rules remain responsible for keeping ordinary CD/Disc/Set/volume structures together.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v391.txt =====
TLO v1.4 Build 391

- Added an "acceptable corruption %" main-window text box and --acceptable-corruption-percent CLI option.
- Input is limited to integers 0 through 100 inclusive; default is 0.
- Before rename/copy/delete/tag mutations, TLO checks directly inventoried logical-show audio files and removes only when corrupt_files * 100 > acceptable_percent * total_audio_files.
- When the threshold is exceeded, TLO sends the logical show folder to the operating-system Trash/Recycle Bin and omits it from inventory only after that move succeeds.
- If Trash/Recycle Bin fails, TLO never permanently deletes as a fallback; it logs CORRUPTION_REMOVAL_FAILED and keeps the folder/inventory record.
- Full Inventory Dry Run reports WOULD_REMOVE_CORRUPTION without changing files.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v392.txt =====
TLO v1.4 Build 392

- Added a secondary Artist DB fallback for potential artist names whose final word is Band.
- TLO always searches the complete candidate first, so a real database artist ending in Band keeps precedence unchanged.
- Only when the complete candidate has no Artist DB match does TLO retry after removing the terminal word Band, case-insensitively.
- The stripped candidate must resolve to exactly one Artist DB master; ambiguous stripped matches are not promoted.
- When the stripped lookup succeeds, TLO stores the matched Artist DB master name under the normal artist-evidence precedence rules.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v393.txt =====
TLO v1.4 Build 393

- Changed the default acceptable corruption percentage from 0 to 100.
- The valid input range remains integers 0 through 100 inclusive.
- An explicit value of 0 remains valid and continues to remove a logical show when more than 0% of its directly inventoried audio files are corrupt.
- The corruption threshold comparison, Trash/Recycle Bin safety behavior, Dry Run behavior, and removal logging are otherwise unchanged.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v394.txt =====
TLO v1.4 Build 394

- Hardened Linux CI regression coverage for the acceptable-corruption feature.
- Five legacy tests intentionally create invalid FLAC placeholder bytes while testing unrelated inventory behaviors. Those tests now explicitly set acceptable corruption % to 100 so a functioning Linux `gio trash` backend cannot remove their synthetic test folders.
- This addresses the GitHub generic-Linux failures in tests v265, v266, v286, v303, and v356 that were seen in Build 392 when the corruption default was 0.
- Runtime application behavior is unchanged from Build 393; acceptable corruption % still defaults to 100 and explicit values from 0 through 100 remain valid.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v395.txt =====
TLO v1.4 Build 395

- Fixed selected-setlist parsing for concise `Location -- Venue` and `Venue -- Location` header lines.
- `Winnipeg, MB -- CBC Winnipeg studios` now resolves to Venue `CBC Winnipeg studios` and Location `Winnipeg MB` when MB is available in the configured region data.
- Date headers may carry a trailing double-dash duration such as `1991-09-21 -- 51:27`; the duration no longer prevents the line from being recognized as date/header evidence.
- Generic venue-list fallback now excludes concise comma-separated performer/personnel lists from venue substring matching.
- One-word venue terms of five or fewer normalized characters must stand alone or appear in an explicit `Venue:` field before generic venue fallback can accept them. This prevents a `Van` venue entry from matching `Townes Van Zandt` while preserving legitimate standalone `Van` metadata.
- Added Build 395 regression coverage for the reported Swingin' on a Star setlist and the reverse `Venue -- Location` form.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v396.txt =====
TLO v1.4 Build 396

- Hardened selected-setlist filename artist fallback so a short one-word Artist DB match embedded in a longer filename cannot become Artist without stronger filename structure.
- `Two Gentlemen.txt` no longer identifies Artist `Two` merely because `Two` exists in artists.sqlite.
- Exact one-word setlist filenames and leading one-word artists with a recognizable date or immediate delimiter remain supported; for example `Prince 1984-06-07.txt` can still identify Prince.
- Hardened trailing full-state/region-name parsing so title-like text such as `Two Gentlemen In New York` is not interpreted as City `Two Gentlemen In`, Region `NY`.
- Preserved legitimate no-comma full-state forms such as `Albany New York` and `Boca Raton Florida`.
- Generic String2/path venue-location splitting now rejects connective/preposition words such as `In` as a one-word city, preventing later re-inventory from amplifying the same bad interpretation.
- Added Build 396 regressions for all of the above cases.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v397.txt =====
TLO v1.4 Build 397 - incremental duplicate-detection correction

- Fixed Add Shows potential-duplicate detection so raw resolved show names and folder basenames are search probes only and can never satisfy the artist predicate directly. A same-date folder with a different artist is no longer sent to dups merely because the date appears in both show strings.
- Artist matching in duplicate detection now uses normalized whole-token/whole-phrase boundaries instead of arbitrary substring containment; for example, artist `Yes` no longer matches `Hayes`.
- Added regressions for same-date/different-artist folders, raw date folder names, token-boundary matching, and DB alias matching.

TLO v1.4 Build 397

Technical-review remediation based on the Build 396 review.

Runtime correctness and safety
- Tightened partial one-word selected-setlist filename artist matching. A date now establishes strong filename structure only when it is the first substantive component after the artist, allowing only ordinary filename separators before the date. Dated false positives such as `Two Gentlemen 1979-10-05`, `Live In Boston 1999-01-01`, and `The Wall 1980-08-09` no longer promote `Two`, `Live`, or `The`; supported forms such as `Prince 1984-06-07`, `Prince - 1984-06-07`, `Prince_1984-06-07`, and `Prince - Purple Rain Tour` remain valid.
- Bounded selected-setlist metadata reads. Text files over 16 MiB are rejected by the header parser; otherwise at most the existing 1 MiB text sample is read because only the first 100 usable metadata lines are consumed.
- Added a 180-second per-file timeout to tlo-deleteDupes full FLAC decode validation. Timeout returns an unverifiable state rather than healthy/corrupt. A kept folder with an unverifiable FLAC blocks relocation of that duplicate cluster.
- Pinned normal update checks to the official `onaracstlo-lab/TradersLittleOrganizer` repository using build-time constants. `TLO_GITHUB_OWNER` / `TLO_GITHUB_REPO` environment variables no longer redirect the update source.
- Update downloads now reject declared assets over a 1 GiB hard ceiling before transfer and stream in 1 MiB chunks. Downloading aborts if bytes exceed the declared release size or the hard ceiling, and declared-size equality is required on completion.
- Update-settings persistence failures are now disclosed in the returned update result rather than silently ignored.
- macOS Trash handling passes the folder path to osascript as argv/data instead of interpolating it into AppleScript source.

Code and architecture hygiene
- Moved the shared location connective/preposition set into tlo_constants so path and setlist location engines cannot drift.
- Replaced the standalone setlistFM.py API implementation with a thin compatibility CLI over production tlo_setlistfm_lookup, including the same TLOHome rate-limit/quota coordination.
- Removed stale per-module `__version_summary__`, `# TLO-GI package version`, and `# TLO-GI version summary` metadata. tlo_version.py remains the authoritative version/summary source; simple `__version__` stamps remain.
- Converted every broad `except Exception: pass` best-effort block in root Python sources into debug-recorded suppression using tlo_diagnostics; no silent broad-pass blocks remain.
- Extracted the String1 - String2 album/performance decision branch from the large metadata resolver into `_resolve_dash_album_or_performance_before_date_resolution` and extracted the one-word filename structure rule into its own predicate.

Tests and documentation
- Added focused unit regressions for positional filename dates, bounded setlist reads, duplicate-cleanup timeout behavior, official updater source, bounded downloads, shared location constants, production setlistFM delegation, and macOS Trash argv handling.
- Added contract coverage requiring exactly one category marker per test module and requiring the gui and slow trait markers to be used.
- Added missing category markers to previously unmarked behavior/contract modules; integration workflows are explicitly marked slow.
- Migrated the touched Build 395/396 setlist regressions out of tests/_legacy_suite.py into categorized modules.
- Expanded the GitHub-compatible test_tlo_requirements.py entry point to include previously omitted categorized modules and the new Build 397 regressions/contracts.
- Consolidated the requirements document so numbered authoritative sections stop at Section 20; superseded Build 392-396 top-level append-only sections were folded into their topical sections.
- Corrected the one-word filename requirement wording to: the date must be the `first substantive component after the artist`.
- Corrected the RTF manual document keywords/build metadata to Build 397 and updated user-facing updater, duplicate-cleanup timeout, filename-artist, and compatibility utility guidance.

Release compatibility
- Public application version remains v1.4.
- GitHub Build Process v067 remains compatible; no new third-party runtime dependency was added.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v398.txt =====
TLO v1.4 Build 398

Corrupt-audio Trash/Recycle Bin policy
- Corruption percentage is calculated from one pre-removal audio-file snapshot so total and corrupt counts cannot come from different directory scans.
- An all-corrupt logical show is always moved to the OS Trash/Recycle Bin and omitted from inventory after a successful move, even when Acceptable corruption % is 100.
- When the logical show remains, each physical music directory is checked separately; an all-corrupt Disc/Set/Volume folder is moved to Trash/Recycle Bin when it can be removed without taking healthy nested music directories with it.
- Every detected corrupt audio file not already contained by a successfully trashed folder is individually moved to Trash/Recycle Bin before inventory/tag/copy/rename processing continues.
- If a whole-show or all-corrupt-folder Trash operation fails, TLO logs the failure and still makes a best-effort attempt to Trash the corrupt files individually. No permanent-delete fallback is used.
- Successful folder/file removals are pruned from active group and ShowMetadata paths before later mutation stages, preventing stale-path fallback.
- Dry run distinguishes whole-show corruption removal, all-corrupt physical music-folder removal, and individual corrupt-file removal.

Tests/documentation
- Added/updated regression coverage for the all-corrupt 100% override, threshold equality/exceedance, an all-corrupt music folder beside a healthy sibling, partial corruption, no corruption, Trash-failure fallback, and path pruning.
- Updated requirements and user manual to identify this corruption policy as Build 398.

Release compatibility
- Public application version remains v1.4.
- GitHub Build Process v071 remains compatible; its version is independent of the TLO application build number.
- No new third-party runtime dependency was added.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v399.txt =====
TLO v1.4 Build 399

Cross-partition potential-duplicate handling
- Add Shows Process New Shows now classifies potential duplicate bootlist matches by the partition identified by Current Backup/Storage Drive and Volume when Check for Duplicates is selected.
- If every matching row is definitively on another partition, TLO assumes the existing folders are unavailable for comparison and retains/stages the incoming show instead of moving it to dups.
- The retained show receives a `(pdupNN)` qualifier. Numbering begins at `(pdup01)` and advances one past the highest existing pdup number in the matching potential-duplicate family.
- Rooted Windows/WSL drive or macOS/Linux mount identity takes precedence. Matching volume labels are used as a fallback for legacy bootlist rows that lack a rooted physical path.
- If any potential match is on the current partition, or partition identity is unknown, the established duplicate-review and copy/alternate handling remains unchanged.
- `(pdupNN)` means potential duplicate only; it is never treated as proof that two folders are copies or alternates.
- The marker is part of final Show identity and propagates to bootlist output, generated setlist naming, tagging/Album parentheticals, and Rename Compliantly folder names when rename is enabled. Without Rename Compliantly, the original physical folder name remains unchanged.
- Dry run reports when a cross-partition-only match would be staged as a pdup instead of moved to dups.
- Add Shows completion now separately reports the count of cross-partition potential duplicates staged as pdup.

Regression coverage
- Added tests for Windows/WSL partition identity, label-only legacy rows, unknown partition handling, mixed local/remote matches, monotonically increasing pdup numbering, compliant pdup family matching, remote-only staging, and preservation of the same-partition dups workflow.

Release compatibility
- Public application version remains v1.4.
- TLO application build advances from 398 to 399 because source behavior changed.
- GitHub Build Process v071 remains independently versioned and does not change for this application-only update.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v400.txt =====
TLO v1.4 Build 400

Corruption verification and fail-closed Trash safety
- Corruption scanning now distinguishes positively proven audio corruption from an unverifiable read/validation result.
- PermissionError, OSError/IOError, unavailable-directory/listing failures, MemoryError/resource failures, and unexpected validator exceptions are classified as unverifiable rather than corrupt.
- Mutagen parse failures and an otherwise readable file that is not recognized as supported audio remain positively corrupt.
- If any audio path or inventoried music directory in a logical show is unverifiable, TLO performs no corruption-driven Trash action and skips rename, tagging, Copy/Delete, and SHN-conversion mutation for that logical show. Inventory metadata may still be recorded.
- The Build 398 policy remains unchanged for proven corruption: an all-proven-corrupt logical show or independently removable physical music folder is trashed regardless of acceptable corruption %, and proven-corrupt individual files are trashed when the logical show remains.
- Windows corruption removal now uses IFileOperation with FOFX_RECYCLEONDELETE and FOFX_EARLYFAILURE instead of deprecated SHFileOperationW. If recycling cannot be completed, TLO reports failure and leaves the item in place; no permanent-delete fallback exists.
- Dry Run now reports corruption as unverifiable and WOULD_NOT_TRASH when validation cannot be completed.

Regression coverage
- Added tests for PermissionError, OSError, directory-read failures, unexpected validator failures, positive Mutagen parse corruption, preservation of the all-proven-corrupt override, and absence of the legacy Windows SHFileOperationW path.

Release compatibility
- Public application version remains v1.4.
- TLO application build advances from 399 to 400 because source behavior changed.
- GitHub Build Process v071 remains independently versioned and unchanged.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v401.txt =====
TLO v1.4 Build 401

Setlist-family correctness
- Replacement cleanup and incremental setlist inference now match only the exact normalized Show setlist plus canonical `(altN)` variants.
- Prefix-only matches are rejected, preventing a replaced Show such as `Phish 1997-11-17` from deleting or surfacing the setlist for `Phish 1997-11-17 Late Show`.
- Replacement cleanup explicitly protects filenames belonging to retained bootlist rows.

Literal directory enumeration

- TLOHome/log paths containing legal glob metacharacters such as `[` and `]` now work literally.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v402.txt =====
TLO v1.4 Build 402

Irreversible transfer verification
- Cross-partition Tag Copy/Delete now requires exact recursive SHA-256 tree identity after relative-path/size/structure verification and before deleting the source.
- Same-partition Copy/Delete remains an os.rename filesystem move and intentionally performs no size or hash comparison.
- Ordinary Tag Copy continues to verify relative paths/sizes/structure without SHA-256 because the original remains available.
- File-size verification now fails on stat errors instead of storing a `-1` sentinel that could compare equal.

Owned temporary-copy rollback
- Forward Copy/Delete and Tag Copy stage into an operation-owned temporary sibling before committing the final destination name.
- Before source deletion begins, a failed copy/verification cleans up only the destination created by the current operation.
- Once source deletion has begun, the verified destination is retained even if source cleanup errors.

Reverse Copy/Delete safety
- Reverse cross-partition restoration now uses the same exact SHA-256 tree comparator before deleting the current moved copy.
- Containment is checked before creating the original parent path.
- Cross-partition restore copies into an owned temporary sibling, verifies it, rechecks that the original path is absent, and then renames the verified copy into place.
- Rollback removes only the operation-owned temporary directory; it never removes `original`.

Regression coverage
- Added same-size/different-bytes hash mismatch tests, stat-failure fail-closed coverage, destination rollback checks, same-partition no-hash coverage, and reverse containment/rollback guards.

Release compatibility
- Public application version remains v1.4.
- TLO application build advances from 401 to 402 because source behavior changed.
- GitHub Build Process v071 remains independently versioned and unchanged.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v403.txt =====
TLO v1.4 Build 403

Duplicate validation and repair safety
- FLAC validation now reserves False for an actual completed decoder failure; timeouts, OSError/resource failures, and unexpected validator infrastructure errors return unverifiable (None).
- Healthy repair sources are staged and SHA-256 byte-verified before installation; the keeper bytes are backed up through the atomic replacement step.
- If a kept folder still has an unrepaired corrupt FLAC, its qualifying duplicate folders are retained for later review instead of being relocated.
- Unverifiable keeper FLACs continue to block duplicate relocation.

Incremental duplicate removal safety
- Delete New / Keep Old now routes the new folder through the same fail-closed Trash/Recycle Bin mechanism used for corruption handling.
- If Trash/Recycle Bin is unavailable or fails, the folder remains in place and the operation reports an error.
- The generated Windows incremental .bat delete writer is intentionally unchanged in this build.

Regression coverage
- Added OSError/MemoryError tri-state validator tests, nonzero-decoder corruption coverage, transactional repair staging checks, fail-closed incremental Trash tests, and updated the unrepaired-keeper behavior contract.

Release compatibility
- Public application version remains v1.4.
- TLO application build advances from 402 to 403 because source behavior changed.
- GitHub Build Process v071 remains independently versioned and unchanged.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v404.txt =====
TLO v1.4 Build 404

Update-channel hardening
- Redirects followed while downloading an update are revalidated at every hop against the existing GitHub HTTPS host allowlist. A redirect to any other host is rejected.
- Release assets must provide a syntactically valid SHA-256 digest. Missing digests fail closed instead of silently disabling integrity verification.
- Existing downloads are reused only when size and SHA-256 both match. Newly downloaded bytes are SHA-256 verified before atomic destination replacement.

Windows custom scanner command safety
- The trusted custom scanner template remains a shell command, but the artifact path is no longer interpolated directly into cmd.exe text on Windows.
- The path is supplied through TLO_SCAN_ARTIFACT_PATH and the template receives a quoted environment-variable reference, preventing %, &, ^, and similar path characters from becoming command syntax during interpolation.

Regression coverage
- Added redirect rejection, missing-digest fail-closed, valid-digest commit, existing-file verification, and Windows custom-scanner path tests.

Release compatibility
- Public application version remains v1.4.
- TLO application build advances from 403 to 404 because source behavior changed.
- GitHub Build Process v071 remains independently versioned and unchanged.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v405.txt =====
TLO v1.4 Build 405

Cancellation log-token safety
- Removed the redundant active_log_tokens runtime/config field. newly_allocated_log_tokens is now the single authoritative set for Ctrl-C and GUI Quit cleanup.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v406.txt =====
TLO v1.4 Build 406

GitHub regression-fixture stabilization
- Application runtime behavior is unchanged from Build 405.
- Five historical regression tests for unidentified-show handling, inventory Copy/Delete transfer, Rename Compliantly, and Artist-in-Album use intentionally tiny placeholder bytes with a .flac suffix. Build 398+ correctly classifies those bytes as corrupt, so those tests must not depend on whether the host Trash backend is available.
- The five non-corruption tests now explicitly neutralize corruption classification only for their own scenarios. They do not mock or disable Trash/Recycle Bin itself.
- Dedicated corruption tests remain responsible for validating real corrupt-file classification, fail-closed Trash behavior, all-corrupt folder handling, and unverifiable read handling.
- Added Build 406 regression checks that keep the neutralization limited to exactly those five tests and ensure it patches only corruption classification, not the Trash implementation.

GitHub/Linux reproduction
- Build 405 could appear fully green on a Linux host without a working Trash backend because fail-closed Trash left the invalid placeholder files in place.
- GitHub generic Linux has a working gio trash backend. There, the same placeholder files were actually removed and the five unrelated orchestration tests failed.
- Build 406 removes that environment dependency. The full suite is validated with a working Trash backend so it matches the GitHub condition that exposed the problem.

TLO v1.4 Build 407

Artist DB terminal suffix normalization
- Extends the established terminal Band fallback to common terminal All Star/All Stars forms: All Star, All Stars, All-Star, All-Stars, AllStar, and AllStars, case-insensitively. A dash may also separate the base artist name from the All-Star suffix.
- The complete candidate is always queried first, so a real Artist DB entry whose full name includes Band or an All-Star suffix always wins.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v408.txt =====
TLO v1.4 Build 408

Generic song-title placeholders
- Treats a complete cleaned song title of Title, Titled, or Titles as Unknown, case-insensitively, when the value comes from a setlist or an existing audio Title tag.
- The rule covers numbered setlists, unnumbered CD/Disc/Set sections, unstructured one-title-per-line setlists, comma-separated setlist items, eTreeDB/setlist.fm text parsed through the normal setlist title parser, and existing audio Title/TIT2-style tag fallback.
- A generic setlist row still occupies its original track position; the title alone becomes Unknown, so later track numbers do not shift.
- Numbered existing title tags such as "01. Title" are normalized first and therefore also become Unknown.
- The comparison is against the complete cleaned title only. Legitimate longer names such as "The Title Track", "A Song Titled Sue", and "Titles Are Hard" remain unchanged.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v409.txt =====
TLO v1.4 Build 409

Numbered setlist safety and blank-track handling
- A leading bare track number such as "01" is now preserved as an Unknown title when the next meaningful numbered song row confirms the sequence, for example "01" followed by "02 Day Waves".
- The confirmation rule is conservative: an isolated bare number before unrelated prose is not manufactured into a track.
- A short numeric-only row can no longer start an unstructured unnumbered-title fallback block. Numeric song names such as "69" and "1999" remain eligible when embedded inside an established multi-line song block.
- Compact personnel credits ending in a recognized instrument/role parenthetical, including forms such as "Gary Burton(vib)" and "Chick Corea (p)", are excluded from unnumbered title candidates.
- Duration-summary lines such as "min 38:24", "38:24 min", and "total time 38:24" are excluded from unnumbered title candidates.
- If local setlist text contains a convincing run of at least three consecutive numbered song rows, TLO will not scavenge unrelated unnumbered prose as a fallback merely because the prose count happens to equal the audio-file count.
- The reported Chick Corea & Gary Burton example now resolves the six titles as Unknown, Day Waves, Desert Air, Crystal Silence, Children Song No. 1, and La Fiesta.

Tests and documentation
- Added Build 409 regression coverage for confirmed leading blank tracks, isolated bare numbers, numeric-only fallback protection, compact personnel notation, duration summaries, the numbered-evidence fallback safety gate, and the complete reported setlist.
- Updated the requirements specification, user manual, source-bundle readme, version stamps, and packaging metadata for Build 409.
- Build 408 change notes are archived in old-change-logs.zip.

Release compatibility
- Public application version remains v1.4.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v410.txt =====
TLO v1.4 Build 410

Explicit unnumbered setlists and multi-disc ordering
- Audio filenames that contain both disc/disk/CD and track tokens now sort by disc first and track second even when punctuation separates the tokens. Collector forms such as "CJM-Disc01,Track01.flac", "Disk02-Track03.flac", "CD03_Track09.flac", and "d04.t05.flac" are recognized as disc/track pairs.
- This prevents multi-disc files in one folder from being interleaved by track number (Disc 1 Track 1, Disc 2 Track 1, Disc 1 Track 2, Disc 2 Track 2), which previously caused written Track Number tags to appear as odd numbers on one disc and even numbers on the other.
- When an explicit Setlist:, Set List:, Songs:, Tracks:, or equivalent section is present but its songs are unnumbered, Notes: and other track-list terminators now end the song section even when the numbered parser has not accepted any song rows. Later collector-note paragraphs beginning 1), 2), 3), and similar numbering can no longer replace the actual song list.
- The unnumbered-song heuristic now preserves legitimate titles beginning with "Thank", including "Thank The Nurse That's Nursing You", while still recognizing explicit thank-you prose such as "Thanks to the taper" and "Thank you" as non-song terminators.
- The encore abbreviation "Enc:" is normalized the same as "E:" and "Encore:" so "Enc:This Land Is Your Land" becomes the title "This Land Is Your Land".
- The reported Country Joe 2001-05-26 example now selects all 19 real songs from the explicit Setlist block and ignores the numbered Notes paragraphs.

Tests and documentation
- Added Build 410 regression coverage for comma/punctuation-separated disc/track filenames, disc-first ordering, numbered Notes after an explicit unnumbered Setlist section, the complete 19-song Country Joe setlist, legitimate Thank-prefixed titles, explicit thank-you prose termination, and Enc: cleanup.
- Updated the requirements specification, user manual, source-bundle readme, version stamps, and packaging metadata for Build 410.
- Build 409 change notes are archived in old-change-logs.zip.

Release compatibility
- Public application version remains v1.4.
- TLO application build advances from 409 to 410.
- GitHub Build Process v071 remains independently versioned and unchanged because it already consumes the application build number at runtime and requires no Build 410-specific code change.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v411.txt =====
TLO v1.4 Build 411

Track-list candidate selection and positive corroboration
- Local track-title selection now compares an exact-count unnumbered candidate before a shorter numbered run can be padded with Unknown titles. A 17-song list therefore beats three numbered collector notes when there are 17 audio files.
- When numbered and unnumbered candidates both exactly match the audio-file count, TLO checks the corresponding audio filenames and existing Title tags for positive corroboration. Matching evidence reinforces a candidate; missing, generic, unreadable, or unrelated filename/tag text contributes zero and never counts against a candidate.
- Filename corroboration accepts either a normal filename-derived title match or the complete normalized candidate song title appearing within the corresponding filename. Existing Title-tag corroboration requires the cleaned usable tag title to match the candidate title.
- A short numbered candidate embedded in surrounding prose/collector notes can lose an otherwise unsupported exact-count tie to a contiguous unnumbered song block. Outside that structural case, an exact-count numbered candidate retains normal precedence when neither candidate has positive corroboration.
- The reported Cramps 1984-06-25 example is covered end-to-end: the three numbered processing notes are ignored and all 17 real titles from Devil With A Blue Dress On through She Said are selected.

Tests and documentation
- Added Build 411 regression coverage for 17-vs-3 count discrimination, filename reinforcement, existing Title-tag reinforcement, reinforcement of a valid numbered candidate, neutral non-matches, and an equal-count prose-embedded numbered-notes ambiguity.
- Updated requirements, manual, source-bundle readme, version stamps, CI compatibility imports, and packaging metadata for Build 411.
- Build 410 change notes are archived in old-change-logs.zip.

Release compatibility
- Public application version remains v1.4.
- TLO application build advances from 410 to 411.
