===== Historical record: CHANGES_v412.txt =====
TLO v1.4 Build 412

Explicit Set/CD/Disc/Disk track-region boundaries
- Explicit unnumbered song-list headings now establish a protected song region. Supported starts include Set 1/2/etc., Disc 1, Disk 1, CD 1, Setlist 1, Set List 1, Track List 1, Songs:, Tracks:, and equivalent numbered forms already recognized by the setlist parser.
- Plausible short artist, venue/location, and lineage/header text above the first explicit song-region boundary is discarded from unnumbered title candidates. Those header lines can no longer be combined with later songs merely because the accidental total equals the number of audio files.
- Subsequent Set/Disc/Disk/CD headings continue the same ordered title sequence. A bare Encore heading is a continuation boundary, not a new start boundary, so songs before Encore remain part of the list.
- Blank lines may separate blocks within an explicit song region. Date-labelled continuation/filler dividers such as "on July 7, 1967:" are skipped as titles while following song lines remain eligible.
- Notes/source/checksum/technical markers and decorative separator lines terminate the explicit song region. Decorated collector thanks lines such as "*Thanks Mike..." remain terminators without breaking legitimate Encore handling.
- The reported Reverend Gary Davis 1967-07-08 case now maps the 16 audio files to the 16 real titles from Introduction through Samson and Delilah. Reverend Gary Davis, Friends' Center, Seattle, WA, and SBD > ? > CDr > EAC > FLAC above Set 1 are not titles.

Tests and documentation
- Added Build 412 regression coverage for the complete Reverend Gary Davis setlist, Set/Disc/Disk/CD/Setlist/Track List boundary variants, multi-disc continuation, and date-labelled filler continuation.
- Preserved existing bare-Encore and decorated-thanks termination behavior through compatibility regressions.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v413.txt =====
TLO v1.4 Build 413

Dated subsection boundaries
- Valid numeric date-prefixed broadcast/show headings with trailing venue, source, broadcast, or subsection context are non-title boundaries. A heading such as "30.10.1948 - Royal Roost, NY - WMCA Radio broadcast" cannot be parsed as track 30.
- Numeric contextual date boundaries require a valid calendar date before they receive boundary status.
- Year-first and the day/month or month/day numeric conventions already used by TLO remain supported.
- A narrow lost-newline repair removes a dated subsection heading that becomes directly attached to an apostrophe-style duration on the preceding numbered song row.

Numbering continuity guardrail
- When an established numbered run expects N, a provisional higher-number row is not committed if the very next numbered row is N. The provisional row is treated as a false positive and normal numbering resumes.
- This prevents a sequence such as tracks 01-07, then the date-heading day value 30, then real track 08 from assigning the date heading to normalized track 8 and padding later files with Unknown.
- Confirmed large forward gaps remain supported. A sequence such as 01, 02, 30, 31 still retains tracks 30 and 31 because the later row reinforces the jump.

Reported regression
- The Tadd Dameron Royal Roost 1948 setlist now preserves all fourteen real numbered songs across the 16.10.1948, 23.10.1948, 30.10.1948, and 06.11.1948 broadcast sections.
- The 30.10.1948 heading is never emitted as a title or normalized as track 8, and the later tracks are no longer replaced by trailing Unknown values because of that false row.

Tests and documentation
- Added Build 413 regression coverage for the complete reported dated-broadcast pattern, the independent resumed-numbering false-positive case, confirmed large-gap compatibility, calendar-date boundary validation, and the lost-newline date-tail repair.
- Updated requirements, manual, source-bundle readme, version stamps, CI compatibility imports, and packaging metadata for Build 413.
- Build 412 change notes are archived in old-change-logs.zip.

Release compatibility
- Public application version remains v1.4.
- TLO application build advances from 412 to 413.
- GitHub Build Process v071 remains independently versioned and unchanged because it already consumes the application build number at runtime and requires no Build 413-specific code change.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v414.txt =====
TLO v1.4 Build 414

Location-safe path artist evidence
- Generic path artist scanning no longer promotes a DB artist phrase when that occurrence is only the trailing city of a longer City, State/Region/Country location suffix.
- Thus "Washington" inside "My Recording 2015-04-10 Kennedy Center Washington, DC" is treated as location text rather than weak artist evidence.
- Exact whole-component artist directories remain eligible; an artist directory named exactly "Washington" is not suppressed by this safeguard.

Structured setlist artist headers
- Structured unlabeled setlist headers may contain one extra room/hall line between Artist and the named Venue.
- A five-line form such as Artist / Concert Hall / Kennedy Center / Washington, DC / Date keeps the first line as Artist and uses Kennedy Center as Venue instead of shifting Concert Hall into the artist slot.
- Conservative collaboration headers separated by a standalone and, with, &, or + may resolve without a combined Artist DB row only when every component independently resolves uniquely in the Artist DB.
- If any component is missing or ambiguous, the collaboration contributes no artist evidence; TLO does not guess.

Bare-number track blocks
- When one contiguous bare-number block starts at 0/1, is consecutive, and exactly matches the audio-file count, the numbers are treated as track positions and every corresponding Title is written as Unknown.
- Bare numeric values themselves are never written as song titles by this rule.
- Count mismatches or multiple competing bare-number blocks contribute no candidate and do not count negatively against other title sources.

Reported regression
- The Chick Corea and Herbie Hancock 2015-04-10 Kennedy Center setlist now resolves artist "Chick Corea and Herbie Hancock", venue "Kennedy Center", location "Washington, DC", and eleven Unknown song titles for the eleven bare numbered positions.
- The City value Washington is no longer promoted as artist merely because Washington is also present in the Artist DB.

Tests and documentation
- Added Build 414 regression coverage for five-line structured headers, strict collaboration resolution, location-tail artist suppression with exact-directory compatibility, full metadata extraction, exact-count bare-number Unknown titles, and count-mismatch neutrality.
- Updated requirements, manual, source-bundle readme, version stamps, CI compatibility imports, and packaging metadata for Build 414.
- Build 413 change notes are archived in old-change-logs.zip.

Release compatibility
- Public application version remains v1.4.
- TLO application build advances from 413 to 414.
- GitHub Build Process v071 remains independently versioned and unchanged because it already consumes the application build number at runtime and requires no Build 414-specific code change.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v415.txt =====
TLO v1.4 Build 415

Copy/Delete destination replacement scope
- Tag Copy/Delete Original no longer treats the destination parent directory as a subtree re-inventory scope merely because that destination already has inventory logs.
- A transfer such as /mnt/d/x -> /mnt/d/boots now preserves all unrelated existing bootlist rows and setlists under /mnt/d/boots while appending the newly transferred shows.
- Postprocess may replace a prior destination row only when its full Volume+Path exactly matches a destination show path produced by the current Copy/Delete run. Child paths, sibling shows, and the rest of the destination tree are preserved.
- Normal direct inventory of an existing destination tree is unchanged and may still re-inventory that requested tree under the ordinary path-overlap policy.

Reported regression
- A run with 29 current records copied/deleted into /mnt/d/boots no longer reports or performs replacement of 2,135 unrelated existing /boots rows. The destination parent is treated as an append container for Copy/Delete ownership, not as permission to replace the entire parent subtree.

Tests and documentation

Structured Live-at setlist header interpretation

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v418.txt =====
TLO v1.4 Build 418

Restored terminal artist suffix performance names
- The guarded terminal Artist DB fallback still searches the complete candidate first and strips a supported terminal suffix only after a complete-name miss.
- When the stripped base resolves uniquely, TLO now uses the Artist DB master only to identify the base artist, then restores the exact stripped terminal text for the performance artist.
- Supported restored suffix forms remain Band, Group, All Star/All Stars, All-Star/All-Stars, AllStar/AllStars, All Star Band/All-Star Band, and plural All Stars Band/All-Stars Band forms.
- Example: if "Example Group" is not in the Artist DB but "Example" uniquely resolves to "Example Master", the performance artist becomes "Example Master Group" rather than "Example Master".
- If the resolved Artist DB master already ends in a supported grouping suffix, TLO does not append another suffix; this prevents names such as "Band Band".
- Full-name matches still win, nonterminal suffix words are not stripped, and ambiguous stripped-base matches remain unresolved.

Artist DB review list
- Restored suffix-fallback performance artists that are not themselves full Artist DB matches are marked in metadata and persisted to TLOHome/artistsNotInDatabase.txt.
- artistsNotInDatabase.txt preserves prior entries, merges current-run entries, sorts case-insensitively, and de-duplicates case-insensitively.
- A DB master that already contains the suffix and is therefore used unchanged is not added to the missing-artist review list.

Tests and documentation
- Added Build 418 regressions covering suffix restoration, exact/full-name precedence, ambiguity, duplicate-suffix prevention, metadata marking, metadata-log round-trip, persistent review-list merge/sort/dedupe, and full metadata extraction.
- Updated requirements, manual, source-bundle readme, version stamps, and CI compatibility references for Build 418.

- Non-compliant metadata records with ALBUM_NAME now use that value as the base Album tag.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v420.txt =====
TLO v1.4 Build 420

Thorough Setlist Matching
- Added a default-off main-window checkbox named Thorough Setlist Matching and CLI switch --thorough-setlist-matching.
- Normal mode preserves early stopping and the established local setlist -> eTreeDB -> setlist.fm fallback behavior.
- Thorough mode collects and compares additional local, eTreeDB, and enabled setlist.fm candidates instead of stopping at the first acceptable source.

Coverage versus authority
- setlist.fm upgrade continues to change access capacity only: normal access remains one request every 600 ms with a 1,400-call run cap; upgraded access remains 14 requests/second and 48,000 requests/UTC day.
- Thorough mode may be used with normal or upgraded setlist.fm access. When Thorough is on without upgrade, GUI/help/review text warns that normal setlist.fm limits can make coverage slower or incomplete.
- With Thorough + setlist.fm enabled, setlist.fm is queried proactively even after a usable eTreeDB answer and additional same-date result pages may be collected.
- More setlist.fm access never raises setlist.fm authority in the final decision.

Setlist deconfliction
- Thorough mode retains exact-count and near-count candidates within +/-2 tracks.
- Candidate scoring considers local structural strength, audio-count distance, positive filename and existing Title-tag corroboration, Venue/Location compatibility, and agreement with independent source families.
- Strong recording-local evidence can beat online consensus; matching eTreeDB and setlist.fm evidence can beat a weak/unverified local parse.
- Sequence alignment can place recording-only Intro/Tuning/Crowd tracks or drop performance-only Encore rows without shifting every later title.
- Near-count mappings require at least two corroborated audio positions and at least 50% coverage of available audio-title evidence.
- Materially different near-tied candidates from different source families are reported as Thorough Setlist Matching ambiguity instead of silently choosing by source order.

Tests and documentation
- Added Build 420 behavior coverage for the new option, GUI/review exposure, proactive setlist.fm behavior, normal-mode short-circuiting, setlist.fm multi-page thorough collection, consensus scoring, local-audio corroboration, and sequence skew correction.
- Updated requirements, manual, source-bundle readme, version stamps, and GitHub-compatible test entry point for Build 420.
- Build 419 change notes are archived in old-change-logs.zip.

Release compatibility
- Public application version remains v1.4.
- TLO application build advances from 419 to 420.
- GitHub Build Process remains independently versioned at v073 and requires no Build 420-specific process change.

- Starts from and preserves the verified v1.5 Build 426 source baseline.
- Recognizes date-bearing show layouts in which numbered music parts are one additional directory below a non-numbered media container, such as Show/FLAC/Set 1 and Show/FLAC/Set 2.
- Supports the existing numbered Set, Disc, Disk, CD, D, Part, Side, and Tape wrapper forms at that lower level.
- Promotes the logical show root across exactly one validated media-container layer, allowing a setlist stored directly in the show folder to apply to all parts.
- Keeps all audio ordering and album-wide sequential Track Number behavior intact, so Set 1 completes before Set 2.

Safety boundaries
-----------------
- Requires the proposed show root to contain a recognized date.
- Requires every music-bearing descendant of that proposed show root to be a direct child of the same validated media container.
- Refuses promotion when unrelated music, such as a nested opening act, also exists below the proposed show root.
- Setlist selection checks only direct files in the validated show root. It does not enumerate or recursively rescan sibling show folders.

Documentation, tests, and packaging
-----------------------------------
- Advances the application build from 426 to 427 while retaining public version 1.5.
- Updates the integrated user manual and TLO requirements in their wrapper/grouping sections.
- Adds focused regressions for Set, Disc, Disk, CD, and D folder forms, correct set ordering/title alignment, sibling-show isolation, unrelated nested music, and undated-container rejection.
- Retains the independently versioned Run-TLO-GitHub-Build.ps1 and TLO_GitHub_Build_Process_Requirements_v075.docx at the source-bundle root.
- Archives Build 426 change notes in old-change-logs.zip and regenerates the Build 427 SHA-256 manifest.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v428.txt =====
TLO v1.5 Build 428
==================

Same-partition sibling collections
----------------------------------
- Starts from and preserves the verified v1.5 Build 427 source baseline.
- Recognizes non-date sibling collection families with incrementing D, CD, Disc, Disk, Set, Part, Side, Tape, terminal (N), and altN suffixes.
- Allows a single year, year span, decade label, or no year in a collection name; a complete performance date excludes this reorganization rule.
- Creates one parent named from the stripped shared release base, retains every accepted source folder beneath it, and inventories only that parent.
- Handles music below a bounded child media container, including D1/FLAC and D2/FLAC.

Content discrimination and tagging
----------------------------------
- Treats numbered D/CD-style folders as explicit multipart evidence.
- Requires usable title evidence for alt folders and keeps identical, substantially overlapping, shifted, or insufficiently evidenced alternatives separate.
- Uses setlist text first and audio filenames as fallback evidence; it does not search another partition for comparison data.
- Concatenates all usable child setlists into parent info.txt in deterministic child/file order.
- Orders all tracks from one accepted child before the next and assigns a continuous collection-wide Track Number sequence: 01-N through 99 tracks and 001-N above 99.

Mutation safety
---------------
- Requires the candidate siblings and proposed parent to report the same filesystem device.
- Uses a unique hidden temporary sibling directory and a transaction journal before moving any source folder.
- Refuses independent destination collisions, overlapping plans, duplicate part indices, and mixed-device families.
- Atomically rewrites current complete-path log entries after the final parent rename.
- Rolls back moved folders and the complete-path log after a failed transaction.
- Recovers a validated interrupted transaction before the next scan; an unreadable or unsafe TLO journal blocks inventory for review.
- Prunes unfinished .tlo-collection-* directories from ordinary phase-1 traversal.
- Dry Run never creates, renames, recovers, or moves collection folders; it blocks for review when an interrupted transaction is present.

Documentation, tests, and packaging
-----------------------------------
- Advances the application build from 427 to 428 while retaining public version 1.5.
- Updates the integrated user manual and TLO requirements with the collection rules and safety boundaries.
- Adds 17 focused regressions covering grouping, similarity/skew, nested media, numbering, same-device enforcement, collisions, rollback, recovery, and deterministic setlist concatenation.
- Retains Run-TLO-GitHub-Build.ps1 and TLO_GitHub_Build_Process_Requirements_v075.docx at the source-bundle root; the build-process version remains independently v075.
- Archives Build 427 change notes in old-change-logs.zip and regenerates the Build 428 SHA-256 manifest.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v429.txt =====
TLO v1.5 Build 429
==================

Re-inventory accounting
-----------------------
- Starts from and preserves the verified v1.5 Build 428 source baseline.
- Adds explicit path-scoped re-inventory reconciliation after new bootlist rows are built.
- Reports previous bootlist rows replaced, Stage 2 groups prepared, the prepared-versus-previous delta, Stage 3 metadata records, pre-metadata omissions, proven-corruption removals, unresolved rows omitted, final new rows, and net replacement-row change.
- Appends the detailed accounting to TLOHome/logs/reinventoryDelta.log and summary.log and prints one concise POSTPROCESS reconciliation status line.
- Covers the reported 3349 previous / 3384 prepared / 3378 Stage 3 / 3376 final-row case, including six corruption removals and two unresolved omissions.

Dated-show grouping safety
--------------------------
- Keeps recognized supplemental, source, and media descendants under a date-bearing show instead of turning them into accidental independent shows.
- Covers layouts including Source 2 EXTRAS, Right Channel (Soundboard Feed), Open Mic Lithium, art-docs, Concert Pictures/Video, Disc 1/Flac, and Disc end tracks.

- Collapses non-compliant buckets that resolve to the same physical MAIN_DIR_PATH before Stage 3, preventing two mutation jobs from tagging or renaming the same show root (including direct-root audio plus D1/D2 layouts).

No-op mutation suppression
--------------------------
- Before writing audio tags, compares the exact normalized Artist, Album, Title, and Track Number with the current file.
- Skips the actual audio save when those written values already match and no stale TRACKTOTAL, DISCNUMBER, or DISCTOTAL metadata needs removal.
- Still writes when any intended value differs, when an existing written field has additional values that would be replaced, or when stale total/disc metadata must be cleared.
- Records matching files as TAG UNCHANGED while retaining them in the successful tagged-file count and adding an unchanged-file statistic.
- Makes the Rename Compliantly no-op guard explicit for Full Inventory and Add Shows so os.rename is not called when the effective compliant target is already the current folder path.

TLO v1.5 Build 430

Detailed re-inventory audit trail
---------------------------------

Sibling collection date boundary

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v431.txt =====
TLO v1.5 Build 431
==================

Destructive-path fail-closed safety
-----------------------------------
- Starts from and preserves the verified v1.5 Build 430 source baseline.
- Corruption classification now performs a real stat/read preflight and inspects chained Mutagen causes/contexts. Missing, locked, unreadable, disconnected, OSError/IOError, PermissionError, MemoryError, resource, or unexpected validator failures are UNVERIFIABLE rather than proven corrupt.
- Any unverifiable logical-show audio/snapshot evidence continues to suppress corruption-driven Trash and later rename/tag/copy-delete/SHN mutation for that group.
- A validator/format failure with no underlying infrastructure failure remains positively corrupt; the existing all-proven-corrupt and acceptable-percentage policies are unchanged.

Safety-critical input and duplicate validation
----------------------------------------------
- GUI and CLI acceptable-corruption-percent now use the same parse_percent_0_100 rule. Initial/default value remains 100; blank GUI input is invalid and cannot silently become 0.
- tlo-deleteDupes FLAC health remains tri-state: a missing/unavailable .flac is UNVERIFIABLE (None), while a real completed decoder failure may still prove corruption.

Trash and log hardening
-----------------------
- macOS osascript and Linux gio Trash subprocesses now use a finite 60-second timeout and verify that a reported-success source actually disappeared.
- Linux gio trash passes -- before the user-derived path; timeout/post-condition failures remain Trash failures with no permanent-delete fallback.
- Tokenized log delete, truncate, and path-prune operations share one alphanumeric token validator before constructing mutation paths.

Regression and platform-test safety
-----------------------------------
- Replaces dependency-shape-only corruption mocks with real-path-aware regressions and adds direct missing-FLAC plus Mutagen-wrapped filesystem-cause tests.
- Adds focused Build 431 tests for blank corruption input, duplicate missing-FLAC tri-state behavior, Trash timeout/argument/post-condition handling, and log-token validation.
- The Build 430 Ctrl+A GUI regression module now import-skips Tkinter cleanly on Tk-less hosts and carries the GUI marker.

Documentation and packaging
---------------------------
- Advances the application build from 430 to 431 while retaining public version 1.5.
- Updates the requirements and user manual with explicit CORRUPT versus UNVERIFIABLE semantics and destructive-operation safeguards.
- Adds acceptable corruption % to the canonical GUI control list.
- Retains the independently versioned GitHub Build Process at v075 without modification.
- Archives Build 430 change notes in old-change-logs.zip and regenerates the Build 431 SHA-256 manifest.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v432.txt =====
TLO v1.5 Build 432
==================

Iterative traversal
-------------------
- Starts from and preserves the verified v1.5 Build 431 source baseline.
- Phase 1 and Phase 2/3 directory discovery now use explicit iterative depth-first stacks rather than Python call-stack recursion.
- Existing sorted/extreme ordering, -ignoreDir/system/.tlo-collection pruning, inaccessible-path logging, representative media discovery, directory counts, and nested-show discovery semantics are preserved.
- Deep valid trees beyond Python's recursion limit no longer fail with RecursionError solely because of nesting depth.

Bounded GUI coordination
------------------------
- Worker-to-GUI prompt marshaling now waits at most 30 seconds for a queued Tk callback.
- If the GUI closes or the callback cannot be serviced, the worker raises the existing readable GUI-closed error instead of waiting indefinitely.

Bounded network metadata reads
------------------------------
- Adds tlo_network_io.py as the shared bounded-read helper for small API/metadata payloads.
- Successful setlist.fm, eTreeDB, and GitHub release-metadata responses are limited to 2 MiB before parsing.
- HTTP error bodies used for diagnostics are limited to 64 KiB; eTreeDB and setlist.fm user-facing detail is truncated to 500 characters.
- Oversized responses fail the lookup/update cleanly rather than being partially parsed or read without a ceiling.
- The existing streamed GitHub release-asset limit remains 1 GiB and is unaffected by the small-metadata bound.

Documentation, tests, and packaging
-----------------------------------
- Advances the application build from 431 to 432 while retaining public version 1.5.
- Updates requirements and manual for iterative deep-tree handling, finite GUI-thread waits, and bounded network metadata reads.
- Adds focused Build 432 deep-tree, finite-wait, and bounded-read regressions.
- Retains the independently versioned GitHub Build Process at v075 without modification.
- Archives Build 431 change notes in old-change-logs.zip and regenerates the Build 432 manifest.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v433.txt =====
TLO v1.5 Build 433
==================

Corruption decision architecture
--------------------------------
- Starts from and preserves the verified v1.5 Build 432 source baseline.
- Extracts corruption handling from the large Phase 2/3 search-path orchestrator into directly testable assessment and mutation stages in tlo_corruption.py.
- CorruptionAssessment records the audio snapshot, positively corrupt paths, unverifiable details, calculated percentage, acceptable threshold, proposed action, and fully corrupt music directories before any destructive mutation occurs.
- CorruptionOutcome records what was actually removed and whether corruption evaluation failed unexpectedly.
- Unexpected corruption-assessment/application failures fail closed as unverifiable; they do not authorize Trash/Recycle operations or later tag/rename mutation for the affected logical show.
- Keeps Build 431's rule that only positively verified corruption may authorize corruption-driven Trash behavior.

--------------------------
- Moves current normative behavior out of the accumulated historical Build 398-432 notes into the owning requirements sections where practical.
- Converts Section 20 into supplemental current requirements plus a concise revision index rather than a chronological dumping ground.

- Records the intentionally retired Appendix C gap and corrects the GitHub Build Process compatibility reference to v074 and later.

GitHub Build Process separation / packaging correction
------------------------------------------------------
- The TLO application source bundle contains no GitHub Build Process artifacts.
- Run-TLO-GitHub-Build.ps1, Create-TLOArtifactSigningMetadata.ps1, GitHub Build Process requirements, and the complete process ZIP are distributed only with the separately versioned GitHub Build Process package.
- The separate build process may inject its own transient build helpers into the disposable build snapshot; those helpers are not source-bundle contents and are not application source artifacts.
- This is a packaging-policy correction to Build 433 and does not change the TLO application build number.

Documentation, tests, and packaging
-----------------------------------
- Advances the application build from 432 to 433 while retaining public version 1.5.
- Updates the application requirements and user manual for the extracted corruption architecture and consolidated current requirements.
- Updates the TLO requirements, source README, and tests to enforce strict source-bundle/build-process separation.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v442.txt =====
TLO v1.5 Build 442
==================

Corruption handling semantics
-----------------------------
- Replaces the single acceptable corruption percentage with two independent decisions:
  - Corrupt files: Keep and report / Delete corrupt files.
  - Folder removal: Never / 100% corrupt only / At threshold.
- Adds Folder corruption threshold (0-100), used only when Folder removal is At threshold.
- Folder removal is decided from the original pre-removal audio snapshot before any individual file is moved.
- Threshold comparison is at-or-above: equality qualifies for folder removal.
- If a folder is retained, the individual-file policy applies only to proven-corrupt files that remain.
- A failed folder Trash operation does not override the individual-file setting. Keep and report never silently becomes file deletion.
- Unverifiable paths continue to fail closed and suppress corruption-driven Trash and later destructive mutation.
- All corruption removal continues to use the OS Trash/Recycle Bin; there is no permanent-delete fallback.

Main Inventory GUI
------------------
- Adds a boxed Corruption Handling group under the performance controls.
- Corrupt files choices: Keep and report; Delete corrupt files.
- Folder removal choices: Never; 100% corrupt only; At threshold.
- Folder corruption threshold remains visible but is disabled unless Folder removal is At threshold.
- Keeps the four-column checkbox grid in its existing internal layout to the right of the performance/corruption controls.

CLI
---
- Removes --acceptable-corruption-percent.
- Adds --corrupt-files {keep,delete}; default delete.
- Adds --corrupt-folders {never,all,threshold}; default all.
- Adds --corrupt-folder-threshold PERCENT (0-100).
- --corrupt-folders threshold requires an explicit --corrupt-folder-threshold.
- --corrupt-folder-threshold is rejected with --corrupt-folders never or all.

Testing and documentation
-------------------------
- Adds Build 442 regression coverage for the GUI controls, CLI validation, full file/folder policy matrix, threshold equality, per-folder threshold evaluation, report-only behavior, and folder-removal failure behavior.
- Updates the formal requirements and user manual to replace the superseded acceptable-corruption model.
- Archives Build 441 change notes in old-change-logs.zip.
- Public application version remains 1.5; application build advances from 441 to 442.
- The independently versioned GitHub Build Process remains separate from the TLO source bundle.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v449.txt =====
TLO v1.6 Build 449
==================

Artist/Album folder parsing
---------------------------
- Adds an artist-aware relaxed dash separator for Artist/Album folder names.
- Accepts Artist - Album, Artist- Album, Artist -Album, and Artist-Album when the complete text to the left of the first accepted dash resolves uniquely to one Artist DB master.
- Does not globally reinterpret hyphens. Unknown or ambiguous left-hand text does not enable the relaxed rule.
- Splits only the DB-confirmed artist delimiter and preserves every later dash in the album/collection title.
- Prevents undated collections such as Van Morrison-Astral Weeks Live from falling through to the generic Van Morrison xxxx-xx-xx identity.
- Applies the same DB-gated spacing tolerance to compliant Artist/Album parsing; ordinary strict spaced-dash behavior remains unchanged.

Regression coverage
-------------------
- Covers all 19 reported Van Morrison folders.
- Covers all four spacing variants around the Artist/Album dash.
- Verifies later album-title dashes are preserved.
- Verifies unknown left-hand text cannot trigger relaxed splitting.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v457.txt =====
TLO v1.6 Build 457

Same-month multi-day performance dates
- Adds canonical recognition of yyyy-mm-dd followed by one or two trailing same-month day values.
- Supported numeric examples include 1970-04-12-13, 1970-04-12-13-14, 1970-04-12,13, and 1970-04-12,13,14.
- Supports textual equivalents including 12-13 April 1970, 12-13-14 April 1970, April 12-13, 1970, and 1970 April 12-13.
- Every day in the range must be a real calendar date and day numbers must increase strictly from left to right.
- Descending, duplicate, impossible, or malformed multi-day ranges are rejected as a whole so TLO does not silently accept only the leading date.
- Uses the first day as the canonical TLO Date and retains the complete original multi-day source range in PARENTHETICALS.
- Applies through the shared parser used by Full Inventory, Compliant and Non-compliant matching, Add Shows/Updater, and Research.

Regression protection
- Existing two- and four-digit year-range rules are unchanged, including 96-98, 1996-97-98, and 1996-1998 behavior.
- Existing separator, partial-date, compact-date, slash-date, textual-date, calendar-validity, artist, venue, tagging, duplicate, collection, corruption, and reversal rules remain unchanged.
- Adds Build 457 behavior tests for valid numeric/textual ranges, one- and two-day suffixes, invalid ordering/calendar dates, leap-year dates, Compliant/Non-compliant extraction, Add Shows, Research, parenthetical retention, and existing year-range regressions.

Packaging and documentation
- Updates application/test build metadata, User Manual, FAQ, requirements, source README, change log, and checksums to Build 457.
- Archives the Build 456 change note in old-change-logs.zip.
- No GitHub Build Process change is required; process v086 already applies to Build 456 and later and has no date-parser coupling.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v460.txt =====
TLO v1.6 Build 460

Guarded Artist + Place + Date + technical-suffix path fallback
- Adds a narrow non-compliant fallback for traded-show folder components such as:
    RTF Paris 7 March 76 flac16
- The artist prefix must resolve directly and uniquely through artists.sqlite. Existing DB aliases/abbreviations are eligible; TLO does not construct or infer an initialism from another artist name.
- The date is recognized by the existing date parser, preserving all existing date validation and normalization rules.
- Everything after the date must consist entirely of recognized media/source technical tokens (for example flac/flac16/flac24, shn, wav, 16-bit/24-bit, SBD, AUD, FM, matrix, soundboard, or audience). Ordinary title text after the date does not activate this fallback.
- Text between Artist and Date is treated conservatively as a venue/location hint:
  - normal state/country-anchored String2 parsing remains authoritative;
  - an exact venues.txt match may be used as Venue;
  - otherwise only one unanchored word may survive as a low-confidence final location-text fallback;
  - unknown multi-word text is not promoted to venue/location metadata.
- The one-word hint is deferred until after eTreeDB, selected setlist metadata, and setlist.fm so it cannot block stronger venue/location metadata.

Regression protection
- Adds Build 460 tests for the RTF/Paris example, direct DB-alias requirement, no initialism synthesis, exact venues.txt venue handling, structured location handling, unknown multi-word rejection, technical-tail gating, and stronger-online-source precedence.
- Adds both Build 459 and Build 460 behavior modules to the GitHub-compatible test entry point so the prior standalone/GUI Tag control parity remains covered there.
- Existing String1 Date String2, Date Artist Venue Location, artist-subdirectory, date, wrapper, album/collection, online lookup, and venue/location precedence rules remain unchanged except for this new guarded fallback.

Documentation/package
- Updates requirements, User Manual, FAQ, source README, version metadata, current-document tests, and package checksums to Build 460.
- Archives Build 459 change notes into old-change-logs.zip.
- The TLO GitHub Build Process remains independently versioned at v088 and is not included in the application source bundle.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v461.txt =====
TLO v1.6 Build 461

Required Search Path input and reusable text control files
- Full Inventory no longer reads TLOHome/toBeInventoried.txt automatically. Search Path is now the required inventory-root input for GUI Inventory and the tlo-gi command line.
- Search Path accepts multiple entries separated by semicolons. Each direct entry uses the same established inventory-control grammar formerly used on a toBeInventoried.txt line: optional [Volume] prefix plus optional --$slam, --$copy, or --$copy-delete directives. Legacy -$slam remains accepted by text control files.
- A Search Path entry whose physical path ends in .txt is treated as an inventory-control text file. The file may have any filename and is parsed exactly like the former toBeInventoried.txt input: blank/#/REM comment handling, one root per line, [Volume], Slam, Copy, and Copy/Delete behavior are retained.
- Search Path may mix direct entries and .txt control-file entries in one semicolon-delimited value.
- Quoted Search Path entries may contain a literal semicolon without being split.
- The separate Slam field/--$slam remains compatible with direct Search Path entries. It is rejected when a .txt control-file entry is present so per-root directives remain unambiguous inside the file. Inline and separate duplicate Slam/copy directives are also rejected as ambiguous.
- Existing duplicate/overlap checks, accessibility checks, volume-label handling, capacity checks, worker scheduling, inventory ownership, and per-path copy semantics remain unchanged after Search Path expansion.

Main Inventory window
- Search Path is now labeled simply "Search Path"; the former "(optional/override)" text is removed.
- The Slam text box is reduced from width 66 to width 33 and no longer stretches across both right-hand columns, making it approximately half its prior width.
- Search Path itself retains the existing compact width and Windows folder drag/drop behavior.

Template and packaging
- toBeInventoried.txt remains part of COMPLETE installations only as an example/template. TLO never auto-loads it from TLOHome.
- The companion GitHub Build Process advances independently to v089 so COMPLETE-package README/template text explains that users must explicitly point Search Path at toBeInventoried.txt (or any other .txt control file) to use the file format.
- UPDATE-package protected-state behavior is unchanged: toBeInventoried.txt is still not shipped in UPDATE ZIPs.

Regression protection
- Adds Build 461 behavior coverage for required Search Path, semicolon-delimited roots, per-entry directives, arbitrary .txt filenames, mixed direct/file inputs, quoted semicolons, CLI-required Search Path, no automatic TLOHome template input, preview expansion, runtime loading, and the revised GUI label/Slam width.
- Historical GUI and inline-validation tests whose old requirements were explicitly superseded are updated while preserving their still-relevant compaction and validation contracts.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v462.txt =====
TLO v1.6 Build 462

Cumulative Search Path drag/drop
- Native-Windows Search Path drag/drop now appends to the existing Search Path value instead of replacing it.
- One drag action may contain multiple folders and/or .txt control files; each dropped item is appended in payload order with semicolons inserted automatically.
- Repeated drag actions remain cumulative. For example: drag folder A, then folder B, then folders C and D together -> A;B;C;D.
- Existing Search Path text is preserved when later drag actions append more entries.
- Dropped .txt control files retain Build 461 behavior and remain explicit control-file entries. Other dropped files retain the legacy behavior of contributing their containing folder.
- A dropped path containing a literal semicolon is quoted automatically so the Search Path parser treats it as one path rather than multiple entries.
- Tagging Path drag/drop is unchanged and continues to replace that single-path field.

Regression protection
- Adds Build 462 behavior coverage for cumulative single-folder drags, multi-folder payloads, mixed folder/.txt accumulation, and literal-semicolon quoting.
- Preserves Build 461 required Search Path/control-file behavior and all prior inventory semantics.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v464.txt =====
TLO v1.6 Build 464

Interrupted sibling-collection recovery
- Fixes the recovery failure that could occur after forcing Quit during sibling-collection aggregation when the completed recovery container occupied the same pathname as one of the original member folders. This is the duplicated-path case such as:
  e:\boots\Pink Floyd - Early Flights\Pink Floyd - Early Flights
- Before restoring members in that collision state, TLO now atomically moves the outer recovery container to a unique sibling recovery path. That frees the original pathname so every staged child can be restored to the exact original sibling location recorded in the journal.
- Successful automatic recovery is reported in the main console and Inventory continues instead of leaving the user to repair the tree manually.
- Recovery remains fail-closed: TLO never guesses which copy is authoritative when both an original and staged copy exist or when a journaled member is missing from both places.

Recovery diagnostics and safety
- When recovery cannot complete, the user-visible error now reports the exact recovery container, journal path, planned final folder when available, and every journaled member's original path, staged path, and current state.
- States explicitly distinguish ORIGINAL_ONLY, STAGED_ONLY, BOTH, MISSING, and the special case where the recovery container occupies an original member pathname.
- An unreadable recovery journal now blocks normal Inventory regardless of whether the journal is still in a hidden temporary folder or has already reached the final collection folder.
- An orphaned .tlo-collection-* temporary folder without a journal now blocks normal Inventory rather than being silently traversed.
- Dry Run remains read-only, but its diagnostic shows the same path/state evidence and tells the user to run a normal Inventory for automatic recovery.

Quit behavior
- The late-stage Quit confirmation now explains that forcing exit during cleanup or collection aggregation can interrupt a collection move and that the next normal Inventory run will attempt journal-based rollback.
- The existing ability to force exit is retained; the recovery journal remains the crash/interruption safety mechanism.

Regression protection
- Adds Build 464 tests reproducing the exact final-container/original-member pathname collision, detailed ambiguous-state diagnostics, unreadable-final-journal handling, orphan temporary-folder handling, and Dry Run recovery guidance.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v465.txt =====
TLO v1.6 Build 465

Cross-runtime interrupted sibling-collection recovery
- Fixes the remaining recovery failure seen when an interrupted sibling-collection journal was written by native Windows with paths such as E:\boots\... and the next Inventory run is performed under WSL where the same tree is /mnt/e/boots/....
- Recovery now translates journaled Windows drive-rooted paths to the equivalent WSL /mnt/<drive>/ path before validation and rollback when running under WSL/Linux.
- Recovery also understands the reverse representation, translating journaled /mnt/<drive>/ paths to the equivalent native Windows drive path when recovery runs on Windows.
- Path translation does not weaken recovery safety. The translated final path and every translated original member path must still validate as direct children of the actual recovery-container parent, and every child name must still match the journal before TLO moves anything.

Pink Floyd interrupted-state regression
- Adds an exact nine-member regression for Pink Floyd - Early Flights: the unsuffixed member plus alt1 through alt8 are all staged inside the colliding recovery container, while the journal records E:\boots\... originals and the live runtime tree is represented as /mnt/e/boots/....
- The regression proves TLO moves the colliding outer container aside, restores all nine original sibling folders, removes the journal/recovery container, reports successful recovery, and continues without manual folder surgery.

Diagnostics
- If cross-runtime recovery still cannot proceed, the error reports the current-runtime path and the original journal path form when they differ, in addition to the recovery container, journal, planned final path, staged path, and member state.
- Existing fail-closed handling for BOTH, MISSING, unreadable journals, and orphan .tlo-collection-* folders is unchanged.

Regression protection
- Adds Build 465 tests for Windows-to-WSL path translation, WSL-to-Windows path translation, the exact cross-runtime colliding-container rollback, and translated diagnostic output.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v466.txt =====
TLO v1.6 Build 466

Sibling collection topology
- Changes physical consolidation of validated alt-family sibling collections so TLO never creates a same-name parent/child path such as Pink Floyd - Early Flights/Pink Floyd - Early Flights.
- TLO continues to create a unique temporary sibling transaction folder at the same filesystem level as the collection members. For an alt family containing the unsuffixed base member, that member is staged inside the aggregate as "(alt0)" before alt1, alt2, and later alternatives.
- The completed aggregate retains the normal stripped shared release name, while the original member is therefore represented as Collection (alt0) instead of a child whose name exactly matches its parent.
- Existing D/CD/Disc/Part collection families retain their established child names and behavior.

Ordering and inventory paths
- alt0 is treated as collection member 0, so its tracks remain before alt1, alt2, and later alternates in continuous collection-wide numbering.
- Complete-path log entries for the unsuffixed original are rewritten to the new alt0 child path.
- Consolidated sibling-group recognition accepts alt0 as the original member without adding "alt0" to the collection-level metadata name.

Transactional safety and recovery
- New collection journals use schema 2 and record both each member's exact original basename and its staged basename.
- Rollback restores the staged alt0 member to its exact original unsuffixed sibling path if any move, generated-info write, or complete-path-log commit fails.
- Recovery remains backward compatible with schema-1 journals produced by Builds 464 and 465, including same-path container recovery and native-Windows/WSL path translation.

Regression protection
- Adds Build 466 tests for the no-Name/Name final layout, alt0 ordering/grouping, complete-path-log rewriting, schema-2 journal contents, and rollback to the exact original unsuffixed member name.


===== Historical record: CHANGES_v467.txt =====
TLO v1.6 Build 467

MP3 tagging
- Promotes MP3 to an explicitly supported first-class tagging path alongside FLAC.
- Standalone Tag, Full Inventory tagging, and Add Shows use the same Artist, Album, Track Number, and Title assignment flow for MP3 files as for FLAC files.
- MP3 corruption checks now use the MPEG parser directly instead of relying only on the generic Mutagen fallback.

Corruption handling
- MP3 files participate in the same pre-mutation corruption population as FLAC files.
- MP3 corruption contributes to the same logical-show and direct-folder corruption percentages and follows the same Corrupt files Keep/Delete policy and Folder removal Never/100%/threshold policy.
- Unverifiable MP3 reads remain fail-closed in the same way as FLAC and suppress corruption-driven mutation.

Delete extra tags
- Adds a new main-window checkbox labeled Delete extra tags in the far-right fourth-row checkbox cell. The label is displayed on two lines and defaults off.
- Adds the --delete-extra-tags command-line option to Inventory and standalone Tag.
- When selected, TLO removes all tag metadata except Artist, Album, Track Number, and Track Title on every file it tags.
- MP3 cleanup removes unrelated ID3 frames including comments and embedded artwork. FLAC cleanup clears unrelated Vorbis-comment fields and embedded pictures. Other supported tag containers are cleared where the format exposes a writable tag container.
- When Delete extra tags is off, established behavior is unchanged: unrelated metadata is preserved except for the existing stale total/disc cleanup rules.
- The no-op write rule remains in force: if a file already contains exactly the four requested fields and no extra metadata, Delete extra tags does not force another save.

GUI, review, and logging
- Review Operation reports Delete extra tags immediately before Dry run, while Dry run remains the final reviewed checkbox value.
- Inventory, Tag, and Add Shows inherit the same current main-window Delete extra tags value.
- Per-show Switches logging and standalone Tag startup logging report the selected cleanup state.
- Refreshes the main-window manual screenshot and documents MP3/corruption/tag-cleanup behavior.

Regression protection
- Adds Build 467 tests for MP3 tagging preservation, four-field cleanup, no-op cleanup detection, explicit MP3 corruption validation, mixed FLAC/MP3 corruption percentages, checkbox placement/review order, and shared tag-writer propagation.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v471.txt =====
TLO v1.6 Build 471

Rename Compliantly exact-case correction
- Rename Compliantly now compares the final intended folder path using exact spelling and capitalization instead of a case-folded Windows path comparison.
- Canonical artist capitalization is therefore applied even when capitalization is the only change, for example `dave van ronk 1978-02-17 Main Point Bryn Mawr, PA` -> `Dave Van Ronk 1978-02-17 Main Point Bryn Mawr, PA`.
- On case-insensitive filesystems, when the differently-cased target spelling is the same existing directory entry, TLO renames through a unique temporary sibling and then to the exact compliant spelling.
- The same-entry case-only rename is not treated as a name collision and does not allocate `(altN)`, `(copyN)`, or Add Shows `_N` suffixes.
- If the final hop of a case-only rename fails, TLO makes a best-effort rollback to the original folder spelling and reports the rename failure.
- Full Inventory/GUI Tag/standalone Tag and Add Shows use the same shared exact-case behavior.

Regression/documentation
- Adds Build 471 behavior coverage for capitalization-only rename detection, the temporary case-only rename hop, inventory/tag rename behavior, and Add Shows rename behavior.
- Updates the User Manual, requirements, FAQ, source README, change log, and checksum manifest for Build 471.
- Carries forward all Build 470 behavior unless superseded above.

Auto Update confirmation

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v473.txt =====
TLO v1.6 Build 473

Unknown-date collection/show descriptor fallback
- Adds a separate DESCRIPTOR and DESCRIPTOR_SOURCE for material whose final Date is exactly xxxx-xx-xx and whose Venue and Location are both blank.
- This is not venue inference. Venue, City, Region, Country, and Location remain blank.
- Descriptor evidence is ranked conservatively from the selected/local setlist header region, descriptive setlist filename, and cleaned main-directory residue.
- Highest-confidence sources include explicit Collection/Release/Bootleg/Event/Session/Broadcast/Program/Show/Recording/Descriptor labels; strong broadcast/session/rehearsal/soundcheck/demo/collection/archive-style headers; quoted/decorated titles; and concise title-like headers separated from the track list.
- Descriptive setlist filenames and cleaned directory residue are lower-priority fallbacks.
- Setlist header scanning stops at track-list/checksum boundaries. Artist/date-only lines, technical lineage, disc/CD/set labels, generic housekeeping text, sentence prose, and track-like rows are rejected.
- The automatic threshold is 60 and descriptors are capped at 60 characters.
- When accepted, the descriptor is appended after Artist xxxx-xx-xx in SHOW_NAME and is used consistently in generated setlist filenames.
- If no safe descriptor exists, established Artist xxxx-xx-xx behavior is unchanged.

Regression/documentation
- Adds Build 473 tests for explicit collection headers, broadcast/session headers, quoted release titles, safe title-header gating, technical-lineage rejection, filename fallback, directory-residue fallback, known-date/location gating, metadata logging, postprocess preservation, and generated setlist naming.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v474.txt =====
TLO v1.6 Build 474

Main Inventory GUI
- Renames the main action button from "Add Shows (incremental)" to the two-line label "Add New Shows"; the associated Add Shows/Updater workflow is unchanged.

Each entry uses the same optional [Volume], --$slam, --$copy, and --$copy-delete grammar as one toBeInventoried.txt line. A .txt control-file path remains valid. Semicolons remain accepted internally for backward compatibility.
- Native-Windows Path(s) drag/drop now appends comma-separated entries and automatically quotes a dropped path containing a comma.

Manual Tweaks / Manual Updates
- Adds a Manual Updates child window with the standard TLO header, Original (to be edited), New Name, Save, and Quit controls.
- Original accepts one folder or one .txt control file by typing/paste or native-Windows drag/drop. New Name accepts typing/paste and folder drag/drop; a dropped folder contributes only its leaf folder name.
- When Original is a .txt file, New Name is disabled. Blank lines, # comments, and REM comments follow the same comment rules as toBeInventoried.txt. Every data line must be exactly Path ----> Folder Name.
- Save for one folder renames the original folder in place to the requested leaf when needed. If the original path has already been renamed to that leaf in the same parent, TLO accepts the existing state.
- Manual Save removes the old bootlist identity and exported TLOHome/setlists file(s), re-reads the renamed folder for setlist/metadata content, and adds the new inventory identity using the user-entered New Name as the authoritative Show name.
- The existing bootlist volume/path structure is preserved; only the original leaf folder name is replaced with the new leaf. Ambiguous leaf-only inventory matches and destination collisions fail closed.
- Batch .txt Manual Updates process each valid Path ----> Folder Name line independently and report per-line failures without hiding successful updates.

Regression/documentation
- Adds Build 474 tests for comma Path(s) parsing, comma-safe drag/drop quoting, removal of the Slam GUI control, Add New Shows/Manual Tweaks button contracts, Manual Updates control-file parsing, in-place rename/inventory/setlist replacement, and already-renamed recovery.

Manual Tweaks / Manual Updates
- Advances the public application version to v1.7 while continuing the existing sequential build numbering at Build 475.

- If the final folder is missing or the update fails, TLO restores/retains the prior inventory and unresolved-show state rather than silently losing the unidentified entry.
- Manual Updates .txt batches preflight unidentified-show lines and collect every required destination Path before applying changes. Canceling a required destination prompt aborts the batch before modifications begin.
- Ambiguous leaf-only unidentified-show matches fail closed.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v479.txt =====
TLO v1.7 Build 479

Stabilization / data integrity
- Fixes GUI Tag completion: is_cancel_requested is imported, completion/issue reporting runs, progress cleanup finishes, and the main action states are recalculated immediately.
- Manual Updates now compares physical paths to canonical rootless bootlist paths and will not leaf-match to a different volume.
- Manual Updates preserves an old exported setlist family when another surviving bootlist row still uses that Show identity.
- Unidentified-show recovery writes the canonical [Volume] rootless-path VolumePath format and uses canonical path comparison for duplicate detection.
- bootlist.csv writers now use same-directory temporary files, flush/fsync, and os.replace. unidentifiedShows.txt removal/restoration uses atomic replacement as well.

Manual Updates / Update Tags
- A parseable New Name is authoritative for corrected Artist/Album tag identity before Update Tags runs. Date-form names use Artist before the date and Album text beginning with the date; Artist - Album names use the two sides directly.
- Manual Updates disables Convert shn during Update Tags. It never converts or deletes SHN/SHNF originals; conversion remains an explicit Tag/Inventory workflow.
- Tag writes occur before inventory finalization and are not rolled back. If a later inventory commit fails, the error explicitly warns that tag changes may already have been applied.

Online metadata / capitalization
- setlist.fm fuzzy same-date results from a different normalized artist are ignored rather than supplying venue/location/setlist metadata.
- Case-only online corroboration now improves only lower/all-uppercase local text when the online spelling provides useful mixed case. Already-good mixed case is preserved, including against all-caps online spellings.
- Location is rebuilt from corrected City/Region/Country components when the existing Location was derived from those components.

Dates and Path(s) parsing
- Definite future dates are rejected. Two-digit years use the most recent century interpretation that is not in the future; in 2026, 00-26 map to 2000-2026 and 27-99 map to 1927-1999.
- A drag/dropped Path(s) value containing a literal semicolon is automatically wrapped in matching double quotes so it remains one entry.
- Matching wrapping single quotes are no longer stripped by shared path-input helpers; apostrophes and single quotes are literal input.

Tests / documentation
- Adds Build 479 behavior coverage for GUI Tag completion, canonical Manual Updates matching, cross-volume protection, shared setlist preservation, canonical unidentified recovery, wrong-artist setlist.fm rejection, capitalization safety, future dates, semicolon drag/drop, literal single quotes, and Manual Update tag identity/SHN protection.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v481.txt =====
TLO v1.7 Build 481

Cybersecurity hardening
- Encodes control characters in machine-parsed metadata, group, conflict, tag-success, and tag-error log output so untrusted tag/remote text cannot inject additional records or log instructions.
- Metadata-log readers reject duplicate single-valued identity keys. Postprocess fallback setlist sources must be local, non-symlink regular files inside the show parent tree; /proc, /dev, /sys, UNC, and device paths are refused.
- Sibling-collection recovery journals are capped at 1 MiB. Production automatic recovery now requires a matching per-operation nonce registry entry under TLOHome/logs/sibling-recovery. generated_info is restricted to info.txt and staged/member names . and .. are invalid. Unregistered journals stop without mutation.
- Windows deleteBackupFolders.bat doubles literal percent signs and starts with setlocal DisableDelayedExpansion.
- Full setlist reads use the shared 16 MiB bounded reader; oversized ordinary-text candidates are excluded before selection. The duplicate-review text viewer samples at most 1 MiB.
- Sibling setlist aggregation ignores symlinked files. Tag Copy refuses source trees containing symbolic links instead of dereferencing targets.
- SHN conversion rejects symlink sources and existing/dangling-link FLAC targets and uses an OS-allocated same-directory temporary FLAC path.
- Issues Open Path refuses UNC/device paths and reveals local files via their containing folder instead of launching the file.
- bootlist.csv prefixes spreadsheet-dangerous leading characters (=, +, -, @, tab, CR, LF) during serialization and removes only TLO's protective prefix when reading.

Additional local hardening
- Compliant folder naming avoids Windows reserved device names such as CON, NUL, AUX, PRN, COM1-COM9, and LPT1-LPT9.
- GitHub updater redirect validation no longer accepts arbitrary *.githubusercontent.com hosts; only explicit GitHub release/download hosts are allowed. This is an application-side allowlist change only. No GitHub Build Process or release-signing changes are included.
- macOS/Linux helper launches use fixed/absolute resolved executable paths where changed by this build.

Tests / documentation
- Adds Build 481 security regressions for log injection, recovery-journal authentication, delete-script escaping, bounded reads, symlink refusal, SHN symlink safety, issue-path safety, spreadsheet escaping, reserved folder names, and GitHub redirect host narrowing.
- Updates requirements, User Manual, FAQ, source README, versioning, change log, tests, and checksum manifest for v1.7 Build 481.
- Archives CHANGES_v480.txt in old-change-logs.zip.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v485.txt =====
TLO v1.7 Build 485

Persistent Copy Requests
- Adds Copy Requests to the main Inventory GUI for copying a requested subset of the inventory over multiple sessions and changing sets of connected source volumes.
- New requests use one .txt Request File plus one Destination; Browse is supported everywhere and native-Windows drag/drop is supported for both fields.
- Request files use the toBeInventoried.txt blank/#/REM comment convention and accept complete Show Name, Artist, Artist + yyyy, Artist + yyyy-mm-dd, and Artist + yyyy-yyyy/yy-yy requests.
- Artist matching uses Artist DB masters/aliases; exact Show Name matching has precedence. yy-yy ranges move forward across a century when needed (99-01 = 1999-2001).
- Requests operate on unique Show identities. Overlapping request lines and duplicate inventory copies do not create duplicate transfers.
- Persistent request state lives under TLOHome/copyRequests and survives application exits and drive swaps. The same list/destination resumes the same request; different lists or destinations stay independent.
- Continue re-evaluates the current inventory and connected volumes. New inventory can reactivate a previously Complete broad Artist/range request.
- Changed source request files prompt for Update Request versus Continue with saved request; a missing original file can continue from the saved copy.
- Preview separates Available, Waiting For Volumes, stale/missing connected sources, unmatched items, and invalid items, and ranks remaining source volumes by how many outstanding Shows they can satisfy.
- Before copying, TLO performs an all-or-nothing free-space check for the entire current pass. Insufficient space copies nothing for that request.
- Copying preserves the source folder name/content, never changes the source, rejects symlinked source trees, copies to a temporary destination, verifies the recursive tree exactly, then atomically promotes the verified folder.
- Non-identical destination collisions are never overwritten/merged. A byte-identical folder already placed at the same destination can satisfy a second request without recopying.
- Adds Process All Active Requests, View Report, Close Request, and Delete Request. The terminal user-chosen state is Closed. Delete Request never deletes copied music.
- Each request maintains cumulative copied.txt, current pending.txt/failed.txt, latest-report.txt, timestamped report history, saved request text, and atomic JSON state.

Tests / documentation / packaging
- Adds Build 485 behavior coverage for mixed request types, Artist aliases, century-wrap ranges, multi-pass drive swaps, duplicate/overlap handling, later inventory discovery, free-space preflight, same-destination satisfaction, request updates, Closed state, reports, and GUI exposure.
- Updates requirements, User Manual, FAQ, version stamps, change notes, and source-bundle metadata.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v504.txt =====
TLO v1.7 Build 504

GUI option-state and setlist.fm upgrade environment detection

- When Tag in Place, Tag Copy, and Tag Copy/Delete Original are all off, Delete Extra Tags is now forced unchecked as well as disabled/greyed.
- The three tagging modes remain mutually exclusive; selecting any one enables Delete Extra Tags again but does not re-check it automatically.
- setlist.fm upgrade environment detection still requires an explicit upgrade-enable variable, but now accepts both SETLISTFMUPGRADE_API_KEY and SETLISTFM_UPGRADE_API_KEY.
- On Windows, TLO checks the current persisted User and System environment values when the launching process inherited a stale environment snapshot, so variables added through Environment Variables or setx are recognized without exposing the API key.
- Normal setlist.fm still requires SETLISTFM_API_KEY, and the upgrade value is expected to contain the same API key.

Tests / documentation / packaging
- Adds Build 504 behavior coverage for Delete Extra Tags clearing, upgrade-variable alias detection, persistent Windows environment fallback, and GUI upgrade enablement.
- Updates requirements, User Manual, FAQ, version stamps, source-bundle metadata, and current-release contracts to v1.7 Build 504.

TLO v1.7 Build 503

Copy Requests direct path/volume items

- A Copy Request item that is a rooted filesystem path, visible volume label, [Volume], or [Volume] path is treated as a direct tree copy rather than Artist/Show matching text.
- Direct path items copy the named directory tree to the selected Destination using the source leaf name; whole-volume items use the requested volume label as the Destination folder name.
- Direct items participate in the same whole-pass free-space preflight and temporary-copy/tree-verification/finalize safety flow as ordinary Copy Request folders.
- Every inventoried Show whose source folder is fully contained in a successful direct copy is recorded in normal completed-Show tracking. Mixed direct and Artist/date/Show request lines are therefore de-conflicted in the same pass and on future Continue passes.
- The direct item itself has persistent completion state so an already completed path/volume item is not recopied on resume.
- Unavailable direct paths/volumes wait for accessibility instead of being interpreted as Artist names.
- Copy Request preview/reporting includes direct-item availability, waiting, completion, copied history, and failures.

Tests / documentation / packaging
- Adds Build 503 behavior coverage for absolute paths, volume labels, [Volume] path syntax, same-pass de-confliction, persistent resume tracking, unavailable-path waiting, and preview/free-space integration.
- Updates Section 14.6, User Manual, FAQ, version stamps, and current-release contracts to v1.7 Build 503.

- Wire --proper-grammar through the real tlo-gi, tlo-ggi, and tlo-tag parsers/configuration. The unreleased --old-grammar spelling remains unsupported.

- Backup Alert names the actual .bat/.sh file.
- Defensive missing/invalid corruption configuration fails closed to keep/never/100 without changing configured Full Inventory defaults.
- Reserve copy/move destinations atomically without overwriting existing directories; retain a verified destination and give source/destination recovery guidance if source cleanup later fails.
- Generated-setlist filename reuse now requires SHA-256 content identity; equal-size different-content setlists receive (altN).
- Valid Artist DB matches are not discarded merely because an Artist/AlbumArtist value contains generic unknown/track marker text.
- Add behavior tests through real parsers, GUI callback, multiprocessing limiter, transfer paths, and setlist export.
- Reconcile the owning requirements sections for the Build 500 review and add functional requirements for Manual Updates, Copy Requests, Redundancy Groups, tlo-gsi, search-artist-db, and Delete Extra Tags availability.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v505.txt =====
TLO v1.7 Build 505

Targeted review remediation and Copy Request safety

Metadata / text decoding
- Setlist header metadata and date-fallback text now use the shared bounded text reader rather than independent UTF-16-first decoders.
- cp1252/latin-1 setlists no longer decode as UTF-16 mojibake merely because their byte length is even.
- Date fallback reads selected RTF and DOCX through the shared format-aware reader instead of treating container/control bytes as plain text.
- Scored decoder call sites used by setlist selection and sibling collection handling now converge on the same byte decoder.

Copy and verification safety
- Tree verification and verification-related size/path walks now fail closed when a subtree cannot be enumerated.
- Copy Requests reject Destination/source overlap in either direction, including Destination equal to the source. Source-as-target is never recorded as Already Satisfied/Complete.
- Whole-volume Copy Requests consistently exclude Windows System Volume Information and $RECYCLE.BIN plus macOS .Spotlight-V100, .fseventsd, .Trashes, and .TemporaryItems from sizing, copying, and verification; Preview/reporting identifies the exclusions.
- A bare volume label that also matches an Artist/alias or exact Show is ambiguous and is rejected with guidance to use [Volume].
- Copy Request destinations are reserved before copying rather than finalized by a check-then-replace race. Stale legacy .tlo-copy-* temporary folders are swept at pass start.
- Direct-source Show coverage filters lexical path candidates before filesystem probes.

setlist.fm / GUI hardening
- setlist.fm upgrade availability now requires SETLISTFMUPGRADE_API_KEY or SETLISTFM_UPGRADE_API_KEY to exactly match SETLISTFM_API_KEY. Key values are never logged.
- --setlistfm-upgrade is inert when --setlistfm-lookup is off rather than raising an unnecessary CLI validation error.
- On Windows, current persisted User/System environment values take precedence over a stale inherited process value; expandable registry values are expanded.
- Delete Extra Tags is also gated in config construction so future programmatic paths cannot leave it active without a tagging mode.
- setlist.fm rate-limit constants are shared instead of copied into multiple GUI/tag configuration builders.

Requirements / manual / FAQ / packaging
- Reconciles the v504 review findings R1-R7 and U1-U4: Section 20 now precedes the appendices, normative rules no longer carry build-history narration, recent manual notes are chronological, Copy Request hazards are documented, and Delete Extra Tags capitalization is consistent.
- The source bundle no longer ships superseded SHA256SUMS_v500-v504.txt or BUILD_VERIFICATION_v500-v504.txt at its root; those prior verification artifacts are archived in old-change-logs.zip.
- Adds executable Build 505 regressions derived from the supplied v504 probes, including permission-error verification, cp1252/RTF/DOCX decoding, overlap/self-copy, Artist/volume collisions, system-folder exclusions, and upgrade-key mismatch.

===== Historical record: CHANGES_v506.txt =====
TLO v1.7 Build 506

v505 review remediation and Copy Request recovery safety

Release blockers
- Fixes the Delete Extra Tags config-builder NameError that prevented Full Inventory, GUI Tag, Add New Shows, and Manual Updates from starting when Delete Extra Tags was checked.
- Fixes Copy Request destination-reservation cleanup so a directory that appears before reservation is never treated as TLO-owned and is never recursively deleted.

Copy Request resilience
- Restores copy-to-temporary, verify, then publish behavior. The final Show/volume folder name never contains a partial copied tree.
- Uses an empty final-name reservation only as a lock; cleanup may remove that reservation only while it is still empty and never recursively deletes the final target.
- Marks TLO-created .tlo-copy-* temporary trees and sweeps only marked stale trees on later passes, leaving similarly named user folders alone.
- Applies Windows/macOS volume-root OS-folder exclusions when a whole volume is requested either by volume syntax or by a direct path that resolves to the connected volume root.

Setlist text decoding / RTF
- Preserves valid UTF-8 spans in mixed UTF-8/cp1252 files while decoding only invalid legacy bytes through cp1252/latin-1.
- Handles bounded samples that end inside a UTF-8 multibyte character without turning the entire sample into mojibake.
- Detects BOM-less UTF-16 LE/BE from NUL placement before UTF-8 decoding.
- Decodes RTF hexadecimal and Unicode character escapes before removing control words so accented artist/venue/location text is retained.

setlist.fm / GUI
- Restores normal environment precedence: explicit nonblank process/session values override persisted Windows values; persisted User/System values are fallback only when the corresponding process value is missing/blank.
- The main GUI now explains whether setlist.fm is disabled for a missing normal key, missing upgrade key, or upgrade-key mismatch without displaying key values.

Verification / maintenance
- Converts the supplied v505 probes into permanent Build 506 behavioral regressions, including all three tag modes, foreign-directory race protection, temp-copy recovery, safe stale-temp ownership, both volume-root syntaxes, decoder edge cases, RTF accents, environment precedence, and upgrade-gate status.
- Deep-tree tests now clean their 1,050-level fixtures iteratively so repeated local pytest runs do not leave recursive cleanup hazards.
- The unused legacy TaggerWindow remains in place by explicit requirement; this build does not remove it.
