===== Historical record: CHANGES_v520.txt =====
TLO v1.7 Build 520
===================
- Fixes tlo-search filename resolution for supported partial dates such as 1973-12-xx so the internal date dashes are preserved.
- tlo-search now calls the shared inventory/postprocess setlist filename creator directly rather than independently assembling the .txt filename.
- Adds regression coverage that compares Search output to the inventory record-based filename result and covers the supported partial-date placeholder forms.
- Carries forward Build 519 application renames and all prior behavior unchanged.

TLO v1.7 Build 519
===================
- Renames the Main GUI source/command from tlo-ggi to tlo-main. Platform artifacts become tlo-main.exe on Windows, tlo-main on Linux/macOS, and tlo-main.app on macOS.
- Renames the collection Search source/command from tlo-gsi to tlo-search. Platform artifacts become tlo-search.exe on Windows, tlo-search on Linux/macOS, and tlo-search.app on macOS.
- Updates help text, dialog identifiers, user documentation, tests, Linux/macOS/Windows distribution scripts, release assembly expectations, protected package-file manifests, and GitHub build/release validation to use the new names.
- No compatibility alias executables are shipped; the new names replace the old application names.
- Carries forward Build 518 main-icon packaging and all prior runtime behavior.

TLO v1.7 Build 518
===================
- Replaces the packaged inventory-app icon artwork with the supplied TLO Main artwork while preserving the existing tlo-inventory-icon asset names and build-script expectations for minimal disruption.
- Regenerates the packaged inventory icon resources in all required forms: icons/tlo-inventory-icon.png, icons/tlo-inventory-icon.ico, and icons/tlo-inventory-icon.icns.
- Adds icons/tlo-main-icon.png as the supplied source artwork while retaining the existing inventory-icon filenames for build compatibility.
- Keeps the Windows .ico resource DIB/BMP-based so the existing Windows packaging and icon-verification rules continue to pass unchanged.
- Carries forward Build 517 source-publication hygiene, Build 516 update-check ordering, Build 515 setlist filename normalization, and the Build 513 independently signed update trust model.
- Updates requirements, User Manual, source metadata, tests, and verification references to Build 518.


TLO v1.7 Build 516
===================
- Fixes Check for updates so the GitHub release build number is compared with the installed build before independently signed metadata is required.
- Older or equal GitHub releases, including pre-signing releases such as Build 512 when Build 514 or later is installed, are reported as not newer instead of producing a signing error.
- Independently signed metadata remains mandatory for every genuinely newer release before its asset name, size, SHA-256, build, package kind, and platform/layout are trusted.
- Adds regression coverage for older unsigned, equal unsigned, and newer unsigned release cases.
- Updates requirements, User Manual, FAQ, source metadata, tests, and verification references to Build 516.

TLO v1.7 Build 515
===================
- Aligns tlo-gsi setlist filename generation with the inventory/postprocess filename normalizer instead of maintaining a separate dash-preservation rule.
- Preserves hyphens in recognized date/date-range tokens while stripping ordinary punctuation elsewhere; `Bob 2015-01-02 Somewhere (pre-fm)` now resolves to `Bob2015-01-02Somewhere(prefm).txt`.
- Adds regression coverage proving search and inventory produce the same filename for trailing parenthetical qualifiers containing dashes.
- Updates requirements, User Manual, source metadata, tests, and verification references to Build 515.

TLO v1.7 Build 514
===================
- Restores the source-bundle/process-separation contract text and carried-forward historical change-log content that Build 513 packaging accidentally omitted.
- No runtime behavior change from Build 513; independently signed update metadata remains unchanged.
- Advances source, tests, requirements, User Manual, release metadata, and verification references to Build 514.

TLO v1.7 Build 513
===================
- Added independently signed update metadata. The built-in updater no longer trusts a SHA-256 digest supplied only by the GitHub Releases API.
- Added tlo_update_trust.py with a build-injected pinned RSA-3072 public key and pure-Python RSA PKCS#1 v1.5 SHA-256 signature verification.
- Update discovery now requires TLO_UPDATE_METADATA.json and TLO_UPDATE_METADATA.sig, verifies the signature first, then authorizes the selected ZIP only when signed name, size, SHA-256, build, package kind, and platform/layout match.
- Update checks fail closed when the public key is not pinned or signed metadata is missing, malformed, mismatched, or invalid.
- Updated requirements, User Manual, FAQ, tests, and build metadata for Build 513.
- GitHub Build Process v096 adds local key generation, public-key injection, and post-release local signing/re-upload support. The private update-signing key is never sent to GitHub Actions.

TLO v1.7 Build 512

Main GUI naming, mode-aware Max Workers, and Copy Request trailing-* folder expansion

- Changes the main GUI title bar from "TLO Inventory GUI" to "TLO Main GUI".
- Changes the bold main-window heading from "Traders Little Organizer™ Inventory App" to "Traders Little Organizer™ Main".
- Changes the automatic Max Workers value to follow the selected Performance Mode instead of always using the machine CPU count: gentle = 1; balanced = min(2, CPU count); fast = CPU count; extreme = min(4 x CPU count, 64), minimum 1.
- Selecting a different Performance Mode resets Max Workers to that mode's automatic ceiling; the field remains editable afterward for another positive ceiling.
- Adds one-time trailing-* expansion for direct Copy Request paths before copying starts.
- c:\TLO* expands to each immediate directory in c:\ whose name begins with TLO (possibly more than one directory).
- c:\TLO\* expands to each immediate child directory of c:\TLO and does not copy the c:\TLO directory itself.
- Wildcard matching selects directories only; regular files and symbolic-link directories are excluded.
- The same trailing-* expansion applies to direct path lines read from Copy Request .txt inputs.
- Wildcard parents must be accessible and at least one directory must match when a new request is created.
- Expanded wildcard paths are persisted in the Copy Request snapshot, so later Preview/Continue/Process All operations do not re-expand or require the original wildcard source to remain unchanged.
- Adds Build 512 regression coverage and updates Requirements, User Manual, FAQ, release metadata, and current-version contracts.

TLO v1.7 Build 511

Copy Request Path(s) mixed input and persistent aggregation

- Renames the New Copy Request input label from Request File to Path(s).
- Uses the same semicolon-separated entry and optional matching-double-quote rules as the main Inventory Path(s) field.
- Allows one Copy Request Path(s) expression to mix direct filesystem paths/volumes, ordinary Artist/date/Show request text, and .txt request-list files.
- Native-Windows drag/drop accepts one or more folders/.txt files and appends later drops instead of replacing the existing Path(s) value.
- Browse .txt can append one or more request-list files to the current Path(s) value.
- Expands each .txt request file immediately when a new request is created, using the same blank-line/#/REM whole-line comment convention as toBeInventoried.txt.
- Aggregates expanded .txt items with direct Path(s) items in input order before Preview/copy processing begins.
- Persists the expanded aggregate under TLOHome/copyRequests; Continue, Process All, Preview, and resume use the saved aggregate and do not require the contributing .txt files to remain available or unchanged.
- Determines an existing Path(s)-based request identity from the Path(s) expression plus Destination before opening any .txt input, allowing a saved request to reopen after a contributing list file is moved or deleted.
- Retains compatibility with Copy Requests created by older builds from a single Request File.
- Adds Build 511 regression coverage and updates Requirements, User Manual, FAQ, release metadata, and current-version contracts.

TLO v1.7 Build 510

Lint-only source correction

- Fixes Ruff F541 in tests/behavior/test_build465_cross_platform_collection_recovery.py by changing an unnecessary raw f-string prefix (rf) to a raw-string prefix (r).
- No application/runtime behavior changes from Build 509.
- Advances source, tests, Requirements, User Manual, source-bundle metadata, and verification references to Build 510.

TLO v1.7 Build 509

Review remediation, Copy Request lock safety, and replacement-folder cleanup

- Corrects Copy Request PID liveness on Windows using Windows process APIs instead of os.kill(pid, 0); POSIX PermissionError is treated as evidence that the process exists.
- Records a process-start identity in Copy Request lock/temp ownership metadata so recycled PIDs are not mistaken for the original owner.
- Adds explicit GUI confirmation for clearing an aged stale Destination lock left by another computer or renamed host.
- Renames the Windows Add Shows cleanup file to deleteReplacedFolders.bat. Each entry identifies its recorded source volume as [Volume Label], including [] for an unlabeled volume, and uses a fully quoted rooted path. The batch file does not require that volume to be connected and is intended to be edited for current drive letters and additional master/backup copies.
- Removes incorrect caret escaping from values already inside double quotes; percent signs remain doubled for cmd.exe expansion.
- Preserves standard RTF smart quote, dash, nonbreaking-space, spacing, bullet, optional-hyphen, and nonbreaking-hyphen controls.
- Validates the normal setlist.fm key before consuming a rate slot and converts Unicode/value request-construction errors to SetlistFMError without exposing the key.
- Applies the shared tlo_path_policy prune rules to Add Shows and tlo-deleteDupes walkers.
- Rejects duplicate normalized ZIP member names, NTFS alternate-data-stream names, Windows reserved device names, and components ending in dot/space.
- Aligns Review Operation/manual/specification checkbox capitalization with the rendered GUI.
- Adds Build 509 regression coverage for the review findings.
- The separately versioned GitHub Build Process remains v094; it already installs and runs Ruff locally and in CI, so no build-process change is required for this remediation.

TLO v1.7 Build 508

Remove obsolete TaggerWindow implementation

- Removed the unused legacy TaggerWindow class from tlo-ggi.py. The retired child Tag GUI is no longer present in the runtime source.
- Removed obsolete active_tagger_window state plus the dead _tagger_is_open and _focus_active_tagger helpers.
- Removed TaggerWindow-only title/layout constants, TAGGER_TITLE consumption, direct run_tagger child-window plumbing, and the child-window tagging-path drag/drop hookup from tlo-ggi.py.
- Removed legacy tests whose only subject was the dead TaggerWindow. Retained behavior coverage now targets App._start_tagging_from_main, the only GUI Tag execution path.
- GUI Tag behavior is otherwise unchanged: it uses master Path(s), current main-window checkbox values, Review Operation / Dry run Preview, shared progress/output, and run_tagger_jobs.
- Standalone tlo-tag is unchanged.
- Requirements, User Manual, FAQ, source metadata, version stamps, and verification contracts advanced to Build 508.

TLO v1.7 Build 507

Safety, recovery, and review remediation

Delete-script safety
- Canonicalizes Add Shows cleanup targets before use and rejects literal .. traversal, drive/mount roots, drive-relative forms, TLOHome, and ancestors of TLOHome. A cleanup target must be at least one directory below its recognized volume/mount root.
- Preserves the bootlist volume identity through deleteBackupFolders generation and writes an execution-time volume-label guard before destructive commands.
- Starts each Add Shows cleanup session with a fresh deleteBackupFolders script, archiving the prior script under TLOHome/logs instead of silently appending commands across sessions.
- Windows cleanup scripts select UTF-8 code page 65001 so non-ASCII paths remain usable.

Copy Request transaction and cancellation
- Treats every Copy Request pass as a recoverable transaction. The current target/temp pair and transaction phase are persisted in request state before destination mutation.
- Holds exclusive request and destination locks for the duration of a pass so concurrent TLO instances cannot sweep each other's live copies or race request state.
- Recovers only TLO-owned pending reservations and temporary trees recorded by that request. Unrecorded or non-empty destination folders are never removed by recovery.
- Keeps TLO ownership metadata through verification/publication and clears it only after completion state is durably recorded, closing the unmarked-temp crash window.
- Adds Cancel Copy. Cancellation is checked between files, cleans only the current TLO-owned pending transaction, preserves completed items, and leaves the request Open for continuation.

RTF, setlist.fm, and input handling
- Replaces the RTF regex stripping path with group-aware conversion that skips font/color/style/info/picture/header/footer destinations, honors ANSI code pages and Unicode escapes, handles TextEdit line breaks, and preserves escaped braces/backslashes and tabs.
- setlist.fm upgrade comparison uses UTF-8 bytes so malformed/non-ASCII pasted values cannot crash GUI startup. Control/invisible key characters produce a non-secret invalid-character status.
- Resolves normal and upgrade keys by process/session tier first and uses persisted Windows values only as fallback for missing values, without logging either key.
- Legacy binary .doc candidates are explicitly treated as unsupported rather than decoded as arbitrary text.

Filesystem and updater hardening
- Centralizes OS-managed directory names and TLO temporary-tree prefixes in tlo_path_policy.py. Phase 1 prunes macOS system folders and TLO temp trees in addition to the existing Windows system folders.
- Reuses the bounded shared DOCX reader for sibling-collection text extraction instead of a separate ElementTree XML parser.
- Restricts the unused open_paths helper so Windows .bat files are never launched through os.startfile.
- ZIP inspection now rejects absolute/drive-qualified/traversal member names, symlink members, excessive member counts, excessive expanded size, and unreasonable compression ratios before accepting an update package.
- SHA-256 remains mandatory. Independent signed release metadata is documented as a future security design requiring a signing key outside ordinary GitHub publishing credentials.

Requirements, documentation, and tests
- Adds Definitions and requirement-keyword conventions; distinguishes GUI labels/actions from internal concepts.
- Adds stable IDs for the new destructive/safety rules, moves destructive safety material into its owning sections, and expands the traceability matrix with real module/test mappings for current features.
- Documents Copy Request interruption/concurrency/cancel behavior, RTF semantics, delete-script safety, updater ZIP validation, shared pruning, and the intentionally retained non-instantiated TaggerWindow.
- Converts the supplied Build 506 probes into permanent corrected-behavior regression coverage and adds transaction, cancellation, delete-script, ZIP, pruning, .doc, and RTF fixtures.
- TaggerWindow remains in place by explicit owner decision and is not the normative implementation of GUI Tag behavior.

Verification
- Build 507 remediation module: 22 passed.
- Full suite: 1,598 passed.

TLO v1.7 Build 505

Targeted review remediation and Copy Request safety

Metadata / text decoding
- Setlist header metadata and date-fallback text now use the shared bounded text reader rather than independent UTF-16-first decoders.
- cp1252/latin-1 setlists no longer decode as UTF-16 mojibake merely because their byte length is even.
- Date fallback reads selected RTF and DOCX through the shared format-aware reader instead of treating container/control bytes as plain text.
- Scored decoder call sites used by setlist selection and sibling collection handling now converge on the same byte decoder.

Copy and verification safety
- Tree verification and verification-related size/path walks now fail closed when a subtree cannot be enumerated.
- Copy Requests reject Destination/source overlap in either direction, including Destination equal to the source. Source-as-target is never recorded as Already Satisfied/Complete.
- Whole-volume Copy Requests consistently exclude Windows System Volume Information and $RECYCLE.BIN plus macOS .Spotlight-V100, .fseventsd, .Trashes, and .TemporaryItems from sizing, copying, and verification; Preview/reporting identifies the exclusions.
- A bare volume label that also matches an Artist/alias or exact Show is ambiguous and is rejected with guidance to use [Volume].
- Copy Request destinations are reserved before copying rather than finalized by a check-then-replace race. Stale legacy .tlo-copy-* temporary folders are swept at pass start.
- Direct-source Show coverage filters lexical path candidates before filesystem probes.

setlist.fm / GUI hardening
- setlist.fm upgrade availability now requires SETLISTFMUPGRADE_API_KEY or SETLISTFM_UPGRADE_API_KEY to exactly match SETLISTFM_API_KEY. Key values are never logged.
- --setlistfm-upgrade is inert when --setlistfm-lookup is off rather than raising an unnecessary CLI validation error.
- On Windows, current persisted User/System environment values take precedence over a stale inherited process value; expandable registry values are expanded.
- Delete Extra Tags is also gated in config construction so future programmatic paths cannot leave it active without a tagging mode.
- setlist.fm rate-limit constants are shared instead of copied into multiple GUI/tag configuration builders.

Requirements / manual / FAQ / packaging
- Reconciles the v504 review findings R1-R7 and U1-U4: Section 20 now precedes the appendices, normative rules no longer carry build-history narration, recent manual notes are chronological, Copy Request hazards are documented, and Delete Extra Tags capitalization is consistent.
- The source bundle no longer ships superseded SHA256SUMS_v500-v504.txt or BUILD_VERIFICATION_v500-v504.txt at its root; those prior verification artifacts are archived in old-change-logs.zip.
- Adds executable Build 505 regressions derived from the supplied v504 probes, including permission-error verification, cp1252/RTF/DOCX decoding, overlap/self-copy, Artist/volume collisions, system-folder exclusions, and upgrade-key mismatch.

TLO v1.7 Build 504

GUI option-state and setlist.fm upgrade environment detection

- When Tag in Place, Tag Copy, and Tag Copy/Delete Original are all off, Delete Extra Tags is now forced unchecked as well as disabled/greyed.
- The three tagging modes remain mutually exclusive; selecting any one enables Delete Extra Tags again but does not re-check it automatically.
- setlist.fm upgrade environment detection still requires an explicit upgrade-enable variable, but now accepts both SETLISTFMUPGRADE_API_KEY and SETLISTFM_UPGRADE_API_KEY.
- On Windows, TLO checks the current persisted User and System environment values when the launching process inherited a stale environment snapshot, so variables added through Environment Variables or setx are recognized without exposing the API key.
- Normal setlist.fm still requires SETLISTFM_API_KEY, and the upgrade value is expected to contain the same API key.

Tests / documentation / packaging
- Adds Build 504 behavior coverage for Delete Extra Tags clearing, upgrade-variable alias detection, persistent Windows environment fallback, and GUI upgrade enablement.
- Updates requirements, User Manual, FAQ, version stamps, source-bundle metadata, and current-release contracts to v1.7 Build 504.

===== Historical record: Manual_selected_build_history_v564.txt =====
Build 564 - Retires the unused Inventory Update path opener and other unreferenced wrappers. GUI opening continues through the hardened live path, which selects RTF files rather than launching a registered handler. The updater now shares one validated post-download and reporting path. New executable regression tests cover failures in transfers, corruption checks and update handling. No changes to user-facing transfer or corruption policy.
Build 563 - Copy/Delete Original now stages in .tlo-partial-* before publication: cross-filesystem copies verify relative structure and sizes plus SHA-256 before publication and source cleanup. Same-filesystem moves use whole-directory atomic renames into staging and then the final name, with safe rollback after a failed publication. Interrupted workers leave marked, excluded staging trees; a failed source cleanup retains the verified published destination. Tag Copy and Copy/Delete share live staging and publication helpers.Â 
Build 562 - Ordinary Tag Copy now copies into a .tlo-partial-* work folder, verifies the entire relative tree and file sizes, and atomically publishes the finished show without overwriting any existing folder. Cancelled/killed workers cannot expose an incomplete copied show under a normal name; an abandoned staging folder is excluded from inventory. Copy/Delete Original staging is planned for the next build.
Build 561 - Native Windows physical-drive grouping now resolves the PowerShell executable from the trusted Windows-directory API and never falls back to PATH or the current directory. Unavailable Windows helper lookup safely returns unknown physical drive; WSL uses its separate existing lookup.
Build 560 - Windows corruption cleanup preflights the native Windows Recycle Bin and its per-volume capacity/settings, refusing destructive calls when recycling is unavailable. A failed Trash operation stops further removal attempts on that show and blocks later mutations. WSL Trash behavior is documented separately.
Build 559 - Windows cleanup scripts now verify each connected drive's current label against the recorded label before rmdir; unlabeled entries never have an active delete command, and any mismatch or unavailable probe skips safely.
Build 558 - Corruption and transfer descriptions: Dry Run never moves, deletes, Trashes, copies, renames, or writes audio tags regardless of the corruption policy. The first-run instructions clarify how Keep and report changes a normal run. Tag Copy verifies structure/paths/sizes; cross-partition Copy/Delete also verifies SHA-256 contents before removing the source. Runtime behavior and default settings are unchanged. GitHub Build Process remains v108 unchanged.
Build 557 - Deep Audio Check: adds a default-off checkbox directly beneath Proper Grammar and the shared --deep-audio-check CLI option. When selected, TLO performs a complete-stream decode with the application-bundled ffmpeg in addition to the normal header/metadata check and, during real tagging, the reversible tag-write check. Actual decoder failures are corruption; decoder unavailability, timeout, cancellation, access/I/O/resource, or validator-infrastructure failures are unverifiable and fail closed. Review Operation and runSettings.log report the selected setting. GitHub Build Process remains v108 unchanged.
Build 556 - Tag-write corruption validation: non-tagging inventory continues to use header/metadata validation only. When Tag In Place, Tag Copy, Tag Copy/Delete Original, or standalone Tag is actually running, TLO also writes and reads back a temporary private metadata value, restores the exact prior value or absence, and verifies the restoration before later mutation. Filesystem/resource or restoration failures remain unverifiable and fail closed. Dry Run performs no temporary tag write. GitHub Build Process remains v108 unchanged.
Build 555 - Shell-safe Path(s) directive syntax: replaces the dollar-prefixed per-path/CLI forms with --/slam, --/copy, and --/copy-delete and does not retain the old dollar forms as aliases. Inventory CLI help no longer prints misleading True, blank, or None defaults for negative/empty options, and shared Tag Copy option help no longer describes GUI-only interaction. GitHub Build Process remains v108 unchanged.
Build 554 - Release-gate restoration: removes the three newly reported Ruff F401 unused imports so the pinned Ruff 0.16.10 source gate is clean. Verification records must use a NOT RELEASE-ATTESTED heading whenever any required release-gate tool or check did not execute, preventing a passing pytest count from being mistaken for complete release attestation. Application runtime behavior is unchanged. GitHub Build Process remains v108 unchanged.
Build 553 - Low-risk maintenance closure: command-line/build helper modules now expose their intended descriptions in --help; residual dead updater state and redundant network/device prefix checks are removed; and the independently pinned build/test and audit-tool dependency locks now explain why shared package versions may intentionally differ. Supported runtime behavior is unchanged. GitHub Build Process remains v108 unchanged.
Build 552 - Parser and GUI robustness: date analysis on arbitrarily long complete-path/text inputs is bounded to the trailing 1,024 characters while preserving original match offsets; Research input is capped at 2,048 characters and artist-plus-date suffix analysis at 256 characters. Background Tk worker completion now treats both TclError and the normal shutdown RuntimeError as a closed-window condition instead of printing daemon-thread tracebacks. GitHub Build Process remains v108 unchanged.
Build 551 - Windows path/device hardening: update ZIP validation now uses the shared Windows reserved-name validator and rejects the superscript COM/LPT 1-3 device-name forms. Explorer and Notepad helper launches are rooted in the Windows directory returned by the Windows API rather than trusting SystemRoot; helper lookup fails closed if that API lookup cannot be completed. GitHub Build Process remains v108 unchanged. Build 550 - Norton scanner receipt integrity: custom Norton scanner commands must invoke a recognized Norton executable first; TLO resolves the executable that will actually run, executes it without a shell, requires a standard Program Files location and valid Norton Authenticode signer on Windows, and records the resolved executable path, SHA-256, signature status, and signer subject in the clean-scan receipt. GitHub Build Process remains v108 unchanged.
Build 549 - Robustness closure: malformed eTreeDB textual dates are rejected without unexpected overflow; oversized eTreeDB/setlist.fm HTTP error bodies remain inside their domain error boundaries; tlo-deleteDupes treats a missing ffmpeg return code as unverifiable and stabilizes ffmpeg diagnostics with LC_ALL=C; lock-status probes no longer create or modify lock files, and owned-lock acquisition retries brief probe collisions before reporting busy. GitHub Build Process remains v108 unchanged.
Build 548 - Documentation/specification correction: documents default corruption Trash/Recycle Bin behavior in Quick Start and FAQ; makes IA-05 and the planned schema-2 expiry/revocation design explicit; corrects Requirements CLI/Appendix/revision-index gaps and Manual inaccuracies; rewrites FAQ build narratives as current behavior; moves the signing test into the unit category; removes stale test-module version stamps; and derives current versioned test-artifact names from tlo_version.VERSION. Supported runtime behavior is unchanged. GitHub Build Process remains v108 unchanged.
Build 547 - Reverse implementation cleanup: removes the obsolete tlo_reverse_copy_delete module and its dedicated historical tests. Still-relevant no-glob and cross-filesystem byte-verification coverage now targets the supported tlo-reverse/tlo_reverse_folders implementation; conflict, symlink-preservation, and failed-verification safety behavior remains directly tested. GitHub Build Process remains v108 unchanged.
Build 546 - Build/audit supply-chain hardening: the pip-audit helper removes inherited PIP_* configuration, uses pip --isolated for pip operations, audits requirements-audit.txt itself, and performs a marker-neutral audit so Windows/macOS-only exact pins are checked on every host. Local Windows signing selects only Microsoft-signed x64 SignTool from Windows Kits, records its path/hash/version/signer, and verifies each signed EXE before malware scanning. GitHub Build Process remains v108 unchanged.
Build 545 - Temporary-tree and symlink safety: TLO's Tag Copy partial trees, case-only rename intermediates, and cross-filesystem reverse-restore trees now use names covered by the shared recursive prune policy, so crash leftovers are not inventoried or compared as Shows. Copy Requests recheck for symbolic links at the copy boundary, copy with link preservation instead of dereferencing, reject any link in the completed temporary tree, and exact tree verification compares symbolic links by link target rather than linked-to bytes. GitHub Build Process remains v108.
Build 544 - Rollback-failure safety: case-only folder renames, Manual Updates, and sibling-collection consolidation no longer silently discard rollback failures. TLO records suppressed rollback exceptions, reports incomplete recovery to the user, and names the current folder/recovery-container or affected state file where practical. Windows Recycle Bin COM-release cleanup failures are also recorded diagnostically. GitHub Build Process remains v108.
Build 543 - Corrupt-input isolation: corrupt/truncated DOCX setlist streams are treated as unusable input, and standalone Tag now contains unexpected processing failures to the affected folder so remaining folders continue. The Add Shows duplicate path is covered by a real corrupt-DOCX regression and completes with consistent staged/bootlist state. GitHub Build Process remains v108.
Build 542 - Update-trust assurance and packaged-FFmpeg containment: adds a real RSA-3072 signature verification vector plus digest-mismatch, signed release-tag, and ZIP parent-traversal regressions. The bundled ffmpeg resolver now rejects a symlinked tlo_ffmpeg_bin directory that resolves outside the packaged application root. Mutation checks confirm each protected control is detected if disabled. GitHub Build Process remains v108.
Build 541 - Release verification integrity: removes the five reported Ruff F401 violations; a pytest run marked with TLO_RELEASE_VERIFY=1 fails if Ruff is unavailable; verification records must say lint not attested when Ruff did not execute. GitHub Build Process remains v108 and application runtime behavior is unchanged.

Build 540 - Release-tool bootstrap hardening: GitHub Build Process v108 downloads no package-manager executable or Windows SDK package after OIDC login; it validates the runner-installed Microsoft SignTool with Authenticode, verifies the pinned Microsoft.ArtifactSigning.Client 1.0.128 SHA-512 before extraction, and runs pip-audit from a separate hash-locked runtime closure after verifying the CPython 3.13.16 ensurepip bootstrap. Application runtime behavior is unchanged.
Build 539 - Release-process supply-chain hardening: GitHub Build Process v107 pins every external GitHub Action to a reviewed full 40-character commit SHA instead of a mutable branch or floating major-version reference. Application runtime behavior is unchanged; signing-tool and pip-audit bootstrap hardening remain planned separately.
Build 538 - Release-test gate correctness: the official Linux release workflow now runs the complete pytest suite by source-root discovery under Xvfb rather than invoking test_tlo_requirements.py alone. The compatibility file remains for historical/direct callers but is not the sole release gate. GitHub Build Process v106 is coordinated.
Build 537 - Portability and contained robustness: Tkinter-dependent regression modules skip before import on systems without Tkinter; Windows Explorer/Notepad helpers use explicit SystemRoot executables; setlist.fm API credentials are non-redirected headers; setlist.fm reservation coordination uses the shared OS-held advisory lock without stale-file deletion; and non-object eTreeDB JSON is reported as an eTreeDB protocol error. Build 536 - Updater package-safety enforcement: downloaded update ZIPs are accepted only when UPDATE_MANIFEST.json declares safe_update true, requires_complete_install false, and the complete canonical protected user-data path set. TLO rejects protected user-data members and ZIP names that collide case-insensitively. The updater requires TLO_UPDATE_METADATA.json and TLO_UPDATE_METADATA.sig, verifies the signature against a build-injected pinned RSA-3072 public key, then authorizes a selected update ZIP only when its signed filename, size, SHA-256, build, package kind, and platform/layout match.
